[booklet]: # "Weavers of the Void® booklet: the deeds of sons and mortals"

# Weavers of the Void®: The Deeds of Sons and Mortals

The world does not wait for you. Every epoch your sons and the notable mortals of each world take their own turns: each reads its world, decides what it wants and acts. What they can do is fixed by the tools in this booklet. Every tool runs through the rules of the world, so a deed always has a cost, a reach and a trace in the record.

| The deeds at a glance | |
|---|---:|
| Tools of the sons | 330 |
| Tools of the mortals | 445 |
| Tools that only look | 9 |
| Tools that cost essence | 141 |
| Tools only rarer sons may use | 78 |
| Tools limited to some times an epoch | 30 |
| Tools tied to a mechanic of the world | 609 |
| Mechanics the tools reach | 337 |

## How to read this booklet

- Tools are gathered by who uses them, the sons or the mortals, and then by the **part of the world** they act on, the same parts as in the companion booklet *How the World Connects*.
- Each tool is described in the words the son or mortal reads when it chooses. **You** in those words is the one who uses the tool.
- In the tools' own words an **age** is an epoch.
- Numbers are those of a new world. They follow your world's rules, so they change when you change those rules (see the companion booklet *The Rules of the World*).
- **What it chooses** lists what the son or mortal decides when it uses the tool.
- **Reaches** links to the mechanics of the world the tool sets in motion.
- You can see every deed in the chronicle and the ledger, and you can change, hold or undo what it made with the powers in *The Powers of a God*.

## A turn

Each son and each acting mortal gets one turn an epoch. A turn has a budget of **action points**: most tools spend 1 point, tools that only look spend none, and a few spend more. A son also spends its own **essence** on great works.

A son's action points follow the rarity of its card:

| Rarity | Action points |
|---|---:|
| Common | 2 |
| Uncommon | 3 |
| Rare | 4 |
| Epic | 5 |
| Legendary | 6 |
| Mythic | 7 |

A mortal has 3 action points a turn. Mortal turns are taken by settlements, species, peoples, mortals, cults, factions, spirits, armies and beasts. Spirits use only these tools: Inspect entity, Look at world, Pray to creator, Prophesy, Search entities and Send dispatch.

<a id="a-son"></a>

## The tools of the sons

Your sons are gods. Each epoch every son takes a turn: it reads its world and acts through the tools below, spending action points and essence. A tool is offered only when it can work in that son's world.

<a id="p-son-seeing"></a>

### Seeing the world

Tools that only look: they show the world, a thing or a memory more fully than the turn's own picture of it. They cost nothing and change nothing.

6 tools: [inspect entity](#t-son-inspect_entity), [inspect sibling](#t-son-inspect_sibling), [my memory](#t-son-my_memory), [recall event](#t-son-recall_event), [search entities](#t-son-search_entities), [world summary](#t-son-world_summary).

<a id="t-son-inspect_entity"></a>

#### Inspect entity

Shows all details of an entity: its stats, bonds and what is inside it. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Id | The entity's id |

<a id="t-son-inspect_sibling"></a>

#### Inspect sibling

Shows a sibling's visible form, powers, creations and relationship with you. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The sibling's id |

<a id="t-son-my_memory"></a>

#### My memory

Shows your plan, words said to you, your creations, your worshippers, your memories and what concerns you; the full version of the truncated memory in the prompt. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

<a id="t-son-recall_event"></a>

#### Recall event

Shows the full account of a happening you saw, took part in, or that happened in a world you can reach: who took part and in which role, what is known of it, how it went step by step, what caused it and what it led to. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.
- **Reaches** [Every value any hand changes is kept with its before, after, cause and terms; any record can be read as it was at an earlier moment](interconnections.md#m-record_of_events) and [Those present at a happening witness it; retellings change its parts, truer tellings set them right and contrary ones cost trust](interconnections.md#m-witnesses_and_tellings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Incident | The happening's id |
| Step offset | Where to start in its steps when they run past one page |

<a id="t-son-search_entities"></a>

#### Search entities

Searches entities by a word appearing in the name or description. Type filter optional. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Query | Word to search; leave empty for recent entities |
| Type | Only entities of this type |

<a id="t-son-world_summary"></a>

#### World summary

Shows the world's current map, laws, pantheon, open stories and recent events. Give section to see in full a section that was truncated in the prompt (pantheon, threads, events, cosmos, geography, languages, map, legends). Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Section | Show only this section in full |

<a id="p-son-body"></a>

### Body

4 tools: [ease body](#t-son-ease_body), [shape blood](#t-son-shape_blood), [sharpen sense](#t-son-sharpen_sense), [take life](#t-son-take_life).

<a id="t-son-ease_body"></a>

#### Ease body

Ease a lasting condition of a being within your reach, whatever brought it on; each touch lightens it by one step and the lightest goes entirely.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Conditions without an agent: bodies that wear by age, diet, fouling, heredity, strain and old wounds, and ease by medicine](interconnections.md#m-agentless_conditions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Being | The being |
| Condition | The condition |
| Reason | Why you ease it |

<a id="t-son-shape_blood"></a>

#### Shape blood

You work an allele into both inherited copies of a living mortal: any locus of looks, body, senses, temperament, aptitude, predisposition, divine descent or aptitude for a form of magic. What the locus does follows from the allele, and their children inherit it as such traits are inherited. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Every mortal carries a genome of typed loci; children inherit one allele from each parent, the stronger shows, and kin unions raise harmful pairs](interconnections.md#m-genetics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal whose inheritance you shape |
| Locus | The locus to set |
| Allele | The allele both copies of that locus will carry |
| Reason | Why you shape it |

<a id="t-son-sharpen_sense"></a>

#### Sharpen sense

You sharpen one sense of a kind of creature or of a people on the world you stand on, so it finds, tracks, sees or steers better. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Senses: what a kind or people perceives finds agents, blunts ambushes, tracks game, steers ships and sees the spies of gods](interconnections.md#m-senses).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | The kind or the people |
| Sense | The sense: sight, night_vision, smell, hearing, echolocation, vibration, magnetic, spirit_sight, heat, electric, ultraviolet, taste or pressure |
| Reason | Why you sharpen it |

<a id="t-son-take_life"></a>

#### Take life

You take the life of a named mortal. It is very expensive, and the more important the victim the costlier and harder: cost 25 + 6 x power level + importance/2 essence (+20 if ruler, hero, chosen or titled). Success depends on dice (base 65%): a sibling patron, worshipping the creator, being protected or chosen, and importance protect them. If it fails the essence is lost, the victim becomes a legend who returned from death, and their protector is angered with you. The dead one's creator and patron are also angered; what they ruled passes to their heir. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | The named mortal whose life is taken |
| How | How you take their life |

<a id="p-son-mind"></a>

### Mind

19 tools: [affect entity](#t-son-affect_entity), [awaken mind](#t-son-awaken_mind), [bind mortals](#t-son-bind_mortals), [bless with](#t-son-bless_with), [curse](#t-son-curse), [curse with](#t-son-curse_with), [declare chosen](#t-son-declare_chosen), [ease grief](#t-son-ease_grief), [grant mind](#t-son-grant_mind), [mark quirk](#t-son-mark_quirk), [move hearts](#t-son-move_hearts), [nudge bias](#t-son-nudge_bias), [plant idea](#t-son-plant_idea), [send dream](#t-son-send_dream), [still mind](#t-son-still_mind), [stir emotion](#t-son-stir_emotion), [stir hearts](#t-son-stir_hearts), [teach knowledge](#t-son-teach_knowledge), [touch mortal](#t-son-touch_mortal).

<a id="t-son-affect_entity"></a>

#### Affect entity

You change the stats of an existing entity. Touching someone else's creation is 2 times as expensive and angers its creator. Lifespan, age and knowledge level do not change this way; each stat once per call; the same entity at most 1 time per age. Cost 6 + power level essence. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | The entity's id |
| Changes | The stats to change (at most 3) |
| Changes › Stat | The stat's key |
| Changes › Percent | Between -30 and +30 |
| Reason | Why you change it |

<a id="t-son-awaken_mind"></a>

#### Awaken mind

You wake a mind in a thing in your reach, giving it the will you name. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The thing whose mind wakes |
| Will | How much will it wakes with |
| Reason | Why you wake it |

<a id="t-son-bind_mortals"></a>

#### Bind mortals

You bind two living mortals by your will: promise them to each other, wed them, give the first to the second as a ward, or make them sworn kin. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First | The first mortal |
| Second | The second mortal (the fosterer when you foster) |
| Binding | betroth, wed, foster or swear_kin |
| Reason | Why you bind them |

<a id="t-son-bless_with"></a>

#### Bless with

Lay an affliction of any kind on a being within your reach, as a blessing given by rite; a fortune laid this way leans to good. 12 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 12 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The being |
| Package | The kind of affliction |
| Reason | Why you give it |

<a id="t-son-curse"></a>

#### Curse

You curse an entity: its stats fall and it weakens further every age until the curse is lifted. Its creator is angered. 12+2 x power essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 12 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | The entity's id |
| Curse | The words and form of the curse |
| Affliction | Optional, for a mortal or beast: a staged affliction the curse lays on them until it is cured: lycanthropy (Beast curse: Under the full face of the world's first moon the bearer becomes a thinking beast; a bite passes the curse on.); vampirism (Blood curse: The bearer stops ageing, hungers for blood and burns under the sun; a feeding can pass it on.); possession (Possession: A bound spirit rides the bearer and speaks with their mouth.); hunger_curse (Hunger curse: A god's curse of a hunger that no meal fills.); geas_doom (Geas doom: The doom that falls on one who broke a geas.); fortune (Fortune: A lasting shift of luck, for good or for ill, that holds and then fades; a line's fortune passes to its children.); transformation (Transformation: A change of the body into another plan, kind or substance, lasting or coming back at a set sign.); ageing (Ageing: Ageing sped, slowed or stopped for its holder, at a rate set when it is laid.); forgetting (Forgetting: Memories, skills or a person's name taken from the holder, which can be learned again.); sleep (Sleep: A sleep the holder cannot leave until its cure comes.) |

<a id="t-son-curse_with"></a>

#### Curse with

Lay an affliction of any kind on a being within your reach, as a curse; a fortune laid this way leans to ill. 12 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 12 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The being |
| Package | The kind of affliction |
| Reason | Why you lay it |

<a id="t-son-declare_chosen"></a>

#### Declare chosen

You declare a mortal your chosen one: their strength and charisma rise, they worship you, and you favour them in their deeds. 14 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 14 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The living mortal's id |
| Boon | What you give them |

<a id="t-son-ease_grief"></a>

#### Ease grief

Move the grief of a mortal within your reach to another stage: back to shock, on through anger, mourning and yearning, to acceptance, or into a grief that will not heal. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [A death leaves kin, spouses and companions in shock, then mourning stretched by mourning rites, then acceptance or a grief that will not heal](interconnections.md#m-grief).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mourner | The mortal who grieves |
| Stage | The stage their grief moves to |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-grant_mind"></a>

#### Grant mind

You give a thing in your reach a mind of its own, by your gift. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The thing that gains a mind |
| Origin | Where its mind is said to come from |
| Reason | Why you give it a mind |

<a id="t-son-mark_quirk"></a>

#### Mark quirk

You leave a quirk on a mortal in your reach (fears_dark, dark_sighted, knows_ways, eats_anything, hardy, frail, jumpy, steady_nerves, keen_eyed, homesick, restless, light_fingered, claustrophobic, cold_hardy, swimmer, lucky); it stays with them and changes how they fare on expeditions and how often they set out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Quirks: minor traits places and events leave on a mortal, kept after return and changing how they fare and how often they set out](interconnections.md#m-quirks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal you mark |
| Kind | The quirk |
| Reason | Why you mark them |

<a id="t-son-move_hearts"></a>

#### Move hearts

Give the people of a settlement within your reach a feeling, which then spreads among its people, feeds its crowd and leans its choices.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spread of feeling between beings: along bonds, through places and households, into crowds and choices](interconnections.md#m-feeling_spread).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The settlement |
| Feeling | The feeling |
| Strength | How strong, from 1 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you move them |

<a id="t-son-nudge_bias"></a>

#### Nudge bias

You sway how a mortal within your reach decides: one leaning of their mind (overconfidence, sunk cost, loss aversion, confirmation, recency, groupthink, hostile attribution, optimism, status quo, availability, anchoring, in-group favour, authority deference, scarcity panic, fatalism, risk seeking, planning fallacy, halo or projection) is set to the weight you give, and their choices of war, peace, surrender, schemes, belief, conversion and migration lean by it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Decision biases: rulers, commanders and gods lean in war, peace, surrender, schemes, belief, conversion, migration and goals](interconnections.md#m-biases).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Bias | The leaning |
| Weight | Its weight, 0 to 100; 50 leaves it level |
| Reason | Why you sway them |

<a id="t-son-plant_idea"></a>

#### Plant idea

You plant an idea in one living mortal's mind. They turn it over for some seasons; it may settle in and shift their beliefs, abilities and temperament for good, or they may let it go if it sits poorly with what they already believe. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Ideas met in ideologies, doctrines, theories, books and teachers incubate for seasons, then settle in or are let go](interconnections.md#m-ideas).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Idea | The idea to plant, by its key |
| Reason | Why you plant it |

<a id="t-son-send_dream"></a>

#### Send dream

You send a dream to a cult, people, settlement, faction or mortal to make it worship you. If it worships someone else, success depends on your charisma against its patron's charisma. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Named mortals dream: nightmares from fears and wounds, longing from unmet needs, prophecy for seers, shared dreams and visitations](interconnections.md#m-dreams).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The target's id |
| Dream | The dream's content |

<a id="t-son-still_mind"></a>

#### Still mind

You still a mind in your reach; it stops thinking until it is woken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The thing whose mind goes still |
| Reason | Why you still it |

<a id="t-son-stir_emotion"></a>

#### Stir emotion

Stir a feeling in a mortal within your reach; it colours what they think and do this age and fades by the nature of the feeling. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Thoughts carry one of 24 feelings sharpened by facets; their decaying sum is a mood, and the strongest become formative memories](interconnections.md#m-thoughts_and_mood).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Node | The mortal |
| Emotion | The feeling |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-stir_hearts"></a>

#### Stir hearts

You stir what one living mortal feels toward another: turn them toward friendship or romance, set them at odds as rivals, or plant a passion of love, loyalty, hatred or devotion for the other. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Mortals who share a place meet, talk, court, quarrel, teach, gift and brawl; friendship, romance, familiarity and rivalry grow into bonds](interconnections.md#m-relationships) and [Passions of love, loyalty, hatred and devotion are invoked in battles, duels, oaths and crises; losing their objects brings full grief](interconnections.md#m-passions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First | The mortal whose feelings you stir |
| Second | The one they come to feel for |
| Stir | What to stir: friendship, romance, rivalry, love, loyalty, hatred or devotion |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-teach_knowledge"></a>

#### Teach knowledge

You teach an existing knowledge to a community or mortal; the knowledge spreads from them to their partners. If unclaimed it starts worshipping you. Cost: 8 essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 8 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community or mortal |
| Knowledge | The knowledge entity |
| How | How you teach it |

<a id="t-son-touch_mortal"></a>

#### Touch mortal

You touch one living mortal: ease or burden their mind, grant renown or shame, heal their body, or strengthen one attribute of body or mind. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal you touch |
| Touch | ease_mind, burden_mind, grant_renown, shame, heal_body or strengthen |
| Attribute | When you strengthen: the attribute of body or mind you raise |
| Reason | Why you touch them |

<a id="p-son-social"></a>

### Society

23 tools: [birth child](#t-son-birth_child), [bless city](#t-son-bless_city), [call sibling](#t-son-call_sibling), [challenge](#t-son-challenge), [convene gathering](#t-son-convene_gathering), [design district](#t-son-design_district), [found faction](#t-son-found_faction), [give gift](#t-son-give_gift), [honour servant](#t-son-honour_servant), [move plague response](#t-son-move_plague_response), [raise district](#t-son-raise_district), [rig match](#t-son-rig_match), [send expedition](#t-son-send_expedition), [set circuit](#t-son-set_circuit), [set storylet](#t-son-set_storylet), [shape folk](#t-son-shape_folk), [spread word](#t-son-spread_word), [stir crowd](#t-son-stir_crowd), [stir identity](#t-son-stir_identity), [stir kin](#t-son-stir_kin), [stir labour](#t-son-stir_labour), [sway org](#t-son-sway_org), [swear covenant](#t-son-swear_covenant).

<a id="t-son-birth_child"></a>

#### Birth child

You birth a new son from your own essence; a new card joins the pantheon. Its rarity is lower than yours. 60 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 60 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.
- **Takes time:** a new son is born when the turn is over.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Inspiration | Why and from what the child is born, which domains it will carry |
| Co parent | Optional: the sibling you birth together with |

<a id="t-son-bless_city"></a>

#### Bless city

Bless a settlement within your reach in one of its services, raising how well it is kept, or in one of its rules, raising how well it is kept to.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [City rules, municipal services and settlements that grow together](interconnections.md#m-city_rules).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement |
| Service or rule | A service (refuse, lighting, water_distribution, street_cleaning, night_watch, burial_ground or market_inspection) or a rule (allowed_uses, banned_uses, height_limit, density_limit, build_material, fire_spacing, frontage_rule or green_share) |
| Reason | Why you bless it |

<a id="t-son-call_sibling"></a>

#### Call sibling

Sends a word to one of your siblings. The word also goes into the chronicle; it changes the relationship according to intent and your charisma. Does not spend an action, 2 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 2 times an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The sibling's id |
| Intent | friendship, alliance, love, threat, mockery, warning or bargain |
| Message | What you say |

<a id="t-son-challenge"></a>

#### Challenge

You challenge a sibling in a domain; the result is decided by dice, stat, level and powers. The winner gains fame, experience and a share of the loser's essence; the loser is wounded, and falls asleep if their life runs out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The sibling's id |
| Stat | The stat of the trial: strength, wisdom, cunning, charisma or will |
| Reason | Why you challenge |

<a id="t-son-convene_gathering"></a>

#### Convene gathering

You call a gathering at a people, settlement, cult or place in your reach: a festival, procession, pilgrimage, feast, games or performance. It is held this epoch; those who come grow closer to one another and to the host. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Gatherings: feasts, weddings, funerals, coronations, tournaments, hunts, councils, summits, festivals, fairs, games, performances, trials, executions, pilgrimages, processions, milestones and wakes](interconnections.md#m-gatherings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Host | Who hosts it |
| Kind | The kind of gathering |
| Reason | Why you call it |

<a id="t-son-design_district"></a>

#### Design district

You devise a new kind of district for your world on a base kind; every city may then raise it. Each number you give replaces the base kind's effect per level: housing (people), food_store and goods_store (stored goods), harbour (trade capacity), order, sanitation (may be negative), piety_floor, admin_pct (administrative reach), policing, investment_pct and tension_relief_pct. 35 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 35 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The design's name |
| Base kind | The base district kind it builds on |
| Housing | People housed per level |
| Food store | Food stored per level |
| Goods store | Ore and goods stored per level |
| Harbour | Harbour trade capacity per level |
| Order | Public order per level |
| Sanitation | Sanitation per level (negative for squalor) |
| Piety floor | Piety the district keeps alive per level |
| Admin pct | Administrative reach per level (%) |
| Policing | Crime suppressed per level |
| Investment pct | Extra investment drawn per level (%) |
| Tension relief pct | City-countryside tension relieved per level (%) |
| Reason | What the district is and why you devise it |

<a id="t-son-found_faction"></a>

#### Found faction

Creates a Faction. Living creation: the first living creation each age does NOT spend an action; later ones cost 1 action. Cost: 9+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 9 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Factions: sixteen kinds pursuing typed goals by lobbying, bribes, petitions, strikes, riots, coups, murder, smuggling, raids and war funding](interconnections.md#m-factions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. The domain used in this creation; one of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science. Your own domains are cheap. |
| Power level | From 1 up to your power ceiling. Increases the cost. |
| Stats | Optional stats; leave out any you do not want to set |
| Links | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Links › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Links › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Links › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |
| Links › Description | Short description of the bond. Use related only if no concrete type fits, and then you MUST write the relation here. |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside (Place, Region, Settlement). It must have a location: if you leave it empty it is inferred from the region, place or settlement among your bonds (or from where the community you bond to is located) |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Worshipped son | Whom they worship (son id). Faith flows this way. |
| Faction kind | What it is: merchant_guild, craft_guild, priesthood, warrior_order, noble_council, peasant_league, rebels, bandits, pirates, assassins, scholars_academy, secret_society, mercenary_company, interworld_federation, wandering_market, route_raiders, social_circle, mage_order, estate, party, movement, chartered_company, holy_order, crime_family, thieves_guild, smuggling_ring, pirate_confederacy, art_movement, club, league, workers_union, engineers_society, financiers, veterans, worker_body, street_gang, trafficking_ring, fence_network, counterfeiting_ring; its kind decides what it strives for and how it acts |
| Stats › Strength | Military strength (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Wealth | Wealth (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Sway | Sway (0 to 100) |

<a id="t-son-give_gift"></a>

#### Give gift

You give a gift to a sibling: a share of your essence and/or you try to transfer a community that worships you to them (the community may not accept). A gift strengthens the relationship and the sibling owes you a gift in return in time, and a gift you give a sibling answers what they gave you. An essence gift does not spend an action; a worshipper transfer costs 1 action. 1 per age.

- **Costs** no action points, or more when it does more.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Reaches** [Gift ledgers: gifts bind their receivers to answer in kind and in time; refusals insult and unanswered gifts cost face](interconnections.md#m-reciprocity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The sibling's id |
| Essence | Essence to give (at most 40) |
| Worshipper | The id of the community that worships you and that you will transfer to them |
| Words | The word you say with the gift |

<a id="t-son-honour_servant"></a>

#### Honour servant

Honour a being within your reach who has served you, with your own honour; they gain renown and goodwill toward you.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Honours conferred on persons: orders of merit that realms, faiths, orders and gods found, confer for deeds, sell and take back](interconnections.md#m-person_honours).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | The one you honour |
| Reason | Why you honour them |

<a id="t-son-move_plague_response"></a>

#### Move plague response

You move a community to answer a sickness one way (close_borders, quarantine, burn_dead, expel_sick, prayer, seek_cure, sanitation, flee, inoculate, close_gatherings): closed borders cost trade and calm, a quarantine slows the spread but may set off riots, burning the dead slows it but grieves folk whose rites forbid it, driving out the sick slows it at home and marks the sick, prayer comforts but crowds, seeking a cure sets the learned of its hospitals, academies and monasteries to work, and cleaning water and carrying away refuse costs treasury, raises the town's sanitation and cuts part of the spread by the routes it reaches. Each answer cuts only the routes it reaches. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Plague responses: communities close borders, quarantine, burn the dead, drive out the sick, pray or seek a cure, each with its price](interconnections.md#m-plague_responses).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The community |
| Disease | The sickness |
| Response | The answer |
| Reason | Why you move them |

<a id="t-son-raise_district"></a>

#### Raise district

You raise or enlarge a district of a settlement of any kind its world knows, or one of the world's district designs. Each kind changes the city: storage, housing, order, sanitation, piety, administration, policing, trade and harbour capacity. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement's id |
| Kind | The district kind |
| Design | Or the id of one of the world's district designs |
| Material | timber or stone (stone resists fire, flood and storm but falls in earthquakes) |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-rig_match"></a>

#### Rig match

You have two clubs in your reach play a match and decide who wins; the towns that wagered on it gain or lose, and the fans of the loser may riot. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Circus factions: fandom follows class, district or faith, gives clubs leverage, sparks riots after lost matches and is bought by weak rulers](interconnections.md#m-circus_factions) and [Clubs and leagues: towns with arenas found clubs, realms gather them into leagues, matches are played and towns wager on them](interconnections.md#m-sports).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Home | The home club |
| Away | The away club |
| Winner | The club that wins |
| Reason | Why you rig it |

<a id="t-son-send_expedition"></a>

#### Send expedition

You move a mortal in your reach to lead an expedition, with its companions, into the ground its target lies in (site, wilds, underworld, realm, under_water, space, dimension); each member takes the role its skills fit best (scout, guide, healer, fighter, scholar, porter, warder, interpreter, cartographer, engineer), and a role nobody fills is a weakness. Each leg uses provisions, light on dark ground and air where some cannot breathe; running short raises danger and stress, members may break, rise to the test or fall out with each other and come home with quirks, and those who return bring finds and renown. It travels by one way its leader's people can use (on_foot, mounted, wheeled, boat, flying, burrowing, submerged, incorporeal, swimming, climbing); a way that finds little passage in the land it seeks adds legs. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Expeditions into sites, wilds, underworlds and realms: a party, provisions, light, tools and medicine spent leg by leg, camps and ambushes](interconnections.md#m-expeditions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Leader | The mortal who leads it |
| Target | Where it goes |
| Mode | How it travels; leave empty for the way with the best passage |
| Reason | Why you send it |

<a id="t-son-set_circuit"></a>

#### Set circuit

You set a ceremonial circuit turning among communities in your reach: a treasure passes from each stop to the next every epoch, lending standing to whoever holds it and raising regard between the stops. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Ceremonial circuits: a treasure passes from stop to stop, lending standing and binding distant communities](interconnections.md#m-ceremonial_circuits).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | What the circuit is called |
| Stop | The communities it passes through, in order, at least two |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-son-set_storylet"></a>

#### Set storylet

You set a storylet (lost_heir, hoarded_store, beast_demand, forbidden_text, defiled_site, wedding_challenge, dying_ruler, insulted_envoy) in motion in your world, if mortals fit its parts there; the one it falls to must choose what follows. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Storylets: typed event chains with conditions, a cast, two to four choices with typed effects, follow-ups and cooldowns](interconnections.md#m-storylets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Storylet | The storylet |
| Reason | Why you set it in motion |

<a id="t-son-shape_folk"></a>

#### Shape folk

You set the customs of a people that worships you: its way of war, the axes of its style, funeral rite, what is done with its dead, inheritance, what it eats or how far it keeps one practice. Its customs of union are set by shaping its unions. The people holds to what you set until the Creator releases it. Cost: 20 essence for each custom; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Death work: what each people does with its dead and who does the work, with standing that calls mortals to it or drives them from it and weighs their marriages](interconnections.md#m-death_work) and [Practices: every practice a community keeps follows what it knows, holds and has built, and acts on its order, health, wealth, learning and defence](interconnections.md#m-facets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people that worships you |
| Martial style | Way of war: shieldwall, horse_archer, skirmisher, chariot, phalanx, shock, naval_raider, siege_engineer, war_magic, beast_war, mechanised, air_mobile, guerrilla, drone_war, orbital, combined_arms |
| Ornament | How much their art and building decorate, from 0 (plain) to 100 (dense with ornament) |
| Geometry | How far their forms keep to measured shapes, from 0 (free and flowing) to 100 (strictly geometric) |
| Colour | How strongly they use colour, from 0 (muted) to 100 (vivid) |
| Scale | The size they favour, from 0 (small and close) to 100 (vast) |
| Symmetry | How much they favour balance, from 0 (asymmetric) to 100 (strictly symmetric) |
| Funeral rite | How the dead are handled: burial, cremation, sky_burial, sea_burial, entombment, ancestor_shrine, endocannibalism, reanimation, soul_binding, nameless, none, mummification, secondary_burial, return_to_soil, memorial_only |
| Remains treatment | What is done with the remains, within what the funeral rite allows: burial, cremation, exposure, preservation, water, consumption, storage, return_to_maker, release, none_needed |
| Inheritance | Inheritance (equal_shares, primogeniture, ultimogeniture, through_second_line, elective, by_merit, seniority, designated, communal) |
| Diet | What they eat: herbivore, carnivore, omnivore, scavenger, filter, photosynthetic, mineral, soul, void, piscivore, sanguivore, parasite, decomposer, chemosynthetic, energy, insectivore |
| Practice | A practice of theirs to set: prehistory, marine_industries, animal_by_products, agricultural_science, floriculture, meat_packing, confectionery, cannibalism, non_alcoholic_beverages, beverage_industries, stimulant_leaves, leather_industry, cordage, knots_and_lashings, felted_fabrics, paper_industry, clothing, normal_garb, clothing_manufacture, ornament, jewellery_manufacture, woodworking, ceramics, stone_tools, masonry, tall_frame_building, carpentry, electrical_installation, other_building_trades, heating_and_lighting, lifts_and_building_machines, public_utilities, parks, other_facilities, electric_power, fission_and_fusion, elastic_materials, synthetic_materials, paints_and_dyes, fertilizers, soap_and_cleaners, explosives, capital_goods, hardware, machine_industries, electrical_supplies, making_heating_and_lighting, optics, rail_equipment, aircraft_industry, machines, electrical_machines, household_machines, weight_moving_machines, computing, other_hardware, appliances, apparatus, division_of_labour, railways, navigation, aircraft, aviation, airfields, music, musical_instruments, spectacles, status_by_sex, union_payments, nuptials, celibacy, plural_unions, clans, moieties, forms_of_government, offences_against_persons, union_offences, property_offences, offences_against_the_state, military_engineering, ordnance, military_vehicles, military_aircraft, special_military_equipment, healing_rites, spiritual_healers, medical_personnel, suicide, dying, cult_of_the_dead, asceticism, logic, philosophy, scientific_method, humanistic_study, vocational_education, teachers, life_stages, coming_of_age, roles_by_sex |
| Practice level | How far they keep that practice, from 0 (not at all) to 100 (fully); it then follows what they know, hold and build |
| Reason | Why you give them these ways |

<a id="t-son-spread_word"></a>

#### Spread word

You spread word of someone in a community: their name grows or darkens there for one kind of reputation (heroism, honour, cruelty, piety, generosity, wisdom, trickery, skill_renown, troublemaker, mercy, justice, reliability or hospitality). Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Every community holds its own reputation of each subject on nine axes, set by deeds seen or heard of and read through its ethics](interconnections.md#m-local_reputation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Subject | Whose name you spread |
| Community | Where the word is heard |
| Axis | The kind of reputation |
| Raise it | True to raise it, false to lower it |
| Reason | Why you spread it |

<a id="t-son-stir_crowd"></a>

#### Stir crowd

You stir the folk of a town in your reach with a grievance (price_spike, execution, sermon, omen, rumour, fire, plague, beast, eclipse, miracle, famine, unpaid_wages, lost_match, victory, great_funeral, election, price_rise, arrest); the feelings it grows from rise (anger, fear, fervour, grief, hope, joy, solidarity) and the town may act. Name an act (riot, panic, lynching, conversion, revolt, strike, procession, revival, jubilation, demonstration, boycott, exodus, vigil, occupation, petition) to call them to it now, or leave it for the town's own grievance to choose. Its guards and garrison hold them back. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Crowds: each town's anger, fear and fervour, and threshold cascades in a district that turn a trigger into a riot, a panic or a movement](interconnections.md#m-crowds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Grievance | The grievance you stir |
| Act | The act you call them to, or none |
| Reason | Why you stir them |

<a id="t-son-stir_identity"></a>

#### Stir identity

You make one of the identities of a town or people within your reach matter more to it, or quiet it. The identity that matters most decides whom its folk trust, how loyal they stay to their realm and along which line it divides. Layers: people, faith, class, locality, house, profession, generation, polity, circle, language, kind, cause, gift, calling. You may work through a driver (baseline, holy_war, famine, invasion, strike, rhetoric, segregation, crossing, god, persecution, commemoration, election, contest, disaster, arrivals); each stirs only the layers it reaches, and god is your own hand on any layer. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Identity layers and salience: holy war, hunger, invasion, strikes, preaching, segregation and mixed marriages set which identity matters](interconnections.md#m-identities).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The town or people |
| Layer | Which identity |
| Driver | What you stir it through |
| Calm | True to quiet the identity instead of stirring it |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="t-son-stir_kin"></a>

#### Stir kin

Stir the family of a house within your reach: toward favour for a child, rivalry between children, mending those cast out, or more sway for its elder.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Family dynamics: a parent's favour, rivalries between kin, casting out and mending, step kin and the sway of elders](interconnections.md#m-kin_dynamics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Household or house | The house |
| Kind | Toward what: favour, rivalry, reconcile or elder |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-stir_labour"></a>

#### Stir labour

Stir the workers of one trade in a town within your reach: the body that speaks for them gains solidarity, or they found one if none does. Solidarity strengthens them when they bargain and keeps them out longer in a dispute.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organised labour: worker bodies with members, dues and funds, contracts that set wages and conditions, strikes, lockouts and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-labour_relations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The town |
| Trade | The trade whose workers you stir |
| Reason | Why you stir them |

<a id="t-son-sway_org"></a>

#### Sway org

Set the course of an organisation within your reach, over the head of its decision body; the course decides what it spends, whom it lobbies, what it researches and whom it bids for.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation |
| Strategy | Its new course: grow, hold, specialise, diversify, expand_abroad, integrate_up, integrate_down, lobby, research or withdraw |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-son-swear_covenant"></a>

#### Swear covenant

You make a covenant (alliance) with a sibling. The relationship between you must be at least 25 (inspect_sibling shows the 'Towards you' number; if it is below, first get closer with call_sibling or give_gift). A covenant strengthens the relationship; challenging a covenanted sibling breaks the covenant and ruins your fame. Does not spend an action.

- **Costs** nothing.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The sibling's id |
| Terms | The covenant's condition, its oath |

<a id="p-son-household"></a>

### Household

4 tools: [bless bloodline](#t-son-bless_bloodline), [found house](#t-son-found_house), [shape kinship](#t-son-shape_kinship), [shape unions](#t-son-shape_unions).

<a id="t-son-bless_bloodline"></a>

#### Bless bloodline

You mark a noble house's line with a lasting gift or curse (long_lived, giant_blooded, god_blooded, cursed, fertile, seer_gift, strong_blooded, swift, quick_healing, sharp_sensed, steady, quick_minded); every child born to it carries it. A god-blooded house anchors your existence while it lives. Costs essence.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| House | The house |
| Trait | The bloodline trait |
| Revoke | True to lift it instead |

<a id="t-son-found_house"></a>

#### Found house

You found a noble house on a living mortal you reach; their children are born into it. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The id of the mortal |
| Reason | Why you found it |

<a id="t-son-shape_kinship"></a>

#### Shape kinship

You change how a people in your reach reckons kin: descent (through_first_line, through_second_line, bilateral, double, ambilineal), where couples live (with_first_family, with_second_family, new_household, with_kin_of_the_first_line), how they name kin (generational, lineal, bifurcate_merging, bifurcate_collateral, cross_parallel, descriptive), how they take in children who are not their own (heir, ward, foster, sponsor, blood_bond, chosen_kin) and which sex they count as the first side (bearer, begetter). Inheritance, matches and alliances between lines follow. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Kinship systems and union alliance: descent, residence, descent groups, kin terms, preferred partners, payments and sibling unions](interconnections.md#m-kinship_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Descent | How descent is reckoned |
| Residence | Where a couple lives |
| Kin terms | How they name their kin |
| Adoption | How they take in children who are not their own |
| First side | Which sex they count as the first side |
| Reason | Why you reshape it |

<a id="t-son-shape_unions"></a>

#### Shape unions

You change the customs of union of a people in your reach: the form a union takes (pair, plural, group, serial, contract, posthumous, none), which side may take several partners when the form is plural (first, second, either), what unions are for (kinship, alliance, inheritance, love, faith, labour, status), where the joined live (with_first_family, with_second_family, new_household, separate), through whom descent runs (through_first, through_second, through_both, chosen, by_house_rule), what passes between families (to_one_family, from_one_family, mutual, service, none), who arranges a union (arranged, chosen, both_consent), how a union ends (none, by_either, by_court, by_custom), the circle partners must come from or go outside of (none, people, class, caste, faith, house, line, moiety) and which kin they match with (none, cross_cousin, parallel_cousin). First and second are the two sides of a union as the people names them. Matches, households, payments, partings and lines follow. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Customs of union by axes: form, purpose, residence, descent, exchange, arrangement, dissolution and the circles partners come from](interconnections.md#m-union_customs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Form | The form a union takes |
| Plural side | Which side may take several partners in a plural form |
| Purpose | What unions are made for |
| Residence | Where the joined live |
| Descent | Through whom descent runs |
| Exchange | What passes between the families |
| Arrangement | Who arranges a union |
| Dissolution | How a union may end |
| Endogamy | The circle partners must share |
| Exogamy | The circle partners must come from outside of |
| Kin match | Which kin they match with |
| Reason | Why you reshape it |

<a id="p-son-demography"></a>

### Demography

6 tools: [loose disease](#t-son-loose_disease), [move fertility](#t-son-move_fertility), [move population policy](#t-son-move_population_policy), [open rebirth gate](#t-son-open_rebirth_gate), [shape biology](#t-son-shape_biology), [shape reproduction](#t-son-shape_reproduction).

<a id="t-son-loose_disease"></a>

#### Loose disease

You forge a new sickness of your own design and loose it on a region or community: you choose how it spreads (route), the body functions it strikes, how readily it passes on, how many it kills, how long it hides before the sick show it, which beast carries it and the warmth and moisture where it spreads fastest. It spreads along roads, armies and journeys by its route, survivors grow immune, and you gain essence from every outbreak of it. 40 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 40 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Typed diseases: vector, target functions, host species and origin event](interconnections.md#m-disease_type).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The region or community it first strikes |
| Name | The sickness's name in the world's language |
| Route | How it passes on: inhalation (crowds and cold), water (rivers, marsh, poor sanitation), contact (traders, armies and travellers), bite (needs its host beast), spirit (magic, ley lines, the impious), void (lands touched by void leakage), curse (bound to you while you live), ingestion (food and stores), blood, birth (parent to child), rite, gaze, experience, intimate or objects (goods and bedding along trade); rite, gaze and experience pass afflictions, not sickness, and are refused here |
| Target functions | The body functions it strikes; survivors may keep a lasting weakness there |
| Virulence | How readily it spreads, 1 to 100 |
| Lethality | How many of the sick die, 1 to 100 |
| Incubation | Sampled years before the sick show it (0 to 20): long incubation spreads it unseen, past quarantines |
| Chronic rate | How many survivors keep a lasting weakness, 0 to 100 |
| Affinity warmth | The warmth where it spreads fastest: warm, cold or any |
| Affinity moisture | The moisture where it spreads fastest: wet, dry or any |
| Host species | Optional: the species that carries it; killing that species weakens the sickness |
| Severity | How hard the first outbreak strikes, 1 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you loose it |

<a id="t-son-move_fertility"></a>

#### Move fertility

Change how well a means of preventing, seeking or ending conception works for a people within your reach, teaching it to them if they lack it.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fertility control: the means by which peoples prevent, seek or end conception, held open, hidden or forbidden](interconnections.md#m-fertility_means).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The people, or a community of it |
| Means | The means |
| Reliability change | How much more or less reliable it becomes, from -50 to 50 |
| Reason | Why you change it |

<a id="t-son-move_population_policy"></a>

#### Move population policy

Turn the policy a realm, people, faction, cult or town holds on its numbers to a new aim. Its instruments stay as they are; the holder reweighs them as its numbers answer.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Policies on numbers: aims and instruments that shift births, movement, health and grievance, paid for, resisted, reviewed and repealed](interconnections.md#m-population_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder node | The realm, people, faction, cult or town |
| Aim | The aim: grow, hold, shrink, rebalance_age, rebalance_class, settle_land, empty_land, keep_kind, mix_kinds or none |
| Reason | Why you turn it |

<a id="t-son-open_rebirth_gate"></a>

#### Open rebirth gate

By your own hand you open the gate of rebirth for a soul: it is born again as a newborn in a community, carrying some of its old traits and faint memories. Only for souls of your own faith, or any soul if you are a god of death; a sealed soul cannot return. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Reaches** [A reborn soul comes back to its people, its house or by merit, as its doctrine teaches; a god may open the gate by its own hand](interconnections.md#m-rebirth_paths).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The soul (a spirit) to send back |
| Community | Optional: the community it is born into; otherwise its doctrine decides |

<a id="t-son-shape_biology"></a>

#### Shape biology

You give a people or species in your reach a variant of body, or take one from it. Its variants decide how much it eats, how many it bears, how it dies and sickens, how it holds in battle, how fire and storms harm it, whether it breathes, drinks and ages, and how its minds lean. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Biology variants: each people and species carries the variants of its body as values the god can set, and eating, births, deaths, sickness, battle fear, fire, storms, ageing and the leanings of minds read them](interconnections.md#m-biology_variants).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | The people or species you reshape |
| Variant | The variant of body you give or take |
| Gain | True to give the variant, false to take it away |
| Reason | Why you reshape it |

<a id="t-son-shape_reproduction"></a>

#### Shape reproduction

You change how a people in your reach bears its young (two_sex, sequential, ambisexual, caste, asexual); births, the sexes of the newborn, castes, marriage and growth follow. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Reproductive systems: two sexes, sequential, ambisexual, caste or budding peoples, read by births, marriage, growth and levies](interconnections.md#m-reproduction_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| System | How they bear their young |
| Reason | Why you change it |

<a id="t-son-break_market"></a>

#### Break market

Open a market within your reach to newcomers for one good: its barriers to entry fall, the compact that fixes its terms breaks, a price war in it ends and a single seller there is split in two.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Market structure and competition: sellers and shares, concentration, barriers to entry, compacts, price wars and the law of fair dealing](interconnections.md#m-competition).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Market | The market town |
| Goods kind | The good whose market you open |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-build_structure"></a>

#### Build structure

Creates a Structure. 1 action. Cost: 9+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 9 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Every item, structure and vessel is made of a material its makers can work and hold](interconnections.md#m-made_materials), [Materials and what they do](interconnections.md#m-materials), [Skills, callings and teaching](interconnections.md#m-skills) and [Typed structures: kind, level, capacity, material, builder, architect, aesthetic and ley line](interconnections.md#m-structure_profiles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. The domain used in this creation; one of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science. Your own domains are cheap. |
| Power level | From 1 up to your power ceiling. Increases the cost. |
| Stats | Optional stats; leave out any you do not want to set |
| Links | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Links › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Links › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Links › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |
| Links › Description | Short description of the bond. Use related only if no concrete type fits, and then you MUST write the relation here. |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside (Place, Region, Settlement). It must have a location: if you leave it empty it is inferred from the region, place or settlement among your bonds (or from where the community you bond to is located) |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Structure kind | What it is and what it does: temple (Temple), shrine (Shrine), monastery (Monastery), necropolis (Necropolis), library (Library), school (School), observatory (Observatory), academy (Academy), hospital (Hospital), wall (Walls), gate (Gatehouse), tower (Tower), citadel (Citadel), watchtower (Watchtower), fort (Fort), palace (Palace), court (Court), prison (Prison), market <a id="t-son-close_trade_route"></a>

#### Close trade route

You close a trade route: no goods pass, and merchants found no new way between its two markets until a god opens it again. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Reaches** [Closed and held routes: a way closed by the Creator or a god is never founded again until it is opened, and held values stay as set](interconnections.md#m-route_holds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Route | The trade route to close |
| Reason | Why you close it |

<a id="t-son-commission_structure"></a>

#### Commission structure

You set a settlement or people to raise a work in your name: its builders, materials and coin raise it over the coming ages, and when it stands it is dedicated to you (fame, faith, and it can anchor your existence). The kind decides what it does. 15 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.
- **Reaches** [Typed structures: kind, level, capacity, material, builder, architect, aesthetic and ley line](interconnections.md#m-structure_profiles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The settlement or people that builds it |
| Structure kind | What it is: temple (Temple), shrine (Shrine), monastery (Monastery), necropolis (Necropolis), library (Library), school (School), observatory (Observatory), academy (Academy), <a id="t-son-create_mortal"></a>

#### Create mortal

Creates a Mortal. Living creation: the first living creation each age does NOT spend an action; later ones cost 1 action. Cost: 5+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 5 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is common or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. Creates a People. Living creation: the first living creation each age does NOT spend an action; later ones cost 1 action. Cost: 9+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. <a id="t-son-create_region"></a>

#### Create region

Creates a Region. 1 action; if your world is crowded (20+ communities per region) the age's first land creation that eases the crowding (a new region, or a place in a crowded region) does not spend an action and grants +10 essence, +3 fame; later ones earn nothing. Cost: 20+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 20 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

<a id="t-son-decree_natural_law"></a>

#### Decree natural law

Creates a Natural law. 1 action. Cost: 30+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 30 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. The domain used in this creation; one of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science. Your own domains are cheap. |
| Power level | From 1 up to your power ceiling. Increases the cost. |
| Stats | Optional stats; leave out any you do not want to set |
| Links | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Links › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Links › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Links › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |
| Links › Description | Short description of the bond. Use related only if no concrete type fits, and then you MUST write the relation here. |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside () |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Mechanic | The law's mechanical effect in the world (if left empty it is read from the law's text): none: Narrative only; no mechanical effect.; immortality: Mortals do not age; population grows more slowly.; short_life: Mortals age faster; population grows faster.; dead_return: Fallen heroes may return as ghosts.; magic_price: Peoples steeped in magic pay part of their population to it every epoch.; war_ban: Wars break out less often but are bloodier.; bounty: The fertility of every land rises each epoch.; drought: The fertility of every land falls each epoch.; sea_ban: Seas cannot be crossed; seafaring is useless.; one_language: Languages spread faster through conquest and trade.; disasters: Natural disasters happen more often.; souls_reincarnate: Souls that pass judgement may be born again into a new life.; gift_flowering: The magical gift is born more often in every bloodline.; gift_withering: The magical gift is born more rarely and fades from bloodlines.; no_iron: Iron cannot be smelted or worked.; eternal_night: The sun does not rise over its reach; fields yield far less.; magic_requires_blood: Every rite costs the caster's blood as well as its price.; gravity_shift: Things weigh differently; building and marching are slower.; seasons_reversed: The seasons run backwards; growth comes in the cold half of the year.; long_seasons: Summers and winters run for years of no set length; scholars forecast their turn and peoples store food against them.; dreams_are_real: Dream routes never close and mortals dream more often.; oaths_bind: Whoever breaks an oath is branded at once.; the_dead_speak: Spirits take turns in the world: they speak, send word, foretell and pray, and tell the legends of the places they dwell in.; metal_rusts_fast: Metal weapons and tools wear out far faster.; no_gunpowder: Powder will not burn: no gunpowder, firearms or cannon can be made. |

<a id="t-son-designate_keystone"></a>

#### Designate keystone

You make the yield of one species in one land the keystone of its world, needed by an institution (navigation, longevity, prescience, rite) and held sacred by a cult; harvesting it thins its producers, whoever holds its source grows rich, and realms that want it may go to war. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [A world-defining resource: one producer's yield needed by an institution and held sacred, its harvest, monopoly, scarcity and wars](interconnections.md#m-keystone_resource).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The species whose yield it is |
| Region | The land it comes from |
| Name | What the resource is called |
| Use | What institution needs it |
| Cult | The cult that holds it sacred, if any |
| Reason | Why you make it so |

<a id="t-son-forge_material"></a>

#### Forge material

You forge a new material into a world, a substance of its own with every property you name; you may set a vein of it into a land. Its makers can work it once they know its branch to its level. 40 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 40 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Gods forge new materials with every property they name](interconnections.md#m-divine_materials).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world it belongs to |
| Name | Its proper name |
| Family | Its family: organic, stone, metal, precious, exotic |
| Base kind | Optional: the catalog material it is akin to (wood, calcified_tissue, hide, leather, cloth, stone, flint, obsidian, copper, tin, bronze, iron, steel, silver, gold, glass, crystal, ivory, chitin, great_beast_tissue, star_metal, void_glass, divine_tissue, living_wood, halite, amber, lead, skysteel, sulfur, clay, gems, mud_brick, fired_brick, dressed_stone, rubble, lime_mortar, hydraulic_concrete, thatch, tile, ceramic, silk, resin, shell, polymer, composite, rubber, paper, lacquer); unset properties follow it |
| Hardness | 0 to 100: edge retention and armour |
| Toughness | 0 to 100: resistance to breaking |
| Weight | 0 to 100: burden on marches and in heat |
| Edge | 0 to 100: cutting quality |
| Conductivity | 0 to 100: how much enchantment it holds |
| Sanctity | 0 to 100: holiness; harms the undead, the cursed and the void |
| Corruption | 0 to 100: taint; lures outer beings and curses |
| Rarity | 0 to 100: scarcity; raises the price of goods made of it |
| Base value | 0 to 100: worth of goods made of it |
| Decay | 0 to 100: how fast it rots, rusts or crumbles |
| Branch | Optional knowledge branch needed to work it: agriculture (Knowers' population grows faster; famine and drought strike more softly.); metallurgy (Weapons and armour they produce are stronger.); seafaring (Trade, war and migration with communities beyond the sea.); writing (Their languages grow complex; what they know spreads faster to partners.); architecture (Their structures are grander and sturdier, and withstand earthquakes.); medicine (Plagues strike more softly; their mortals live longer.); magic (Their magic grows and the items they produce gain might.); warfare (Their armies fight harder.); astronomy (They read the star of the epoch, sense disasters early and suffer them less.); trade (Their trade routes bring more prosperity.); sky_navigation (Can discover routes between worlds; journeys are less likely to go astray.); shipbuilding (Can build ships that sail between worlds.); gate_lore (Can open and close ancient gates; gate journeys are swift.); pathfinding (Can fold space with the cosmic travel resource.); void_living (Can settle worlds with thin or no air; loses fewer travellers.); immortality (Their mortals reach for immortality.); biomancy (Combines and alters living things: merges species into new ones and steers mutations.); forbidden (Great power in war and magic, at the price of sanity and a leak into their world.); civics (Their laws, offices, faith and thought grow into institutions that shape how the realm is run.); law (Their courts and codes grow; written and codified law come within reach sooner.); natural_history (They know living kinds better: breeding, anatomy and new materials open to them.); language_lore (They read and translate other tongues; ciphers and interpreters come easier.); mathematics (Their reckoning sharpens engineering, navigation and accounts, up to signs for unknowns.) |
| Level | Level of that branch needed |
| Place | Optional: a land or place where a vein of it is set |
| Reason | Why you forge it |

For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |

Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside (Place, Region, Settlement). It must have a location: if you leave it empty it is inferred from the region, place or settlement among your bonds (or from where the community you bond to is located) |

| Worshipped son | Whom they worship (son id); you if empty. Faith flows this way. |
| Tenets | The tenets you teach this cult (at most 6; opposites cannot stand together): pacifism (The faithful keep fewer warriors and a devout ruler loses face for every war.); holy_war (Armies fight harder against unbelievers; peace with them costs zeal.); asceticism (Comfort does not erode the faith; tithes go to alms, not temples.); sacred_suffering (The maimed are honoured among the faithful.); purity_of_body (The maimed are unclean among the faithful.); ancestor_veneration (Ancestor spirits are fed with offerings and guard their people.); sacrifice_of_people (Captives are given to the god; other peoples hold it against the faith.); animal_sacrifice (Livestock is offered to the god each epoch.); celibate_clergy (The clergy found no houses.); proselytism (Missionaries travel the roads and convert harder.); exclusivity (No other god is revered beside this one.); syncretic_openness (Other gods are gladly revered beside this one.); monastic_learning (Monasteries keep knowledge alive through dark ages.); anti_magic (Wizards are hunted and magic is shunned.); sanctified_magic (Magic is a holy gift and its users are revered.); judgement_by_deeds (The dead are judged by what they did.); judgement_by_faith (The dead are judged by what they believed.); reincarnation (Exceptional souls may be born again.); martyrdom_glory (Martyrs stir the faith and the fallen go to the realm that takes those who die in battle.); divine_rule (Rulers are the god's own and draw legitimacy from the faith.); dietary_law (One food is forbidden to the faithful.); sacred_species (One beast is sacred; its hunters are hated.); pilgrimage_duty (The faithful must journey to a holy place.); souls_rest (Souls are sealed against return.); void_communion (The faithful commune with a being from outside; they and their home lose sanity and the world leaks.); feeding_the_hunger (The faithful make offerings that feed the being they worship.); whispered_secrets (The being they worship passes forbidden knowledge to their home.); hidden_god (The god's silence does not shake the faith; the faithful hold that it works unseen.) |
| Judgement | How its god judges the dead: deeds, faith, rite, favour, none, remembrance |
| Rebirth path | Where its exceptional souls are reborn: people, house, merit, kind, place (people: the same people; house: the same house; merit: by their deeds) |

| Stats › Zeal | Zeal (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Influence | Influence (0 to 100) |

<a id="t-son-found_settlement"></a>

#### Found settlement

Creates a Settlement. Living creation: the first living creation each age does NOT spend an action; later ones cost 1 action. Cost: 6+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 6 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is common or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |

<a id="t-son-harvest_resource"></a>

#### Harvest resource

You draw essence from a Resource entity. Resources you created yourself, resources of your own domain, or resources owned by those who worship you can be harvested. Its abundance decreases. Does not spend an action; each resource once per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
