<a id="t-son-make_bond"></a>

#### Make bond

You declare a narrative bond between two existing things (descends_from, source_of, tells_of, loves, fears, prophesies, caused, celebrates, forbids, related, pursues, companion, part_of). It adds a connection to the world. Worship, enmity, knowledge, ownership, captivity and alliance are not made with this tool; they have their own tools. Needs 2 essence, does not spend an action, 3 per age.

- **Costs** no action points and 2 essence.
- **At most** 3 times an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Source | The entity that is the source of the bond |
| Target | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Target › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Target › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Target › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |
| Target › Description | Short description of the bond. Use related only if no concrete type fits, and then you MUST write the relation here. |

<a id="t-son-open_road"></a>

#### Open road

You open a road between two known worlds, one of which you reach, by a way of travel you choose. 45 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 45 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First world | The id of the first world |
| Second world | The id of the second world |
| Method | The way of travel |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-open_trade_route"></a>

#### Open trade route

You lay a trade route between two markets of the world you are on (peoples or settlements): merchants carry goods along it from this age, the cheap market selling to the dear one, tolls fall to the masters of the lands it crosses. If you or a sibling closed the way between them before, it opens again. 25 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Reaches** [Closed and held routes: a way closed by the Creator or a god is never founded again until it is opened, and held values stay as set](interconnections.md#m-route_holds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First | The first market (a people or settlement) |
| Second | The second market |
| Mode | How goods travel: road, river, sea, caravan or sky (leave empty to let the land decide) |
| Reason | Why you lay this road |

<a id="t-son-shake_coin"></a>

#### Shake coin

Shake or steady trust in a currency whose issuer is within your reach. Trust moves prices, the value of the currency against others and whether a peg can be held.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Monetary authorities: rates, issuing for the state, pegs, reserves and the lender of last resort](interconnections.md#m-money_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Currency | The currency |
| Trust change | How far trust moves, from -30 to 30 |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-shield_convoy"></a>

#### Shield convoy

You guard a convoy on the road: it gains guards against raiders and sets out again if weather held it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Convoys: routes carry their goods in convoys with guards, migrants and missionaries, raided by bandits and beasts, lost at sea or delayed by weather](interconnections.md#m-convoys).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Convoy | The convoy |
| Reason | Why you guard it |

<a id="t-son-stir_market_mood"></a>

#### Stir market mood

Lift or sink the mood of an exchange in a town within your reach. Mood moves every price listed there; a lifted mood feeds bubbles, a sunk one feeds crashes.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Securities and exchanges: listed claims, prices moved by results, rates, mood and herding, bubbles, crashes, margin calls and bonds](interconnections.md#m-securities).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Exchange | The exchange |
| Direction | Which way the mood turns: up or down |
| Reason | Why you move it |

Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside () |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Legend category | What the legend would become if belief ever made it walk the world: creature, spirit, resource, relic |
| Archetype | The kind of tale it is, which sets the lesson it presses on its tellers: hero_quest, monster, trickster, creation, world_disaster, betrayal, lost_golden_age, chosen_one, cursed_line, return_of_a_ruler, haunted_place, founding, exile, transformation, bargain, sacrifice, rivalry, discovery, deception, union, ascent, fall |
| Stats › Spread | Spread (0 to 100) |

<a id="t-son-utter_prophecy"></a>

#### Utter prophecy

Creates a Prophecy. 1 action. Cost: 10+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 10 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. The domain used in this creation; one of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science. Your own domains are cheap. |
| Power level | From 1 up to your power ceiling. Increases the cost. |
| Stats | Optional stats; leave out any you do not want to set |
| Links | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Links › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Links › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Links › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |
| Links › Description | Short description of the bond. Use related only if no concrete type fits, and then you MUST write the relation here. |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside () |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Predicate | What exactly must happen for it to come true, checked by the world itself: dies, falls, born_in, sky_event, war_between, ruler_of, within_epochs |
| Subject | The one the foretelling names: who dies (dies), what falls (falls), the house (born_in), the realm (ruler_of), the first side (war_between); a sky event (sky_event) and a span of ages (within_epochs) name no one |
| Second | For war_between: the second side |
| Archetype | For ruler_of: the kind of legend the coming ruler must fit (hero_quest, monster, trickster, creation, world_disaster, betrayal, lost_golden_age, chosen_one, cursed_line, return_of_a_ruler, haunted_place, founding, exile, transformation, bargain, sacrifice, rivalry, discovery, deception, union, ascent, fall) |
| Sky kind | For sky_event: the sign in the sky (eclipse, comet, alignment, supernova, black_sun, void_storm, flare, aurora, transit) |
| Event kind | For within_epochs: the kind of happening foretold |
| Within epochs | In how many ages it must come true; if it does not, the seer loses standing |
| Consequence | What it means when it comes true: rule, fall, death, war, birth, omen |
| Consequence | Whom the consequence touches |
| Stats › Certainty | Certainty (0 to 100) |

<a id="p-son-polity"></a>

### Polity

22 tools: [anoint founder](#t-son-anoint_founder), [begin long plan](#t-son-begin_long_plan), [bind league](#t-son-bind_league), [bless works](#t-son-bless_works), [call to step aside](#t-son-call_to_step_aside), [cast rite](#t-son-cast_rite), [commission artwork](#t-son-commission_artwork), [crown ruler](#t-son-crown_ruler), [found school](#t-son-found_school), [further plan](#t-son-further_plan), [inspire relief](#t-son-inspire_relief), [limit fleets](#t-son-limit_fleets), [make interworld treaty](#t-son-make_interworld_treaty), [move economic order](#t-son-move_economic_order), [proclaim right](#t-son-proclaim_right), [raise throne](#t-son-raise_throne), [seal treaty](#t-son-seal_treaty), [shape rule](#t-son-shape_rule), [stir memory](#t-son-stir_memory), [sway cult](#t-son-sway_cult), [sway vote](#t-son-sway_vote), [void treaty](#t-son-void_treaty).

<a id="t-son-anoint_founder"></a>

#### Anoint founder

You mark the ruler of a realm in your reach as its charismatic founder; their charisma rises, and at their death the realm must turn that charisma into a dynasty or into rules, or split. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Kinds of legitimacy and the routinisation of charisma: custom, charisma and rules, and the crisis at a founder's death](interconnections.md#m-legitimacy_types).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm |
| Reason | Why you mark its ruler |

<a id="t-son-begin_long_plan"></a>

#### Begin long plan

You set in motion a long plan that outlives any one epoch (ecological_transformation, bloodline, conversion, wonder, forecast, network, megastructure, programme, realm, line, reform, exploration, restoration): it advances step by step, may come to light the less secret it is, and is fulfilled when its last step is taken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Long plans: ecological transformations, bloodlines, conversions and wonders held by realms, orders and gods, carried past their authors](interconnections.md#m-long_plans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What the plan aims at |
| Name | What the plan is called |
| Target | The land, world, house, people or town it aims at |
| Steps | Steps it needs, 1 to 100 |
| Secrecy | How secret it is kept, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you begin it |

<a id="t-son-bind_league"></a>

#### Bind league

Strengthen or loosen a league of realms whose members are within your reach. Its strength weighs on whether members obey its decisions and pay its dues.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organisations of polities: leagues with charters, dues, votes and vetoes, sanctions, courts, common force, shared money and federation](interconnections.md#m-leagues).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| League | The league |
| Strength change | How far its strength moves, from -30 to 30 |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-bless_works"></a>

#### Bless works

Bless public works: their quality rises, and finished works are made whole again.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Works | The works |
| Reason | Why you bless them |

<a id="t-son-call_to_step_aside"></a>

#### Call to step aside

Call one within your reach who holds an office of a realm or heads an organisation to step aside. Most heed it and leave by abdication, retirement, resignation, withdrawal or exile, as their state asks; some refuse.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Leaving office: abdication, retirement, resignation, withdrawal and exile by choice, with pensions, rallying points and returns](interconnections.md#m-departures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | The one you call |
| Reason | Why you call them |

<a id="t-son-cast_rite"></a>

#### Cast rite

You work a rite you know. It must be lawful where it is worked, its requirements met and its price paid; it may backfire. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Rite | The rite to work (a rite id) |
| Target | Whom or where it is aimed at, if anyone |
| Vessel | The worshipping community the rite is worked through; you pay its price in essence |

<a id="t-son-commission_artwork"></a>

#### Commission artwork

You commission a work of art. It moves the views of those who hold it, raises the standing of whom it portrays, and a realm or town pays for it from its treasury. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for it, in the world's naming language |
| Medium | What kind of work: verbal, musical, movement, flat_image, solid_image, built, worn, culinary, scent, light, recorded, interactive, landscape, body, ritual_performance, textile_image, made_mind_art, tactile, mind; songs, plays and dances live only while someone can perform them |
| Subject | Whom or what it portrays (an entity id), if anyone |
| Author | The artist who makes it (a mortal id), if any; a skilled hand makes a finer work |
| Patron | Who commissions and keeps it (a community id); one of your worshipping communities |

<a id="t-son-crown_ruler"></a>

#### Crown ruler

You set a ruler in power over a people, settlement, faction or cult you reach that has none. 25 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The id of the community |
| Reason | Why it needs a ruler |

<a id="t-son-found_school"></a>

#### Found school

You found a school of thought, a creed that spreads along roads, through scholars, books and schools, and can move a realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Civics gate governments, offices, laws, taxes, privileges and creeds: what a realm has not learned it cannot found](interconnections.md#m-civic_gates).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for the school or creed |
| Tenets | What it teaches, 1 to 3 tenets: legalism, restraint, egalitarianism, divine_right, mercantilism, militarism, asceticism, rationalism, nature_harmony, xenophobia, cosmopolitanism, anti_god, creatorism |
| Founder | The mortal follower who founds it in your name |

<a id="t-son-further_plan"></a>

#### Further plan

You take one of your long plans forward by a step. 1 step an epoch is its own pace. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Long plans: ecological transformations, bloodlines, conversions and wonders held by realms, orders and gods, carried past their authors](interconnections.md#m-long_plans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Plan | Your long plan |
| Reason | Why you further it |

<a id="t-son-inspire_relief"></a>

#### Inspire relief

You move a realm or a cult in your reach to send aid to a town (goods, healers, labour, money, credit, food, shelter, medicine, soldiers, knowledge, transport); aid earns gratitude, a favour and a gift owed in return, and love for the giver's god, though a rival may turn it away. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Relief diplomacy: realms and cults send goods, healers and labour to stricken towns for gratitude, favours, gifts owed and love for their god](interconnections.md#m-relief).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Giver | The realm or cult that sends it |
| Settlement | The town it reaches |
| Kind | What is sent |
| Reason | Why you move them |

<a id="t-son-limit_fleets"></a>

#### Limit fleets

You bind two realms at peace in your reach to a naval treaty capping each fleet at a tonnage; an admiralty that builds past it breaks the treaty, and one whose admiral cares little for law may hide ships until they are found out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Naval budgets, admiralty politics and naval treaties: the admiral contends with the marshal, builds ships and keeps or cheats a fleet limit](interconnections.md#m-admiralty).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First | One realm |
| Second | The other realm |
| Tonnage | The tonnage each fleet may reach |
| Frame | The class of ship the cap counts, if only one; one of: riding_mount (Riding mount), pack_mount (Pack mount), travois (Travois), sledge (Sledge), handcart (Handcart), cart (Cart), wagon (Wagon), covered_wagon (Covered wagon), carriage (Carriage), chariot (Chariot), war_wagon (War wagon), beast_carrier (Beast carrier), rail_car (Rail car), locomotive (Locomotive), steam_carriage (Steam carriage), motor_car (Motor car), motor_truck (Motor truck), motorcycle (Motorcycle), armoured_car (Armoured car), tank (Tank), walker (Walker), crawler (Crawler), construct_walker (Construct walker), construct_carrier (Construct carrier), battering_ram (Battering ram), siege_tower (Siege tower), mantlet (Mantlet), catapult (Catapult), trebuchet (Trebuchet), ballista (Ballista), bombard (Bombard), field_gun (Field gun), mortar (Mortar), rocket_battery (Rocket battery), raft (Raft), canoe (Canoe), rowboat (Rowboat), boat (Boat), river_barge (River barge), sailboat (Sailboat), galley (Galley), longship (Longship), cog (Cog), carrack (Carrack), galleon (Galleon), frigate (Frigate), warship (Warship), ship_of_the_line (Ship of the line), steamship (Steamship), ironclad (Ironclad), battleship (Battleship), submarine (Submarine), living_ship (Living ship), fire_ship (Fire ship), balloon (Balloon), airship (Airship), glider (Glider), ornithopter (Ornithopter), sky_sailer (Sky sailer), skyship (Skyship), flying_carriage (Flying carriage), aeroplane (Aeroplane), rotorcraft (Rotorcraft), flying_car (Flying car), flying_construct (Flying construct), orbital_lander (Orbital lander), shuttle (Shuttle), solar_sailer (Solar sailer), starship (Starship), generation_ship (Generation ship), void_ark (Void ark), cloud_skimmer (Cloud skimmer), aerostat (Aerostat), deep_submersible (Deep submersible), burrower (Burrower), planar_vessel (Planar vessel), hovercraft (Hovercraft), amphibious_carrier (Amphibious carrier), landing_craft (Landing craft), amphibious_raft (Amphibious raft), tunnel_cart (Tunnel cart), mine_train (Mine train), delving_construct (Delving construct), deep_crawler (Deep crawler), diving_bell (Diving bell), attack_submarine (Attack submarine), gas_harvester (Gas harvester), deep_diver (Deep diver), rift_skiff (Rift skiff), planar_ark (Planar ark), divine_vessel (Divine vessel), space_fighter (Space fighter), freighter (Freighter), mining_barge (Mining barge), corvette (Corvette), dreadnought (Dreadnought), jet_fighter (Jet fighter), bomber (Bomber), drone (Drone), trireme (Trireme), coastal_trader (Coastal trader), catamaran (Catamaran), fleet_carrier (Fleet carrier), hover_tank (Hover tank), bicycle (Bicycle), rickshaw (Rickshaw), palanquin (Palanquin), tram (Tram), maglev (Maglev), bus (Bus), tractor (Tractor), apc (Armoured personnel carrier), self_propelled_gun (Self-propelled gun), missile_carrier (Missile carrier), motorboat (Motorboat), hydrofoil (Hydrofoil), container_ship (Container ship), tanker (Tanker), destroyer (Destroyer), cruiser (Cruiser), icebreaker (Icebreaker), vtol (Vertical lift craft), spaceplane (Spaceplane), escape_pod (Escape pod), space_tug (Space tug), carrier_vessel (Carrier vessel), battle_mech (Battle mech), bioship (Bioship) |
| Reason | Why you bind them |

<a id="t-son-make_interworld_treaty"></a>

#### Make interworld treaty

You establish an interworld treaty between two communities in separate worlds: alliance or trade. There must be a known, open road between the two worlds and one of the communities must be on the world you are currently on; enemies do not make treaties. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| A | The community on the world you are on |
| B | The community on the other world |
| Kind | alliance (side by side in war) or trade (goods and prosperity flow) |
| Terms | The treaty's condition, its oath |

<a id="t-son-move_economic_order"></a>

#### Move economic order

You move one axis of the economic order of a realm in your reach. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Economic orders: a realm's ownership, allocation, labour and distribution move goods, favour classes and change in stages against their losers](interconnections.md#m-economic_orders).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm |
| Axis | The axis you move: ownership, allocation, labour or distribution |
| Value | Its new value, one the axis allows |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-proclaim_right"></a>

#### Proclaim right

You proclaim in a realm within your reach that a category of its folk (citizens, subjects, resident foreigners, protected minorities or the bound) holds or loses a right: office, marriage, arms or the courts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Civics: evidence before the courts, citizenship and its rights, borders on trade, poor relief and standard measures](interconnections.md#m-civics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The realm |
| Category | Whose right |
| Right | The right |
| Granted | Whether they hold it |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-raise_throne"></a>

#### Raise throne

You raise a throne of power at a place you reach and hold it yourself; its holder draws power from it. 80 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 80 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The id of the place, region, settlement or structure |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-seal_treaty"></a>

#### Seal treaty

You bind two or more parties (realms, communities, houses, cults, beasts) by a typed treaty sealed in your name: its kind decides how it behaves and its clauses run every age. Costs essence.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | peace, truce, alliance_defensive, alliance_offensive, non_aggression, vassalage, tribute, marriage_pact, trade_pact, condotta, guarantee, interworld, coalition, divine_pact, naval_limit, arms_limit, mutual_aid or border_accord |
| Parties | Each party's id and role (signatory, overlord, vassal, guarantor, mediator, witness) |
| Clauses | Typed clauses: kind (call_to_arms, tribute, cession, hostage, marriage, trade_rights, tolls_waived, religious_freedom, demilitarised, champion, extradition, wergild, non_aggression, route_control, resource_demand, research_agreement, fleet_limit, autonomous_weapons_ban, mind_seizure_ban, soldier_copy_ban, replicator_weapon_ban, civil_network_shield, civil_host_shield, no_mind_compulsion, no_plague_powers, no_designed_disease, mass_weapon_limit, return_of_refugees, shared_water, captive_exchange), who owes it, who gains, and its terms by name, as the clause kind requires |
| Epochs | How many ages it holds; empty for ever |
| Parties › Party | The party's id |
| Parties › Role | Its role: signatory, overlord, vassal, guarantor, mediator or witness |
| Clauses › Kind | The clause's kind |
| Clauses › Obligor | Who owes it |
| Clauses › Beneficiary | Who gains by it |
| Clauses › Params | Its terms by name, as the clause kind requires |

<a id="t-son-shape_rule"></a>

#### Shape rule

You reshape who chooses the rulers of a realm in your reach (tribe, nobles, council, army, priests, citizens, party, shareholders, algorithm) and how much of its spending goes to public goods; small circles of choosers buy loyalty with private gifts, wide ones with public works. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Selectorate and winning coalition: who chooses the ruler sets the spending mix between public goods and private rewards](interconnections.md#m-selectorate).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm |
| Selectorate | Who has a say in choosing its ruler |
| Public share | Share of its spending on public goods, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you reshape it |

<a id="t-son-stir_memory"></a>

#### Stir memory

Stir the memory of a fallen realm within your reach, so that a movement to raise it again grows stronger.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Restoration of fallen realms: memory, movements, risings, and the old realm raised again under its name, laws and rights](interconnections.md#m-restoration).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Fallen polity | The fallen realm |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-sway_cult"></a>

#### Sway cult

Raise or lower the intensity of the cult of a living leader within your reach.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cults of living leaders: instruments, legitimacy and sincerity, banned criticism, and the cult's fate at the leader's death](interconnections.md#m-leader_cults).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cult | The cult |
| Intensity change | How far its intensity moves, from -30 to 30 |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-sway_vote"></a>

#### Sway vote

Turn a vote of the people within your reach toward yes or no before it is held.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Referendums: questions put to the people, called by right, campaigned, held, found out and carried or ignored](interconnections.md#m-referendums).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Referendum | The vote |
| Side | Which way it turns: yes or no |
| Reason | Why you turn it |

<a id="t-son-void_treaty"></a>

#### Void treaty

You make one party break a treaty in force: the treaty ends, guarantors may march and the breaker loses honour. Costs essence.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Treaty | The treaty |
| Breaker | The party that breaks it |

<a id="p-son-law"></a>

### Law

5 tools: [answer oracle](#t-son-answer_oracle), [lay geas](#t-son-lay_geas), [lift geas](#t-son-lift_geas), [stir witch hunt](#t-son-stir_witch_hunt), [weigh reckoning](#t-son-weigh_reckoning).

<a id="t-son-answer_oracle"></a>

#### Answer oracle

A realm whose legal system asks the gods for verdicts waits on a case; you give the oracle your answer, true or not, and the court follows it. Left unanswered, the oracle names the truth only by chance. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Legal systems: realms judge by custom and sworn oaths, a written code, sacred law, case law, an oracle's answer or a mix](interconnections.md#m-legal_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Case | The case awaiting trial |
| Verdict | guilty or acquitted |
| Reason | Why you answer so |

<a id="t-son-lay_geas"></a>

#### Lay geas

You lay a geas on a mortal in your reach: a taboo it must never break or a duty it must keep doing (taboo: oathbreaking, guest_killing, cowardice, theft, killing_kin, desecration, betrayal, torture, magic_use, hoarding_in_famine; duty: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning; condition: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning). While it is kept you gain favour and dominion where the mortal lives; broken, its penalty falls on the mortal. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Geasa: taboos and duties laid on mortals by gods, seers and binding rites, favour while kept and doom when broken](interconnections.md#m-geasa).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Bearer | The mortal |
| Mode | taboo or duty: taboo, duty, condition |
| Act | The act it forbids or asks |
| Reason | Why you lay it |

<a id="t-son-lift_geas"></a>

#### Lift geas

You lift a geas you laid. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Geasa: taboos and duties laid on mortals by gods, seers and binding rites, favour while kept and doom when broken](interconnections.md#m-geasa).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Geas | The geas |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-stir_witch_hunt"></a>

#### Stir witch hunt

You stir a witch hunt in a town within your reach: its folk accuse one another of witchcraft each epoch until its fervour fades, and the courts try the accused. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Witchcraft: blamed misfortune brings accusations the courts try, real casters may be accused, and stress lights witch hunts](interconnections.md#m-witchcraft).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-weigh_reckoning"></a>

#### Weigh reckoning

Strengthen one measure of a reckoning in a realm within your reach, or add it if the reckoning lacks it.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Reckoning after a fallen regime: tribunals, inquiries, bars from office, amnesties, restitution, reparation, memorials and open records](interconnections.md#m-reckoning).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Reckoning | The reckoning |
| Measure | Which measure: tribunal, truth_inquiry, office_bar, amnesty, amnesty_for_confession, restitution, reparation_to_victims, memorial or record_opening |
| Reason | Why you weigh it |

<a id="p-son-intrigue"></a>

### Intrigue

1 tool: [scour streets](#t-son-scour_streets).

<a id="t-son-scour_streets"></a>

#### Scour streets

You scour a district of a town: every criminal organisation's hold on its streets weakens. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Turf: criminal organisations hold districts, grow under weak watch and fight turf wars that kill and disorder the town](interconnections.md#m-turf).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| District | The district |
| Reason | Why you scour it |

<a id="p-son-communication"></a>

### Communication

6 tools: [bless network](#t-son-bless_network), [hide truth](#t-son-hide_truth), [retell event](#t-son-retell_event), [sever network](#t-son-sever_network), [sway outlet](#t-son-sway_outlet), [unveil truth](#t-son-unveil_truth).

<a id="t-son-bless_network"></a>

#### Bless network

You bless a network in your reach: it runs more reliably, is harder to break into and comes back up if it was down. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Networks carry messages, records, control, money, minds and opinion; members come to depend on them and suffer when they go down](interconnections.md#m-networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Network | The network |
| Reason | Why you bless it |

<a id="t-son-hide_truth"></a>

#### Hide truth

You hide a truth about something in your reach from those you name; only some can perceive it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Truths are hidden from whole peoples, kept, sought, breached, covered up, revealed and in time forgotten](interconnections.md#m-concealment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Title | What the truth is |
| Subject | What it is about |
| Kind | What kind of truth: existence, nature, presence, power, event, origin, awakening, programme, cosmic, location or lineage |
| Hidden from | From whom: everyone, a_people, a_polity, the_uninitiated, a_world, the_cosmos, a_house or a_faith |
| Reason | Why you hide it |

<a id="t-son-retell_event"></a>

#### Retell event

Tells the peoples who worship you a version of a happening, changed as you choose or told truly; it spreads as a rumour, and those who knew it otherwise may contradict it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Those present at a happening witness it; retellings change its parts, truer tellings set them right and contrary ones cost trust](interconnections.md#m-witnesses_and_tellings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Incident | The happening's id |
| Distortions | The changes to make to the telling; leave it empty to tell it truly |
| Distortions › Kind | What the change does |
| Distortions › Role | The role whose holder is told otherwise, for a changed doer, one acted on or other part |
| Distortions › Fact | The key of the fact told otherwise, for a changed amount or outcome |
| Distortions › Step | The id of the step added, dropped or told otherwise |
| Distortions › Told | The id of the record told in its place |
| Distortions › Told amount | The amount told instead |
| Distortions › Told year | The year told instead |
| Distortions › Told action | What is said to have been done in an added step |
| Distortions › Told outcome | The outcome told instead |
| Distortions › Told value | The value told instead, for a fact of fixed choices or a thing used |
| Distortions › Told cause | The id of the happening told as its cause |

<a id="t-son-sever_network"></a>

#### Sever network

You bring a network in your reach down for a while; what depends on it goes without. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Networks carry messages, records, control, money, minds and opinion; members come to depend on them and suffer when they go down](interconnections.md#m-networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Network | The network |
| Reason | Why you sever it |

<a id="t-son-sway_outlet"></a>

#### Sway outlet

Turn the stance and lean of an outlet: what it tells and how its audience is moved follow the new lean.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Recorded and mass media: media and their fidelity, outlets that reach, earn, report, sway opinion and are found false, censored or seized](interconnections.md#m-media).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outlet | The outlet |
| Stance | The value it leans on |
| Lean | How far it leans, from -100 against to 100 for |
| Reason | Why you turn it |

<a id="t-son-unveil_truth"></a>

#### Unveil truth

You make a hidden truth known to a people, a realm or a town in your reach. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Truths are hidden from whole peoples, kept, sought, breached, covered up, revealed and in time forgotten](interconnections.md#m-concealment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Truth | The hidden truth |
| Population | Who comes to know it |
| Reason | Why you make it known |

<a id="p-son-war"></a>

### War

17 tools: [bless host](#t-son-bless_host), [close the way](#t-son-close_the_way), [commission vessel](#t-son-commission_vessel), [design unit](#t-son-design_unit), [disband host](#t-son-disband_host), [incite war](#t-son-incite_war), [lay siege](#t-son-lay_siege), [offer peace](#t-son-offer_peace), [plant spies](#t-son-plant_spies), [raise host](#t-son-raise_host), [shape equipment](#t-son-shape_equipment), [shape vehicle type](#t-son-shape_vehicle_type), [steer theatre](#t-son-steer_theatre), [still weapon](#t-son-still_weapon), [stock depot](#t-son-stock_depot), [sway occupation](#t-son-sway_occupation), [wage war](#t-son-wage_war).

<a id="t-son-bless_host"></a>

#### Bless host

You strengthen an army you reach: its morale, its equipment or its experience rises by 10. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Army | The id of the army |
| Aspect | What you strengthen |
| Reason | Why you strengthen it |

<a id="t-son-close_the_way"></a>

#### Close the way

Close a way between worlds for some epochs, so that no host, people or trade crosses it.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [War and migration between worlds: campaigns, orbits, landings and supply, bombardment, blockade, and peoples moving between worlds](interconnections.md#m-interworld).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Route | The way |
| Epochs | For how many epochs, from 1 to 5 |
| Reason | Why you close it |

<a id="t-son-commission_vessel"></a>

#### Commission vessel

You have a community lay down a vessel on the frame you choose, with a captain if you name one; it is built to that community's own type for the frame, drawn now if it has none, and carries the weapons that type mounts. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Reaches** [Vehicle types and groups: frames, sections of differing criticality, weapon mounts and techniques; yards build groups that march, sail and fight with armies](interconnections.md#m-vehicles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The community or mortal that owns the vessel |
| Name | The vessel's proper name |
| Frame | The frame it is built on; one of: riding_mount (Riding mount), pack_mount (Pack mount), travois (Travois), sledge (Sledge), handcart (Handcart), cart (Cart), wagon (Wagon), covered_wagon (Covered wagon), carriage (Carriage), chariot (Chariot), war_wagon (War wagon), beast_carrier (Beast carrier), rail_car (Rail car), locomotive (Locomotive), steam_carriage (Steam carriage), motor_car <a id="t-son-design_unit"></a>

#### Design unit

You teach a community a new kind of soldier: a named type of its own, built on a battlefield role, with its loadout, doctrine and formation; its armies muster it in place of the old kind when their stores allow. Values you leave empty follow from the role and from what the community can make. 4 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 4 essence.
- **Reaches** [Kinds of soldier: each realm draws its own named types for its battlefield roles, refits them as better equipment is known and teaches them to allies](interconnections.md#m-unit_types) and [Weapon profiles of reach, volleys, accuracy and piercing; hard targets shrug off what cannot pierce; volleys run out](interconnections.md#m-arms_and_targets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The community that will field it |
| Name | What the soldiers are called |
| Base kind | The battlefield role it fills; one of: levy (Levy), line_infantry (Line infantry), heavy_infantry (Heavy infantry), shield_infantry (Shield infantry), pike_infantry (Pike infantry), light_infantry (Light infantry), shock_infantry (Shock infantry), guard_infantry (Guard infantry), marines (Marines), slingers (Slingers), archers (Archers), crossbow_infantry (Crossbow infantry), javelineers (Javelineers), gunners (Gunners), grenadiers (Grenadiers), light_cavalry (Light cavalry), heavy_cavalry (Heavy cavalry), mounted_archers (Mounted archers), mounted_gunners (Mounted gunners), beast_riders (Beast riders), sky_riders (Sky riders), chariots (Chariots), vehicle_crew (Vehicle crew), siege_crew (Siege crew), artillery_crew (Artillery crew), naval_crew (Naval crew), sky_crew (Sky crew), void_crew (Void crew), war_beasts (War beasts), bound_beasts (Bound beasts), sappers (Sappers), engineers (Engineers), battle_casters (Battle casters), support_casters (Support casters), summoners (Summoners), gifted (Gifted), dampeners (Dampeners), detectors (Detectors), constructs (Constructs), summoned_host (Summoned host), mechanised_infantry (Mechanised infantry), special_forces (Special forces), partisans (Partisans), peacekeepers (Peacekeepers), medics (Medics), drone_operators (Drone operators), signals_troops (Signals troops), powered_troopers (Powered troopers), drop_troops (Drop troops), pilots (Pilots) |
| Melee | Strength hand to hand, 0 to 100 |
| Missile | Strength at range, 0 to 100 |
| Defence | How well it holds, 0 to 100 |
| Armour | What it wears; one of: none, hide, padded, mail, scale, plate, enchanted, ballistic, composite, powered, force_field |
| Shield | What it carries to ward blows; one of: none, small, large, tower, riot, energy |
| Terror | Fear it strikes, 0 to 100 |
| Reach | How far its weapons reach, 0 (hand to hand) to 5 (siege range) |
| Volleys | Volleys it looses in one phase of battle, 0 to 6 |
| Accuracy | How true its blows and shots land, 0 to 100 |
| Piercing | How well its weapons pierce hard armour, 0 to 100 |
| Doctrine | How it fights; one of: shieldwall, horse_archer, skirmisher, chariot, phalanx, shock, naval_raider, siege_engineer, war_magic, beast_war, mechanised, air_mobile, guerrilla, drone_war, orbital, combined_arms |
| Formation | How it stands; one of: line, column, wedge, square, loose, shieldwall, dispersed, entrenched, echelon, convoy, screen, layered, circle |
| Description | What sets these soldiers apart, in your words |
| Equipment | Equipment models it carries, by id; models of the community or its people |

<a id="t-son-disband_host"></a>

#### Disband host

You send a standing army home: its soldiers return to their people and it leaves the field. 2 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 2 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Army | The army to disband |
| Reason | Why it goes home |

<a id="t-son-incite_war"></a>

#### Incite war

You stir enmity between two communities (faction, people or settlement). If it takes hold, war may break out between them; the world's course decides the war. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| A | The first community |
| B | The second community |
| Pretext | The pretext of the enmity |

<a id="t-son-lay_siege"></a>

#### Lay siege

You set an army of one side of a war against a settlement of the other: it encamps and cuts the settlement off. 6 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| War | The war |
| Army | The besieging army |
| Settlement | The settlement to besiege |

<a id="t-son-offer_peace"></a>

#### Offer peace

You end a war on terms you set: name the victor and what the beaten must give (land, tribute, hostages, the right to rule), or no victor for a white peace. 5 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 5 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| War | The war to end |
| Victor | The side that wins, or empty for a white peace |
| Terms | What the peace demands: cession, vassalage, hostage, reparations, tribute, liberty, throne, break_cult |

<a id="t-son-plant_spies"></a>

#### Plant spies

You plant a network of spies for one community inside another under a cover you choose; it gathers intel, steals secrets and stirs trouble. 6 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The community the spies serve |
| Target | The community they watch |
| Cover | merchant, priest, envoy or beggar |

<a id="t-son-raise_host"></a>

#### Raise host

You call a community to arms: its warriors and nobles muster as a standing army, in war or in peace; its people grow fewer while they serve. 8 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 8 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The community that raises the army |
| Soldiers | How many it calls to arms (at most 2000, or 10% of its people when that is more) |
| Commander | A living mortal to lead it, if any |
| War | The war it marches to, if any |

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Reaches** [Equipment models: each holder forges named weapons, armour and materiel of its own families and materials into stores its armies draw on](interconnections.md#m-equipment_models).
