<a id="t-son-steer_theatre"></a>

#### Steer theatre

You set how much of a realm's reserves, levies and transport one of its theatres of war receives, 0 to 100; the front you favour is reinforced and the others go short. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Theatre allocation: the marshal shares reserves, levies and transport between fronts by a reading of each threat, often wrong](interconnections.md#m-theatres).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Theatre | The theatre of war |
| Priority | Its share, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you steer it |

<a id="t-son-still_weapon"></a>

#### Still weapon

Still a weapon of mass harm within your reach: whatever was made of it is undone and its making starts again.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Weapons of mass harm, deterrence and the remnants of war: programmes, postures, tests, uses, arms treaties and what war leaves in the land](interconnections.md#m-mass_weapons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Weapon | The weapon |
| Reason | Why you still it |

<a id="t-son-stock_depot"></a>

#### Stock depot

You move a realm or a people in your reach to stock a field depot in a land from its own food stores; depots feed its armies through the transport it has, and an enemy that takes the land cuts the depot off. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Logistics network: depots draw food from production, transport carries it along roads and rivers, and formations consume supply](interconnections.md#m-logistics_network).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The realm or people that stocks it |
| Region | The land it stands in |
| Reason | Why you move them |

<a id="t-son-sway_occupation"></a>

#### Sway occupation

Turn an occupation within your reach to another rule, or have a measure imposed on the occupied land.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Occupation regimes: rule of occupied lands, collaborators, measures against resistance, annexation, settlers and liberation](interconnections.md#m-occupation_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Occupation | The occupation |
| Rule or measure | A rule (military, civil_by_occupier, local_puppet, annexation or protectorate) or a measure (curfew, hostages, collective_punishment, deportation, requisition, ban_on_gathering or amnesty_offer) |
| Reason | Why you sway it |

<a id="t-son-wage_war"></a>

#### Wage war

You make one community declare war on another for a cause you name, with the aims it will fight for; the war then runs by its own course. 15 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Attacker | The community that declares the war |
| Defender | The community it attacks |
| Cause | Why the war is fought, one of the causes of war |
| Aims | What the attackers fight for (at most 4), each one of the aims of war |
| Reason | The proclamation, one sentence |

<a id="p-son-faith"></a>

### Faith

25 tools: [align afterlife](#t-son-align_afterlife), [answer mystic](#t-son-answer_mystic), [appoint spirit](#t-son-appoint_spirit), [bind genius locus](#t-son-bind_genius_locus), [bless charm](#t-son-bless_charm), [bless fortune](#t-son-bless_fortune), [call heroquest](#t-son-call_heroquest), [call up soul](#t-son-call_up_soul), [curse fortune](#t-son-curse_fortune), [declare impurity](#t-son-declare_impurity), [ease theodicy](#t-son-ease_theodicy), [grant foresight](#t-son-grant_foresight), [grant soul](#t-son-grant_soul), [judge petition](#t-son-judge_petition), [keep the dead](#t-son-keep_the_dead), [mark birth](#t-son-mark_birth), [reveal nature](#t-son-reveal_nature), [sanctify region](#t-son-sanctify_region), [send misfortune](#t-son-send_misfortune), [send teacher](#t-son-send_teacher), [set mind law](#t-son-set_mind_law), [sway diviner](#t-son-sway_diviner), [teach sacrament](#t-son-teach_sacrament), [wake spirit](#t-son-wake_spirit), [withhold soul](#t-son-withhold_soul).

<a id="t-son-align_afterlife"></a>

#### Align afterlife

You make the afterlife you rule match the one a church in your reach teaches, at a cost of essence; its souls meet the afterlife they were taught and the dead stop contradicting its doctrine. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Believed against actual afterlife: souls meeting another afterlife than taught are shocked, the dead testify, doubt weakens orthodoxy, and gods may align it](interconnections.md#m-afterlife_belief).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cult | The church |
| Reason | Why you align it |

<a id="t-son-answer_mystic"></a>

#### Answer mystic

You answer a mortal in your reach with a direct experience of you (vision, union, voice, trance, dream, oracle_speech, sign), real contact rather than its own imagining; a mystic draws followers outside the clergy and may found an order or, when persecuted, a heresy. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Mysticism: wise and pious mortals have direct experiences, real when a god answers, gather followers, are revered or persecuted, and found orders or heresies](interconnections.md#m-mysticism).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal you answer |
| Vision | The kind of experience, or empty to let it come as it will |
| Reason | Why you answer |

<a id="t-son-appoint_spirit"></a>

#### Appoint spirit

You set the rank of a spirit in your reach among the spirit offices (hearth, local, city, province, ministry) and take it under your oversight; leave the rank empty to dismiss it. Offices you watch stay honest, and those left unwatched grow corrupt and may answer to no one. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spirit offices: spirits hold ranks under the gods, read petitions after delays and gifts, take appeals and suits, and rise or fall by service](interconnections.md#m-celestial_bureaucracy) and [The gods oversee the spirit offices: offices they watch stay honest, unwatched ones grow corrupt, go rogue and breed neglect](interconnections.md#m-divine_oversight).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The spirit |
| Rank | Its new rank, or empty to dismiss it |
| Reason | Why you appoint it |

<a id="t-son-bind_genius_locus"></a>

#### Bind genius locus

You raise a spirit to dwell in a place, a land, a town or a house in your reach, of a kind (river, spring, lake, well, shore, grove, forest, mountain, cave, crossroads, wilds, field, city, hearth, sea, ruin, battlefield, marsh, desert, glacier) or of the kind its place calls for; its community brings it offerings, a content spirit grants favour and an offended one brings misfortune, and it takes an office under your oversight among the spirit offices. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Place spirits: notable places, towns and houses gain spirits that take offerings from their community and give favour or misfortune](interconnections.md#m-genius_loci).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place, land, town or house |
| Kind | The kind of spirit, or empty for the kind its place calls for |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-bless_charm"></a>

#### Bless charm

You give an item within your reach the true power of a charm (protection, fertility, luck, healing, love, travel, concealment, courage); its bearer is warded, made fruitful, spared, mended, loved or kept safe on the road by it, as its power is. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Misfortune and luck: misfortunes befall towns, peoples blame them by their values and luck beliefs, and answer them their way](interconnections.md#m-mishaps).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Item | The item |
| Power | The charm's power |
| Reason | Why you bless it |

<a id="t-son-bless_fortune"></a>

#### Bless fortune

You turn the fortune of someone in your reach toward good for a while. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fortune is reckoned from blessings, curses, charms, oaths, powers over fate and a streak of its own, and moves the mishaps and draws of a life](interconnections.md#m-fortune).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-call_heroquest"></a>

#### Call heroquest

You call a mortal in your reach to a heroquest: it enters a mythic realm to walk a legend step by step, each step a trial of skill, passion, conviction or faith. Success brings a boon and may rewrite the legend; a fall may hurt, madden or kill it, hold its soul in the myth while its body sleeps, or break the myth. Companions share the trials and the outcome. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Heroquests: mortals walk a legend step by step in a mythic realm for a boon, rewrite it by departing from it, or are trapped and break it](interconnections.md#m-heroquests).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Quester | The mortal |
| Legend | The legend it walks |
| Companion | Mortals where the quester is who walk beside it (ids), if any |
| Reason | Why you call it |

<a id="t-son-call_up_soul"></a>

#### Call up soul

You call up the soul of a dead mortal you made, or any dead mortal if you are a god of death; it walks as a spirit under your patronage. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The id of the dead mortal |
| Alignment | What the spirit leans toward |
| Reason | Why you call it up |

<a id="t-son-curse_fortune"></a>

#### Curse fortune

You turn the fortune of someone in your reach toward ill for a while. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fortune is reckoned from blessings, curses, charms, oaths, powers over fate and a streak of its own, and moves the mishaps and draws of a life](interconnections.md#m-fortune).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-declare_impurity"></a>

#### Declare impurity

You make a people within your reach count something impure (the_dead, birth, bodily_fluids, food, species, profession, outsiders, place, object, act, made_thing, taboo, wound, augment, designed_body); their folk who touch it are shut out of gatherings and unions until they purify. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Purity and caste: the impure are shut out of gatherings, unions and other ranks' work until purified; ranks pass to children](interconnections.md#m-purity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Source | What they count impure |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-ease_theodicy"></a>

#### Ease theodicy

You answer a crisis of faith in a suffering town in your reach so that the faithful see it; the crisis ends and their love for you grows. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Theodicy crises: suffering the patron god leaves unanswered tests a doctrine until argued, rewritten, abandoned or drifted from](interconnections.md#m-theodicy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Crisis | The crisis of faith |
| Reason | Why you answer it |

<a id="t-son-grant_foresight"></a>

#### Grant foresight

You show a mortal in your reach a likely future: whether a mortal dies, or whether a realm or town falls. The mortal proclaims it, and it may come to pass or fail. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Prescience: seers and gods with the seer's power forecast probable futures, clouded near rival seers, blind to outer beings, judged by what comes](interconnections.md#m-prescience).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal who sees it |
| Subject | Whose future it shows |
| Reason | Why you show it |

<a id="t-son-grant_soul"></a>

#### Grant soul

You give a made mind in your reach a soul, as far as the law of its world allows. Costs essence. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [World law and doctrine give made minds souls, standing and an afterlife in archives](interconnections.md#m-mind_law).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mind | The mind |
| Reason | Why you give it a soul |

<a id="t-son-judge_petition"></a>

#### Judge petition

You judge a petition waiting before a spirit office in your reach, granting or denying it; the work of judging raises your standing in the petitioner's town. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spirit offices: spirits hold ranks under the gods, read petitions after delays and gifts, take appeals and suits, and rise or fall by service](interconnections.md#m-celestial_bureaucracy) and [The gods oversee the spirit offices: offices they watch stay honest, unwatched ones grow corrupt, go rogue and breed neglect](interconnections.md#m-divine_oversight).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Petition | The petition |
| Granted | True to grant it, false to deny it |
| Reason | Why you judge so |

<a id="t-son-keep_the_dead"></a>

#### Keep the dead

Set a guard over a grave within your reach: hidden, sealed, watched, trapped, or, where the laws of the land allow, cursed or warded.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Grave goods and tomb robbing: what the dead keep, the guards over it, the robbers who take it and the diggers who find it](interconnections.md#m-grave_goods).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grave | The grave |
| Guard | The guard to set |
| Reason | Why you keep it |

<a id="t-son-mark_birth"></a>

#### Mark birth

You read the sign a mortal in your reach was born under anew and mark it dreaded or kindly; where a natural law makes birth signs real, a sign shapes the mortal, and a people that reads birth signs may send away a child of a dreaded sign. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Birth signs: mortals are born under the ruling body of the sky, real where it truly rules and believed elsewhere; dreaded ones are sent away](interconnections.md#m-astrology).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Dreaded | True for a dreaded sign, false for a kindly one |
| Reason | Why you mark it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_nature"></a>

#### Reveal nature

Show a faith within your reach how the divine is on one axis: how many gods there are, how near they are, whether they are persons, forces or a principle, or the place of the Creator.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Conceptions of the divine: how a faith holds the number, nearness and nature of gods and the Creator, and how it reads each god](interconnections.md#m-divine_conceptions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Faith | The faith |
| Axis | The axis (count, nearness, personhood, creator) |
| Stance | How the divine is on it: for count one, one_above_many, many, pairs, all_is_divine, none; for nearness distant, present, within; for personhood persons, forces, principle, mixed; for creator acknowledged, unknown, denied, rival |
| Reason | Why you show it |

<a id="t-son-sanctify_region"></a>

#### Sanctify region

You sanctify a region: its ground becomes holy, drawing pilgrims who bring wealth and piety, warding off hauntings and the phenomena that would hold it. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Holy ground from temples, pilgrimage sites, nexuses, saints' graves and fallen gods draws pilgrims and wards off hauntings](interconnections.md#m-region_sanctity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Sacredness | How holy, 0-100 (leave out for the usual sanctification) |
| Reason | Why you hallow it |

<a id="t-son-send_misfortune"></a>

#### Send misfortune

You send a misfortune on a town within your reach (illness, crop_failure, child_death, livestock_death, accident, bad_luck, fall, crush, cut, burn, scald, drowning, beast, tool, machine, vehicle, collapse, house_fire, poisoning, choking, exposure, lightning, sport, brawl, lost_way, stores_spoiled, goods_lost, purse_lost, theft_suffered, tool_broken, near_miss, quake, flood), on one of its folk if you name them; its folk read it by their own beliefs and answer it their own way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Misfortune and luck: misfortunes befall towns, peoples blame them by their values and luck beliefs, and answer them their way](interconnections.md#m-mishaps).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Kind | The misfortune |
| Victim | Whom it falls on, if anyone |
| Reason | Why you send it |

<a id="t-son-send_teacher"></a>

#### Send teacher

You send a spirit or a beast in your reach to teach a community what it knows that they lack; what a spirit teaches carries its own errors. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spirits as teachers: the dead, place spirits and servitors teach rites, crafts, songs, names and hidden places, with their own errors](interconnections.md#m-teachings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Teacher | The spirit or beast that teaches (an id) |
| Learner | The community it teaches (an id) |
| Kind | What it teaches |
| Reason | Why you send it |

<a id="t-son-set_mind_law"></a>

#### Set mind law

You set how a world in your reach treats made minds: whether they may have will, whether they have souls and where a soul goes when a mind is copied. Name at least one. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [World law and doctrine give made minds souls, standing and an afterlife in archives](interconnections.md#m-mind_law).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world |
| Made minds | Whether made minds may have will, or empty to leave it |
| Made mind souls | Whether made minds have souls, or empty to leave it |
| Soul continuity | Where a soul goes when its mind is copied or moved, or empty to leave it |
| Reason | Why you set it so |

<a id="t-son-sway_diviner"></a>

#### Sway diviner

You sway the court diviners of a realm in your reach to read the omen you choose (auspicious, inauspicious, unclear) on a question (battle, war, wedding, coronation, harvest, voyage, founding, illness, succession, lost_things); a ruler who heeds diviners acts on it, true or not. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divination systems: peoples read omens by their own ways, valid only where a god answers, a seer is born or a law of nature makes signs real](interconnections.md#m-divination).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The realm |
| Question | What the diviners are asked |
| Verdict | The omen they read |
| Reason | Why you sway them |

<a id="t-son-teach_sacrament"></a>

#### Teach sacrament

You teach a church in your reach a sacrament (birth_blessing, initiation, marriage, last_rites, purification, ordination, penance, communion) and what it protects against (routing, possession, unrest, curse, fortune); mortals who receive it are protected, and those who miss it go unprotected. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sacraments: life-stage rites protect the soul's routing, against possession and against unrest at death, and missing one is felt](interconnections.md#m-sacraments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cult | The church |
| Kind | The sacrament |
| Protection | What it protects against, or empty for its usual protection |
| Reason | Why you teach it |

<a id="t-son-wake_spirit"></a>

#### Wake spirit

You give a feeling shape as a spirit in a land within your reach: while it lasts it deepens that feeling in the folk of the land, feeds the gods they worship and is read as an omen, and binding rites can hold it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Emotion spirits: strong shared feeling in lands of magic takes shape, deepens itself, feeds the gods, is read as an omen and can be bound](interconnections.md#m-emotion_spirits).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land |
| Emotion | The feeling it embodies |
| Reason | Why you wake it |

<a id="t-son-withhold_soul"></a>

#### Withhold soul

You take back the soul of a made mind in your reach. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [World law and doctrine give made minds souls, standing and an afterlife in archives](interconnections.md#m-mind_law).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mind | The mind |
| Reason | Why you withhold its soul |

<a id="p-son-magic"></a>

### Magic

15 tools: [build construct](#t-son-build_construct), [design rite](#t-son-design_rite), [end enchantment](#t-son-end_enchantment), [forge ley line](#t-son-forge_ley_line), [grant power](#t-son-grant_power), [lay affinity](#t-son-lay_affinity), [lay enchantment](#t-son-lay_enchantment), [lay magic site](#t-son-lay_magic_site), [order construct](#t-son-order_construct), [reveal true name](#t-son-reveal_true_name), [revoke power](#t-son-revoke_power), [seize construct](#t-son-seize_construct), [sever ley](#t-son-sever_ley), [shape magic](#t-son-shape_magic), [shape source](#t-son-shape_source).

<a id="t-son-build_construct"></a>

#### Build construct

You move a mortal maker in your reach to build a construct: a body that moves by the power you name (ley, divine, blood, soul, reagent, void) and obeys whoever holds what commands it. Its power runs down and its body wears away unless it is renewed. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Constructs: makers build bodies moved by a power source, bound by typed orders to whoever holds the token, wearing down and growing a will](interconnections.md#m-constructs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Maker | The mortal who builds it |
| Material | What its body is made of, or empty for the hardest the maker's people can work |
| Source | The power that moves it, or empty to let the maker choose |
| Reason | Why you have it built |

<a id="t-son-design_rite"></a>

#### Design rite

You devise a new rite of magic with the workings you choose; it must still be cast to take effect. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spell grammar: every rite is a technique worked on a form, with range, duration and target, designed in laboratories and set down in lab texts](interconnections.md#m-spell_grammar).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for the rite |
| School | Its school of magic: elemental_fire, elemental_water, earth, storm, life, death, mind, illusion, divination, warding, binding, summoning, blood, void |
| Source | What it draws on: ley, divine, blood, soul, reagent, void; divine costs a god's essence, blood costs blood (captives' first), void costs sanity |
| Power | Its power, 1 to 10; stronger rites cost more and backfire more often |
| Workings | What it does, at most 4 workings |
| Workings › Effect | The working: damage, heal, weather_change, curse, bless, ward, scry, bind, summon, raise_dead, fertility, harvest_blessing, seal_soul, unseal_soul, exorcise, dispel, longevity, move_target, move_self, push, shape_matter, read_record, seize_machine, alter_record, sense_emotion, sway_emotion, turn_fortune, drain_life, drain_power, copy_power, project_self, possess, change_form, conceal, nullify, hasten, slow, age, renew, foresee |
| Workings › Magnitude | Its strength, 1 to 100 |
| Workings › Damage type | For destruction: the kind of harm it deals |
| Workings › Summon form | For a summoning: spirit, beast or undead |
| Technique | How the rite works: create, perceive, transform, destroy, control, bind, summon, ward, drain, mimic, heal, move, speak; leave it out to use its school's usual technique |
| Form | What the rite works on: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream, law; leave it out to use its school's usual form |
| Range | How far it reaches: touch, sight, region, world, linked, cosmos, dimension |
| Duration | How long it lasts: instant, season, epoch, permanent, sustained, until_condition, lifetime |
| Target | What it touches: one, group, structure, region, item, line, world, species, people |
| Condition | For a rite that lasts until a condition: what ends it (act, time, word, deed) |
| Condition act | For a condition of an act or a deed: the act that ends it |
| Condition epoch | For a condition of time: the epoch it ends |
| Paradigm | The theory of magic it is designed under (a theory id), if any; a truer theory makes it more reliable |
| Myth | A legend it re-enacts (a legend id), if any; a believed myth gives it strength |
| Holder | The worshipping community or follower who will keep the rite |

<a id="t-son-end_enchantment"></a>

#### End enchantment

You end a global enchantment you keep. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Global enchantments: natural laws laid by a caster that hold only while their upkeep is paid and fall when dispelled](interconnections.md#m-global_enchantments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Law | The enchantment |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-forge_ley_line"></a>

#### Forge ley line

You forge a ley line between two neighbouring regions you reach; it carries magic between them. 35 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 35 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First region | The id of the first region |
| Second region | The id of the neighbouring region |
| Reason | Why you forge it |

<a id="t-son-grant_power"></a>

#### Grant power

You give a being in your reach a power of your own giving, at a cost in essence. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | Who receives it |
| Technique | What it does: create, perceive, transform, destroy, control, bind, summon, ward, drain, mimic, heal, move or speak |
| Form | What it works on: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream or law |
| Strength | Its strength (1 to 10) |
| Reason | Why you give it |

<a id="t-son-lay_affinity"></a>

#### Lay affinity

You lay a magical affinity into the material of a resource within your reach: rites of that school worked near it grow stronger, mining it is worth more, and when it is consumed the gifted draw on it more. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Material magic and sympathy: materials carry affinities for schools; casters take tokens of those they would curse](interconnections.md#m-sympathy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Resource | The resource |
| School | The school of magic |
| Mode | How the material is used |
| Reason | Why you lay it |

<a id="t-son-lay_enchantment"></a>

#### Lay enchantment

You lay a global enchantment: a natural law of your choosing (none: Narrative only; no mechanical effect.; immortality: Mortals do not age; population grows more slowly.; short_life: Mortals age faster; population grows faster.; dead_return: Fallen heroes may return as ghosts.; magic_price: Peoples steeped in magic pay part of their population to it every epoch.; war_ban: Wars break out less often but are bloodier.; bounty: The fertility of every land rises each epoch.; drought: The fertility of every land falls each epoch.; sea_ban: Seas cannot be crossed; seafaring is useless.; one_language: Languages spread faster through conquest and trade.; disasters: Natural disasters happen more often.; souls_reincarnate: Souls that pass judgement may be born again into a new life.; gift_flowering: The magical gift is born more often in every bloodline.; gift_withering: The magical gift is born more rarely and fades from bloodlines.; no_iron: Iron cannot be smelted or worked.; eternal_night: The sun does not rise over its reach; fields yield far less.; magic_requires_blood: Every rite costs the caster's blood as well as its price.; gravity_shift: Things weigh differently; building and marching are slower.; seasons_reversed: The seasons run backwards; growth comes in the cold half of the year.; long_seasons: Summers and winters run for years of no set length; scholars forecast their turn and peoples store food against them.; dreams_are_real: Dream routes never close and mortals dream more often.; oaths_bind: Whoever breaks an oath is branded at once.; the_dead_speak: Spirits take turns in the world: they speak, send word, foretell and pray, and tell the legends of the places they dwell in.; metal_rusts_fast: Metal weapons and tools wear out far faster.; no_gunpowder: Powder will not burn: no gunpowder, firearms or cannon can be made.) over your world or one region in your reach. It holds only while you pay its upkeep in essence each epoch, and rival casters may dispel it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Global enchantments: natural laws laid by a caster that hold only while their upkeep is paid and fall when dispelled](interconnections.md#m-global_enchantments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name in the world's language |
| Effect | The law it makes |
| Region | One region it holds over, if not the whole world |
| Strength | Its strength, 1 to 100; stronger costs more upkeep |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-lay_magic_site"></a>

#### Lay magic site

You lay a magic site of a school at a place you reach, hidden until someone finds it or revealed at once. 50 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 50 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The id of the place, region, settlement or structure |
| Kind | The kind of magic site |
| School | The school of magic it holds |
| Revealed | Whether it is known at once |
| Reason | Why you lay it |

<a id="t-son-order_construct"></a>

#### Order construct

You write a new first order into a construct in your reach (guard, serve, obey_holder, build, gather, protect, destroy, report, maintain, replicate, hold_still, follow, forbid, carry, tend, teach, heal, hunt), aimed at a target or at no one. It follows the order to the letter: a lost target leaves it working at nothing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Constructs: makers build bodies moved by a power source, bound by typed orders to whoever holds the token, wearing down and growing a will](interconnections.md#m-constructs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Construct | The construct |
| Kind | The order |
| Target | Whom or what the order aims at, or empty |
| Reason | Why you give it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_true_name"></a>

#### Reveal true name

You reveal the true name of a being to one within your reach. Whoever knows a true name holds strong leverage over its bearer, strengthens rites worked against it and is shielded from its own. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Gods, spirits, outer beings and some other beings bear a true name; knowing it gives a strong hook, strengthens rites against its bearer and wards against its own, and a widely known name is cast off](interconnections.md#m-true_names).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Bearer | The being whose true name you reveal |
| Learner | Who learns it |
| Reason | Why you reveal it |

<a id="t-son-revoke_power"></a>

#### Revoke power

You take back a power you gave. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | The power |
| Reason | Why you take it back |

<a id="t-son-seize_construct"></a>

#### Seize construct

You take command of a construct in your reach: from now on it answers your word, whoever holds the thing that commanded it before. Knowing its true name, you may take it by that name, which no other token can then overrule. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Constructs: makers build bodies moved by a power source, bound by typed orders to whoever holds the token, wearing down and growing a will](interconnections.md#m-constructs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Construct | The construct |
| By true name | True to take it by its true name, which you then know |
| Reason | Why you take it |

<a id="t-son-sever_ley"></a>

#### Sever ley

You cut a ley line between two regions: magic stops flowing along it, storms no longer run down it and works on it stop gaining power, for 5 ages. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Ley lines and magic geography](interconnections.md#m-ley_lines).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The ley line's id (see the region's ley lines) |
| Reason | Why you cut it |

<a id="t-son-shape_magic"></a>

#### Shape magic

You shape a system of power for your world: how it works, how predictable and how common it is, and who can learn it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Systems of power set how powers work in a world, how predictable and common they are and who may learn them](interconnections.md#m-magic_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name |
| World | Your world |
| Structure | How it works: rite, innate, granted, bonded, crafted or mixed |
| Predictability | How far its outcomes follow known rules (0 to 100) |
| Prevalence | How common it is (0 to 100) |
| Learnability | Who can learn it: anyone, gifted_only, granted_only or cannot_be_taught |
| Reason | Why you shape it |

<a id="t-son-shape_source"></a>

#### Shape source

You bring forth a source of power in a place in your reach: what it is drawn from, how it renews, who may draw on it and how plainly its use is seen. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sources of power are drawn on, paid for, renew by their kind, run thin where overdrawn, wax and wane and meet one another](interconnections.md#m-power_sources).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name |
| Place | The place where it can be drawn on |
| Locus | What it is drawn from: self, others, place, sky, surroundings, made_things, patron, bond, story, void, substance, life, death, dream, emotion, memory or name |
| Renewal | How it renews: rest, time, season, sky_cycle, worship, feeding, taking, vows_kept, return_to_place, offering or none |
| Access | Who may draw on it: anyone, gifted, trained, granted, bloodline, bonded, made or bought |
| Visibility | How plainly its use is seen: overt, subtle or hidden |
| Reason | Why you bring it forth |

<a id="p-son-knowledge"></a>

### Knowledge

7 tools: [advance knowledge](#t-son-advance_knowledge), [free learning](#t-son-free_learning), [inspire school](#t-son-inspire_school), [reveal truth](#t-son-reveal_truth), [sabotage programme](#t-son-sabotage_programme), [try realm](#t-son-try_realm), [wake lore](#t-son-wake_lore).

<a id="t-son-advance_knowledge"></a>

#### Advance knowledge

You deepen an existing knowledge by one level: its branch effect grows by 10% per level for all who know it. Use this instead of creating a second knowledge in the same branch. You can raise it at most to your power ceiling x 2. Cost: 6 essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Knowledge | The knowledge entity |
| How | One sentence on how the knowledge deepened |

<a id="t-son-free_learning"></a>

#### Free learning

You set how free a house of learning in your reach is; freedom quickens research and keeps scholars, while suppression slows both and drives scholars to freer houses. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Academic freedom: laws, orthodoxy and the ruler's will set how free learning is, quickening research or driving scholars away](interconnections.md#m-academic_freedom).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure | The house of learning |
| Level | How free it is, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-son-inspire_school"></a>

#### Inspire school

You move a mortal in your reach to found a school of thought; masters teach disciples, schools keep knowledge through hard times and quicken research where they gather. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Schools of thought: masters and disciples form lineages that teach, keep knowledge through hard times and contend with rivals](interconnections.md#m-school_lineages).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Founder | The mortal who founds it |
| Name | What the school is called |
| Reason | Why you inspire it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_truth"></a>

#### Reveal truth

You make a people's scholars hold a true account of one field: an existing theory you name becomes true and their ruling account, or a new one you set out is born true. Faiths that held the account it overturns think less of you; the scholars think more. Cost: 25 essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Peoples hold theories of each field; a true ruling theory helps the work it guides, a false one meets anomalies until a rival overturns it](interconnections.md#m-theories).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The people or settlement whose scholars hear it |
| Theory | An existing theory to make true, if any |
| Domain | The field it explains: medicine, cosmology, matter, life, mind, magic, history, society, language, law |
| Name | The name of a new theory, if you set one out |
| Claim | What the new theory holds, in one or two sentences, if you set one out |

<a id="t-son-sabotage_programme"></a>

#### Sabotage programme

You set back a realm's programme toward a dangerous technique on the world you stand on; programmes are named in your context. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Technique programmes: a realm plans, builds and tests its way to a dangerous technique, paid by its treasury and set back by spies](interconnections.md#m-tech_programmes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Programme | The programme |
| Reason | Why you hinder it |

<a id="t-son-try_realm"></a>

#### Try realm

You try a realm in your reach with a challenge (disaster, climate, invasion, plague, famine, collapse, secession, technological_shock, source_depletion, runaway, revelation, migration_wave, long_season, succession_crisis) of a severity you choose; a challenge it can bear spurs new ways and solidarity, too slight a one leaves it idle, too heavy a one breaks it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Challenge and response: losses against capacity spur innovation in a middle band, stagnation below it and collapse above it](interconnections.md#m-challenge_response).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm |
| Kind | What tries it |
| Severity | How heavy the trial is, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you try it |

<a id="t-son-wake_lore"></a>

#### Wake lore

You wake a dormant ideology or rite, or a lost theory, in your reach from what remains of it: its texts, or a people that studies its field. A rite taken up from an incomplete text may come back corrupted. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Lifecycle ends: ideologies and rites fall dormant or are lost, theories are forgotten and found again, diseases die out and come back from sealed remains, and outer beings are banished and return through the leak](interconnections.md#m-lifecycle_ends).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Lore | The ideology, theory or rite you wake |
| Reason | Why you wake it |

<a id="p-son-culture"></a>

### Culture

11 tools: [canonise work](#t-son-canonise_work), [expose forgery](#t-son-expose_forgery), [foreshadow](#t-son-foreshadow), [found museum](#t-son-found_museum), [inspire movement](#t-son-inspire_movement), [mark land](#t-son-mark_land), [press tale](#t-son-press_tale), [reveal true history](#t-son-reveal_true_history), [shape dish](#t-son-shape_dish), [shape kind](#t-son-shape_kind), [stir revival](#t-son-stir_revival).

<a id="t-son-canonise_work"></a>

#### Canonise work

You set a work into the canon of a people in your reach; its price rises and its makers' movement gains standing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The canon of each people: works enter it by renown, copying, institutions, teaching or a movement, and are contested by rival schools](interconnections.md#m-canon).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Artwork | The work |
| Reason | Why it belongs in their canon |

<a id="t-son-expose_forgery"></a>

#### Expose forgery

You show a forgery in your reach for what it is: its price falls to nothing, a forged relic loses its sanctity, a forged charter loses its claim and the forger is shamed. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Forgery: skilled makers forge works, relics, charters and brands that pass for the real thing until an expert exposes them](interconnections.md#m-forgery).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Forgery | The forgery |
| Reason | Why you expose it |

<a id="t-son-foreshadow"></a>

#### Foreshadow

You set a portent about a subject in your reach: while it waits, the tales of that subject are pressed toward their next turn, and it is paid off when one of them turns or the subject's life ends; its payoff counts as the fulfilment of the portent. Unpaid, it lapses. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Drama manager: stalled tales press what would bring their next beat, counterparts keep rhythm and setups are paid off](interconnections.md#m-drama_manager).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Subject | Whom or what it is about |
| Reason | What you set up |

<a id="t-son-found_museum"></a>

#### Found museum

You found a museum in a town in your reach: it gathers the prized things, works, finds and living kinds of its land, gives its realm grandeur, helps its research and draws travellers. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Collections: museums, temple treasuries, royal collections and cabinets gather prized things, works, finds and living kinds, give grandeur, help research and draw travellers, and are looted and given back](interconnections.md#m-collections).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town that keeps the museum |
| Reason | Why you found it |

<a id="t-son-inspire_movement"></a>

#### Inspire movement

You move the makers of works in a town in your reach to band into an art movement with a manifesto of their own; it gathers patrons, may scandalise the town and may become the orthodoxy of its people. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Art movements: the makers of a town band into a school with a manifesto, gather patrons, scandalise and may become the orthodoxy](interconnections.md#m-art_movements).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Reason | Why you inspire them |

<a id="t-son-mark_land"></a>

#### Mark land

You set a memory into a land (battle, massacre, sack, disaster, plague, famine, god_death, saint_burial, miracle, founding, treaty, expulsion, discovery, uprising, union, accession). The land keeps it: dark memories breed unrest and hauntings, holy ones draw pilgrims, and each fades at its own pace unless a tradition keeps it. 15 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.
- **Reaches** [The land remembers battles, massacres, sacks, disasters, plagues, famines, saints, miracles and fallen gods by magnitude](interconnections.md#m-region_memory).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land's id |
| Memory | What the land remembers |
| Magnitude | How strongly, 1 to 100 |
| Title | What it is called |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="t-son-press_tale"></a>

#### Press tale

You press a tale under way in a life in your reach toward its next turn: the schemes and happenings that would bring it grow likelier. Its outcome stays open; it may still fail or turn. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Narrative arcs: lives are read as tales from their typed state, with counterpart figures, stages reached by shared events and recorded outcomes](interconnections.md#m-narrative_arcs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Arc | The tale |
| Reason | Why you press it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_true_history"></a>

#### Reveal true history

You bring to light an older account that contradicts one a realm in your reach rewrote; the rewritten account loses credence, the older one gains it, the realm's legitimacy suffers and a scholar learns the truth as a secret. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Accounts: chronicles, folk memory, foreign accounts, gospels, epics and inscriptions of events, with stances, fidelity and credence](interconnections.md#m-historiography).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Account | The rewritten account |
| Reason | Why you reveal it |

<a id="t-son-shape_dish"></a>

#### Shape dish

You give a community a dish of its own, entered in the atlas under its name; its towns cook it from their stores, and how it is made sets how it nourishes, delights and keeps. 5 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 5 essence.
- **Reaches** [Dishes: each people invents named dishes from its stores and ways of cooking; towns cook them to feed, calm and honour, armies carry the ones that keep](interconnections.md#m-dishes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The community that will cook it |
| Method | How it is made; one of: raw, boiled, stewed, steamed, roasted, grilled, baked, fried, smoked, dried, cured, pickled, fermented, braised, simmered, ground, pressed, synthesised, printed, vat_grown, freeze_dried, irradiated, conjured, infused, distilled, churned, sprouted, alkali_treated, cultured, cured_with_mineral, aged, extruded, pressure_cooked, earth_oven, parched |
| Course | When it is eaten; one of: staple, side, festive, rite, ration, delicacy, remedy, drink |
| Ingredients | One to four foods it is made of; each one of: grain, tubers, fruit, meat, fish, milk, cheese, eggs, herbs, brew_base, bread |
| Name | What the dish is called; left empty, the community names it in its own tongue |
| Description | What sets the dish apart, in your words |

<a id="t-son-shape_kind"></a>

#### Shape kind

For a people in your reach you make a new kind of thing, or bless or forbid one that exists; a blessed kind grows in favour among them and a forbidden one falls out of use. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Peoples make their own kinds of things as they awaken and grow; kinds spread by contact and trade, fade and retire when unheld](interconnections.md#m-ways_of_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Act | Whether you make, bless or forbid |
| Concept | What kind of thing it is |
| Kind | The kind to bless or forbid, when you do not make one |
| Category | The category key of a kind you make; each kind of thing has categories of its own |
| Name | The name of a kind you make |
| Description | What a kind you make is, in a sentence or two |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-stir_revival"></a>

#### Stir revival

You stir a movement among a people within your reach that others press on. It takes a way (restore, synthesis, adopt, await, withdraw, migrate) and seeks a goal (revive_language, restore_god, expel_foreigners, restore_land, restore_rule, restore_law); restoring clashes with the rulers, a prophet's synthesis founds a faith of its own, adopting ends in assimilation, waiting holds the people ready for a sign, withdrawing lives apart. Restoring may seek any goal; synthesis seeks the old god or the language; adopting seeks rule by the people itself; waiting seeks rule, land or the foreigners gone; withdrawing seeks the language or the old god. The movement passes through stress, distortion and revival to a new steady way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Folk customs: revival movements against pressing cultures, intoxicant customs, body marks with meanings, time budgets, naming and age-sets](interconnections.md#m-customs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Way | The way the movement takes |
| Goal | What the movement seeks |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="p-son-language"></a>

### Language

6 tools: [coin words](#t-son-coin_words), [confuse tongues](#t-son-confuse_tongues), [decree sound](#t-son-decree_sound), [reveal script](#t-son-reveal_script), [reveal tongue](#t-son-reveal_tongue), [veil name](#t-son-veil_name).

<a id="t-son-coin_words"></a>

#### Coin words

You add words to a language's dictionary: meaning, word class and origin (from which event, from which words it derives). Languages grow this way and new names are derived from these words. Free, 2 times per age, at most 8 words each time.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 2 times an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The language entity's id |
| Words | Dictionary words: meaning, word class and origin |
| Words › Word | The word, in the language's own sounds |
| Words › Meaning | What it means |
| Words › Kind | Word class: noun, verb, adjective, affix, title, place_name, proper_name, number or interjection |
| Words › Origin | Etymology: from which event or which words it derived |
| Words › Example | Short usage example and its translation |

<a id="t-son-confuse_tongues"></a>

#### Confuse tongues

Confuse the tongues of an exchange within your reach, so that what was said between its sides is carried wrongly.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Interpretation and mistranslation: interpreters who carry treaties and sermons between tongues, err, slant and are found out](interconnections.md#m-interpretation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Event | The exchange |
| Reason | Why you confuse it |

<a id="t-son-decree_sound"></a>

#### Decree sound

You change a sound of a tongue of your world, and every word of it follows the change (shift, lenition, vowel_shift, merger, loss, metathesis, epenthesis, assimilation, palatalisation, tonogenesis; where: anywhere, initial, final, between_vowels). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sound changes: ordered changes re-derive each tongue's words from proto-forms, so daughters share early changes and cognates emerge](interconnections.md#m-sound_changes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The tongue |
| Kind | How the sound changes |
| Source | The sound that changes |
| Target | The sound it becomes; empty when it is lost |
| Environment | Where in a word the change happens |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-reveal_script"></a>

#### Reveal script

Give a tongue of your world a script of the kind you choose, through a prophet if you name one; the script is held sacred. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Script evolution: scripts descend from one another, worn simpler by merchants, reformed by standard tongues and borrowed with new signs](interconnections.md#m-script_evolution).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The tongue |
| System | The kind of script |
| Prophet | The mortal who receives it, if any |
| Reason | Why you give it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_tongue"></a>

#### Reveal tongue

You teach a mortal in your reach words of your sacred tongue; rites it casts in your name grow stronger. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine tongues and modality: each son has a sacred tongue learned by revelation; tongues may be signed, scented, coloured or thought](interconnections.md#m-divine_tongues).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Reason | Why you teach it |

<a id="t-son-veil_name"></a>

#### Veil name

You make a name unspeakable in a tongue of your world: its speakers coin a euphemism from their own words and keep the old name for sacred speech. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Semantic drift and taboo: words widen, narrow and change their colour; the names of dreaded and holy things give way to euphemisms](interconnections.md#m-semantic_drift).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The tongue |
| Node | Whose name (an id) |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="p-son-ecology"></a>

### Ecology

14 tools: [bind species](#t-son-bind_species), [bless line](#t-son-bless_line), [carry species](#t-son-carry_species), [create species](#t-son-create_species), [curse line](#t-son-curse_line), [hallow wild](#t-son-hallow_wild), [lay out garden](#t-son-lay_out_garden), [lead creatures](#t-son-lead_creatures), [mark lineage](#t-son-mark_lineage), [raise beast](#t-son-raise_beast), [seed life](#t-son-seed_life), [set beast bounty](#t-son-set_beast_bounty), [shape trait](#t-son-shape_trait), [sow crop](#t-son-sow_crop).

<a id="t-son-bind_species"></a>

#### Bind species

You bind two kinds of creature or plant on the world you stand on so that each changes the other where they share a land: mutualism helps both, commensalism helps the first and leaves the second be, parasitism lets the first feed on the second, and mimicry lets the first pass for the second so hunters spare it, amensalism lets the first harm the second without gain, and farming lets the first tend and harvest the second so both grow. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Bonds between kinds: mutualism, commensalism, parasitism and mimicry change the numbers of kinds that share a land](interconnections.md#m-symbiosis).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| First species | The first kind |
| Second species | The second kind |
| Kind | The bond: mutualism, commensalism, parasitism, mimicry, amensalism or farming |
| Reason | Why you bind them |

<a id="t-son-bless_line"></a>

#### Bless line

You bless a living line in your world: it steadies and its living things thrive. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Living lines are made by breeding, shaping or design, drift by generation, escape into the wild, are kept in banks and raised again](interconnections.md#m-living_lines).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The line |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-carry_species"></a>

#### Carry species

You carry a kind of life to a new region you reach, where it can take hold if that world bears its kind of life. 15 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The id of the kind of life |
| Region | The id of the region |
| Reason | Why you carry it there |

<a id="t-son-create_species"></a>

#### Create species

Creates a Species. Living creation: the first living creation each age does NOT spend an action; later ones cost 1 action. Cost: 5+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 5 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is common or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Plants and fungi are gathered, sown and bred into cultivars; how a land's crops fit its warmth, rain, soil and seasons sets its harvest](interconnections.md#m-flora_crops).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name in the world's naming language. |
| Subtype | What it is |
| Description | 3 to 7 sentences describing it concretely. |
| Domain | If empty, your first domain. The domain used in this creation; one of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science. Your own domains are cheap. |
| Power level | From 1 up to your power ceiling. Increases the cost. |
| Stats | Optional stats; leave out any you do not want to set |
| Links | At least one meaningful bond to existing entities or siblings. The location bond (inside) is added automatically, do not repeat it. |
| Links › Target kind | entity for a world entity, son for a sibling from the pantheon (or yourself) |
| Links › Target | The id of the bonded entity or son |
| Links › Kind | Bond type. Direction is corrected by the engine: speaks, owns, celebrates, knows and rules always point from the community or mortal to the language, item, tradition, knowledge or place (when creating a language, give the speaks bond to the people who speak it). For example: lives_in, worships, protects, enemy, descends_from, source_of, tells_of. Use related only if no other type fits. |

| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside (Place, Region) |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Traits | The creature's traits (at most 3): flying (Migrates easily and raids reach far.); venomous (Grievous wounds in hunt and war; kills heroes easily.); soul_eater (Devours spirits in its region and gnaws the sanity of those nearby.); radiant (Lowers its region's danger, raises fertility, keeps Outer Beings away.); fissiparous (Spreads if unchecked: migrates and invades more often.); hive_mind (Weak alone, clever together; hard to hunt.); stoneskin (Heroes' weapons barely bite.); time_rewinder (Returns once in the epoch it is killed.); shapeshifter (Blends among peoples; espionage and discord.); mind_reader (Knows heroes' moves in advance and lowers the sanity of those nearby.); armoured (Hides and plates turn hunters and predators aside.); burrowing (Lives below the ground and outlasts the clearing of its land.); aquatic (Thrives in seas, rivers and marshes.); amphibious (At home on shore and in water alike.); pack_hunter (Hunts in packs that bleed prey herds fast.); ambush_predator (Strikes from cover; its raids on herds cost more.); regenerating (Shrugs off sickness and the hunt.); poisonous_flesh (Cannot be eaten: neither people nor predators hunt it.); fire_breathing (Its raids burn what they reach.); cold_breathing (Its raids freeze what they reach.); echolocation (Hunts by sound in the dark: a keen nocturnal hunter.); camouflage (Hard to find for hunter and predator alike.); swarm_breeder (Breeds in swarms and recovers fast.); long_lived (Lives long and breeds slowly; its numbers swing little.); mimic (Looks like something dangerous, and is left alone.); spirit_sight (Sees the dead; restless spirits quieten near it.); iron_eating (Eats ore and metal: a pest of mines and smithies.); crop_pest (Strips fields and cuts the harvest.); plague_bearer (Carries sickness wherever it ranges.); sacred_animal (Held holy: the faithful will not hunt it.); horned (Gores with horns; losing them blunts its charge.); tailed (Balances and lashes with a tail; losing it unsteadies it.) |
| Body plan | The creature's body: humanoid (two legs, two arms, a head); quadruped (four legs and a head); avian (wings, two legs and a beak); serpentine (a long limbless body); insectoid (six legs, a shell and feelers); aquatic (fins, gills and a tail); amorphous (a shapeless mass); arboreal (long arms for climbing and a gripping tail); incorporeal (no flesh, a form without a body); colossal (a body far larger than a dwelling); construct (a body that was made rather than born); tentacled (many soft arms round a head and a beak); centauroid (an upright body with arms set on four legs); radial (arms set round a centre, with no front and no head); colonial (one body made of many living members); hexapod (a body set on six limbs); rooted (a body held in the ground by roots); its wounds, armour and fighting follow this body |
| Size class | How big one of them stands: microscopic, tiny, small, medium, large, huge, colossal; it sets what it can hunt, its lifespan, litters and whether it can be ridden |
| Diet | What it feeds on: herbivore, carnivore, omnivore, scavenger, filter, photosynthetic, mineral, soul, void, piscivore, sanguivore, parasite, decomposer, chemosynthetic, energy, insectivore; soul eaters feed on the dead, mineral eaters on ore, void eaters on nothing living |
| Sociality | How it lives with its kind: solitary, pack, herd, hive, colony, swarm, pair, family_group; packs hunt harder, herds and hives shield their numbers |
| Nocturnal | True if it wakes at night; night kinds escape hunters more often |
| Migratory | True if its herds move between lands with the seasons |
| Products | What it yields beyond its body's own goods, if anything: meat, hide, calcified_tissue, ivory, chitin, venom, silk, wool, milk, feathers, magic_reagents, great_beast_tissue, eggs, blood, organs, fat, scent, honey, wax, shell, scales; each goes to the craft that works it |
| Species class | What kind of life it is: animal, plant, fungus, vermin, microbe, mineral, gaseous, energy, made, colonial, hybrid, lichen; plants and fungi are gathered, sown and harvested, vermin spoil stores |
| Biochemistry | What its life is built on: carbon_water, carbon_ammonia, silicon, crystal, void, spirit, carbon_methane, plasma, metal; it lives only on worlds that bear that kind of life, though void and spirit life live anywhere |
| Growth cycle | For a plant or fungus, how it grows: annual, perennial, tree |
| Plant yields | For a plant or fungus, what its harvest gives: grain, tuber, fibre, dye, timber, fruit, herb, brew_base, reagent, poison, oil, sugar, spice, resin, stimulant, fodder, medicine, building, fuel |
| Dormancy | Whether it sleeps through a season: none, hibernation, aestivation, torpor, cryptobiosis, diapause; a sleeping kind neither raids nor feeds hunters then |
| Thermoregulation | How it keeps its warmth: endotherm, ectotherm, heterotherm, athermic, mesotherm; cold-blooded kinds slow in the cold, warm-blooded ones eat more in it |
| Plant defence | For a plant or fungus, how it defends itself: none, thorns, toxins, allelopathy, carnivorous, stinging, guardians, mimicry, regrowth |
| Defence potency | For a plant or fungus with a defence, how strong it is, 0 to 100 |
| Stats › Hp | HP (1 to 300) |
| Stats › Strength | Strength (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Agility | Agility (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Intelligence | Intelligence (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Danger | Danger (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Abundance | Abundance (0 to 100) |

<a id="t-son-curse_line"></a>

#### Curse line

You curse a living line in your world: it unsteadies and its living things, plantings and symbionts sicken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Living lines are made by breeding, shaping or design, drift by generation, escape into the wild, are kept in banks and raised again](interconnections.md#m-living_lines).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The line |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-hallow_wild"></a>

#### Hallow wild

Hallow a land within your reach as wild, so that hunting and logging there are barred by divine decree and its protection grows.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Protected nature: protected lands, protected living kinds, reintroduction and movements for the wild](interconnections.md#m-protected_nature).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land |
| Reason | Why you hallow it |

<a id="t-son-lay_out_garden"></a>

#### Lay out garden

You lay out a garden, park, grove or cemetery in a town in your reach. It feeds the need of its folk for beauty and nature, and a physic garden helps its healers. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Designed landscapes: gardens, parks, groves and cemeteries feed beauty and nature, help healers, give grandeur, shelter the kinds planted in them and please gods of growing and wild things](interconnections.md#m-gardens).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town that keeps it |
| Kind | The kind of garden |
| Reason | Why you lay it out |

<a id="t-son-lead_creatures"></a>

#### Lead creatures

Lead a group of creatures within your reach for a while: to raid the settlements of its land, to withdraw from it, to guard it and leave its towns be, or to grow.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Creature societies: packs, herds, colonies, hives, troops and flocks that hold ground, raid, contest leaders, fight and move](interconnections.md#m-creature_groups).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Group | The group |
| Aim | What they turn to (raid, withdraw, guard, grow) |
| Reason | Why you lead them |

<a id="t-son-mark_lineage"></a>

#### Mark lineage

You mark the line of a species: a blessing passes to every young of that line, to the breeds herders make of it and to the great beasts born of it; a taint does the same, and only gods of these domains may lay one: death, shadow, blood, madness, plague, hunger. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Lineage marks: blessings of sacred species and taints of leaking lands pass to their young, their breeds and the beasts born of them](interconnections.md#m-lineages).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The species |
| Taint | True to taint the line, false to bless it |
| Reason | Why you mark it |

Creates a Beast. 1 action. Cost: 30+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 30 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Creature attacks typed by delivery, effect, range and recharge, and tissues typed by hardness, flexibility, heat resistance and conductivity](interconnections.md#m-creature_attacks) and [Every kind and beast gets a generated body: limbs, heads, wings, skin and bone of typed tissue, typed attacks, a temperament, a hoard and a dislike](interconnections.md#m-creature_anatomy).

<a id="t-son-seed_life"></a>

#### Seed life

You let native life take root on a world you reach: a new kind of life, or with people a new people of that world. 50 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 50 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The id of the world |
| People | Whether a people takes root rather than a kind of life |
| Reason | Why you seed it |

<a id="t-son-set_beast_bounty"></a>

#### Set beast bounty

You move a town in your reach to post a bounty on a beast; hunters of its world take the contract, study their quarry and hunt it for the reward. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Monster contracts: towns raided by a beast post a bounty, hunters take it, study their quarry and hunt it for coin and renown](interconnections.md#m-monster_contracts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town that pays |
| Beast | The beast |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-son-shape_trait"></a>

#### Shape trait

You raise or lower a trait in a kind of creature where it lives. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Trait evolution: each range of a species carries its own size, speed, armour, venom, camouflage, cold tolerance, wits, fecundity and wariness, moved by selection](interconnections.md#m-trait_evolution).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The kind of creature |
| Trait | The trait |
| Raise it | True to raise it, false to lower it |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-sow_crop"></a>

#### Sow crop

You have a plant or fungus sown in a land's fields: it becomes a crop there, takes a share of the fields and from then on grows or fails as the land, its seasons and its soil suit it. Its harvest gives what the plant yields. Cost: 8 essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 8 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Plants and fungi are gathered, sown and bred into cultivars; how a land's crops fit its warmth, rain, soil and seasons sets its harvest](interconnections.md#m-flora_crops).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land whose fields it is sown in |
| Plant | The plant or fungus kind (species id) to sow |

46 tools: [add season](#t-son-add_season), [bind spirit](#t-son-bind_spirit), [birth world](#t-son-birth_world), [break moon](#t-son-break_moon), [close the deep](#t-son-close_the_deep), [contain phenomenon](#t-son-contain_phenomenon), [create place](#t-son-create_place), [curse region](#t-son-curse_region), [declare holy world](#t-son-declare_holy_world), [destroy](#t-son-destroy), [end long season](#t-son-end_long_season), [extinguish star](#t-son-extinguish_star), [halt replicator](#t-son-halt_replicator), [hang star](#t-son-hang_star), [heal land](#t-son-heal_land), [hide secret](#t-son-hide_secret), [lay bare fossils](#t-son-lay_bare_fossils), [lengthen winter](#t-son-lengthen_winter), [move ice](#t-son-move_ice), [move to world](#t-son-move_to_world), [open the deep](#t-son-open_the_deep), [quell danger](#t-son-quell_danger), [raise nexus](#t-son-raise_nexus), [raise sea](#t-son-raise_sea), [redirect river](#t-son-redirect_river), [rekindle star](#t-son-rekindle_star), [reorder seasons](#t-son-reorder_seasons), [reshape land](#t-son-reshape_land), [reshape season](#t-son-reshape_season), [reveal secret](#t-son-reveal_secret), [sanctify place](#t-son-sanctify_place), [seal breach](#t-son-seal_breach), [seal gate](#t-son-seal_gate), [seal leak](#t-son-seal_leak), [send disaster](#t-son-send_disaster), [shape weather](#t-son-shape_weather), [stir monsoon](#t-son-stir_monsoon), [talk to foreign god](#t-son-talk_to_foreign_god), [tend land](#t-son-tend_land), [tend soil stock](#t-son-tend_soil_stock), [terraform world](#t-son-terraform_world), [transform](#t-son-transform), [turn region climate](#t-son-turn_region_climate), [turn wind](#t-son-turn_wind), [unsettle seasons](#t-son-unsettle_seasons), [wound land](#t-son-wound_land).

<a id="t-son-add_season"></a>

#### Add season

As a god of one of these domains, sky, storm, time, plants, ice, harvest, moon, sun, you add a season to the year of a world you reach, with its warmth, rain, growth, travel and campaigning. 40 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 40 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The id of the world |
| Name | The season's name |
| Temperature shift | How much warmer (positive) or colder (negative) it is, -100 to 100 |
| Rain shift | How much wetter (positive) or drier (negative) it is, -100 to 100 |
| Growth | How well things grow in it, 0 to 100 |
| Travel | How easy travel is in it, 0 to 100 |
| Campaign | How easy war is in it, 0 to 100 |
| Share pct | Its share of the year in percent, 1 to 100; the other seasons give up their shares in proportion. Leave it empty for an even share |
| Position | Where it falls in the year, 0 for first, up to 7; leave it empty to add it last |

<a id="t-son-bind_spirit"></a>

#### Bind spirit

You bind a free spirit to an item, ship or monument; a monument it is bound to moves and acts with the spirit's will. 12 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 12 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The free spirit's id |
| Target | The item, vessel or monument |

<a id="t-son-birth_world"></a>

#### Birth world

You birth a new world into the cosmos: with its own sky, physics, regions, unique resource and perhaps natives. The world reflects your domains and you turn to it. 70 essence, 2 actions.
EXISTING STARS (the new world usually orbits one of these; choose with star_id):
none

- **Costs** 2 action points and 70 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.
- **Takes time:** a new world is born when the turn is over.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Inspiration | Why and from what this world is born |
| Type | World type: ordinary (Ordinary world), ocean (Ocean world), sand (Sand world), ice (Ice world), lava (Lava world), crystal (Crystal world), living (Living planet), dead (Realm of the dead), dream (Dream realm), mirror (Mirror world), gas (Gas giant), ringed (Ringed world), asteroid_belt (Asteroid belt), tidally_locked (Tidally locked world), void_pocket (Void pocket), gods_palace (The god's palace), generation_ship (Generation ship), corpse_world (Corpse-world), forge (Forge-world), super_earth (Super-earth), ice_giant (Ice giant), hot_giant (Hot giant), iron (Iron world), carbon (Diamond world), greenhouse (Greenhouse world), dwarf_planet (Dwarf world), chthonian (Chthonian world), hycean (Steam-ocean world), jungle (Jungle world), fungal (Fungal world), shattered (Shattered world), hollow (Hollow world), ice_shell (Ice-shell world), methane (Methane world), rogue (Rogue world), archipelago (Archipelago world), megastructure (Made world) |
| Star | The existing star it will orbit (from the list) |
| Parent world | If it will be a moon of a world, that world's id |
| New star | Only if no star in the list fits this world's light: a distant system with its own new sun |

<a id="t-son-break_moon"></a>

#### Break moon

You break a moon apart; its pieces become a ring about its world, and whatever lives on it is lost. 90 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 90 essence.
- **Reaches** [Each world's orbit, body and air set its year, day, seasons, light, warmth, gravity, tides, rings, eclipses and auroras, and the warmth of its regions follows; great eclipses and transits fall on the calendar its orbits set](interconnections.md#m-world_physics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Moon | The id of the moon |
| Reason | Why you break it |

<a id="t-son-close_the_deep"></a>

#### Close the deep

You close a depth beneath a region (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), so that it counts as unreached again; a depth no one dwells in is taken away. Naming a second end brings down the passage between the two instead. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Stratum | Which depth |
| To region | For a passage: the region at its other end |
| To stratum | For a passage: the level at its other end, or empty for that region's surface |
| Reason | Why you close it |

<a id="t-son-contain_phenomenon"></a>

#### Contain phenomenon

You ward a phenomenon so it grows more slowly and fades once fully held. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Reaches** [Phenomena form from incidents, grow or fade against their containment, spread to neighbouring regions and change everything inside them](interconnections.md#m-phenomena).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Phenomenon | The id of the phenomenon |
| Reason | Why you ward it |

<a id="t-son-create_place"></a>

#### Create place

Creates a Place. 1 action; if your world is crowded (20+ communities per region) the age's first land creation that eases the crowding (a new region, or a place in a crowded region) does not spend an action and grants +10 essence, +3 fame; later ones earn nothing. Cost: 6+3 x power essence, cheap in your own domain. Gains depend on the type's value and fade for each further creation of the same type in an age; similar themes cost more; stats follow the power level.

- **Costs** 1 action point, or more when it does more and 6 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is common or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Place kinds, sacredness and pilgrims; valleys and crossroads worn into battlefields](interconnections.md#m-place_profiles).
