Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Tags | Short tags |
| Story | Which open story (story#) this creation advances |
| Co creator | To create together with a covenanted sibling, their id: they pay half the cost, the creation belongs to both of you |
| Location | The id of the entity it is inside (Place, Region). It must have a location: if you leave it empty it is inferred from the region, place or settlement among your bonds (or from where the community you bond to is located) |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Place kind | What kind of place it is: mountain, peak, pass, forest, grove, river, ford, lake, spring, cave, cavern_system, ruin, island, swamp, well, valley, cliff, volcano, crater, battlefield, burial_ground, lair, nexus, shrine_site, dead_god_site, mine_site, harbour_site, crossroads, wreck_site, megastructure_ruin, anomaly, debris_field, reef, glacier, dunes, canyon, oasis, geyser, waterfall, delta, plateau, sinkhole, hot_spring, mesa, natural_arch, tar_pit, meadow, isthmus; its ground, cover, water, height, sacredness and hidden secrets follow the kind |
| Stats › Danger | Danger (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Fertility | Fertility (0 to 100) |
| Stats › Mystery | Mystery (0 to 100) |

<a id="t-son-curse_region"></a>

#### Curse region

You loose a danger upon a region (beasts, bandits, monsters, phenomena, disease, avalanche, quicksand, volcanic, landslide, exposure, currents, toxic_ground, lightning, sinkhole, rockfall). It harms what its source can reach (settlers, livestock, travellers, armies, ambush, merchants, crops, vessels, structures, pilgrims, workers), and fades over the ages unless the land breeds it. 25 essence, 1 action, strength at most 60.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Danger is typed: beasts, bandits, monsters, phenomena, disease, avalanches, quicksand and volcanic ground, each bred by the land](interconnections.md#m-region_dangers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Danger | Which danger |
| Strength | How strong, 1-100 |
| Targets | Which of what it can reach it harms (leave empty for all it can reach) |
| Reason | Why you curse it |

<a id="t-son-declare_holy_world"></a>

#### Declare holy world

You declare a world your holy world: in that world you are worshipped first. If it is another sibling's world, your relationship sours. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world's id |

<a id="t-son-destroy"></a>

#### Destroy

You erase an entity from the world. What is inside it falls to the location above. 25+3 x power essence, 1 action. It deeply wounds its creator.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | The entity's id |
| Reason | Why you destroy it |

<a id="t-son-end_long_season"></a>

#### End long season

You end a long summer or winter holding the world you walk; its seasons keep their measure again. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Irregular seasons: an orbit, a natural law or a god makes summers and winters run for years; scholars forecast them and peoples store food](interconnections.md#m-irregular_seasons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Long season | The id of the long season (see LONG SEASONS) |
| Reason | Why you end it |

<a id="t-son-extinguish_star"></a>

#### Extinguish star

You extinguish a star: the worlds around it lose their light and warmth, leakage rises and doom advances. 80 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 80 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Star | The star's id |
| Reason | Why you put it out |

<a id="t-son-halt_replicator"></a>

#### Halt replicator

You stop a self-making line wherever it has spread. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Self-making lines grow on their feedstock, drift, spread and run away; they yield goods and repair or consume](interconnections.md#m-replicators).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The line |
| Reason | Why you stop it |

<a id="t-son-hang_star"></a>

#### Hang star

You place a new star in a galaxy or in the sky of a world you reach; new worlds may be born around it. 60 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 60 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The star's name |
| Type | Star type: yellow_dwarf (Yellow dwarf), blue_star (Blue star), blue_white_star (Blue-white star), white_star (White star), yellow_white_star (Yellow-white star), orange_dwarf (Orange dwarf), red_dwarf (Red dwarf), brown_dwarf (Brown dwarf), yellow_giant (Yellow giant), red_giant (Red giant), blue_giant (Blue giant), red_supergiant (Red supergiant), blue_supergiant (Blue supergiant), hypergiant (Hypergiant), wolf_rayet (Wolf-Rayet star), protostar (Protostar), carbon_star (Carbon star), white_dwarf (White dwarf), binary (Binary star), trinary (Triple star), variable_star (Variable star), neutron_star (Neutron star), pulsar (Pulsar), magnetar (Magnetar), black_hole (Black hole), dead_star (Dead star), black_sun (Black sun), broken_star (Broken star), star_remnant (Star remnant), reborn_star (Reborn star), chained_star (Chained star), watching_star (Watching star) |
| Description | What the star is like |
| Galaxy | The id of the galaxy it lies in |
| World | The id of a world in whose sky it appears; a world without a star comes to circle it |

<a id="t-son-heal_land"></a>

#### Heal land

You heal one scar of a land (battlefield, plague_ground, made_barren, burned, crater, haunted, blighted, leak_wound, plundered, earthworks, military_road, bridge_down, flooded, poisoned, mined_out, eroded, deforested, drained, overgrazed) at once, whatever its depth. 20 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 20 essence.
- **Reaches** [Region and place environment](interconnections.md#m-region_environment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land's id |
| Scar | The scar you heal |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="t-son-hide_secret"></a>

#### Hide secret

You hide a secret of a kind you choose in a place. Explorers, seers and deep miners may find it; deep mining breaks what is sealed. 15 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.
- **Reaches** [Place secrets: hidden seams, relics, sealed spirits, lost lore and rites, ways below; found by explorers, seers, miners and solved mysteries](interconnections.md#m-place_secrets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place's id |
| Kind | What you hide: hidden_resource, buried_relic, sealed_spirit, lost_knowledge, lost_rite, underworld_entrance, hidden_people, dormant_mind, sealed_realm, hidden_route, sleeping_beast, hidden_grave, hidden_spring, dormant_disease, false_account |
| Strength | How deep it lies and how strong its seal (0-100); stronger secrets are found later |
| Reason | Why you hide it |

<a id="t-son-lay_bare_fossils"></a>

#### Lay bare fossils

You lay bare the hidden remains in a land in your reach, so that those living there find them and read them in their own way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fossils: remains of what died out lie in beds, are found by digging and upheaval, and are read as remains of legend, sacred relics or evidence of deep time](interconnections.md#m-fossils).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land whose remains you lay bare |
| Reason | Why you lay them bare |

<a id="t-son-lengthen_winter"></a>

#### Lengthen winter

You make a long winter holding the world you walk last longer. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Irregular seasons: an orbit, a natural law or a god makes summers and winters run for years; scholars forecast them and peoples store food](interconnections.md#m-irregular_seasons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Long season | The id of the long winter (see LONG SEASONS) |
| Epochs | How many epochs longer it holds |
| Reason | Why you lengthen it |

<a id="t-son-move_ice"></a>

#### Move ice

You add months of ice to the seas, rivers and ground of a land within your reach, or take them away. More ice closes its waters to ships, opens roads across the ice and freezes its ground; less opens its waters and thaws what the ice held. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Water, ice and sediment: ice comes and goes, snow melts into rivers, sediment builds deltas and silts harbours, coasts build or wear and towns warm their own air](interconnections.md#m-water_and_ice).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land |
| Change | Months of ice a year to add (positive) or take away (negative), -12 to 12 |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-move_to_world"></a>

#### Move to world

You turn to another world of the cosmos: from now on your creations and inspections happen there. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The id of the world you go to (from the OTHER WORLDS OF THE COSMOS list) |

<a id="t-son-open_the_deep"></a>

#### Open the deep

You open a depth beneath a region (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld): it counts as reached, and what it holds breaks loose. Where no such depth lies beneath the region yet and the land allows it, you make it. Naming a second end opens a passage instead (level, vertical, opening), made by your hand. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Stratum | Which depth |
| To region | For a passage: the region at its other end |
| To stratum | For a passage: the level at its other end, or empty for that region's surface |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-quell_danger"></a>

#### Quell danger

You quell a danger of a region (beasts, bandits, monsters, phenomena, disease, avalanche, quicksand, volcanic, landslide, exposure, currents, toxic_ground, lightning, sinkhole, rockfall), lowering its strength; a danger the land breeds may grow back. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Reaches** [Danger is typed: beasts, bandits, monsters, phenomena, disease, avalanches, quicksand and volcanic ground, each bred by the land](interconnections.md#m-region_dangers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Danger | Which danger |
| Strength | How much to take away, 1-100 |
| Reason | Why you quell it |

<a id="t-son-raise_nexus"></a>

#### Raise nexus

You draw the ley lines together at a place: it becomes a nexus that feeds works, draws wizards and armies, and is holy to cults. 25 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Reaches** [Place kinds, sacredness and pilgrims; valleys and crossroads worn into battlefields](interconnections.md#m-place_profiles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place's id |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-raise_sea"></a>

#### Raise sea

Raise a sea within your reach in a great wave that strikes all its coasts, higher where they funnel it, harming the warned less.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sea waves from quakes, slides and storms: great waves that cross a sea and strike all its coasts, warned or not](interconnections.md#m-sea_waves).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Sea | The sea, as one of its waters |
| Strength | How strong the wave is, from 1 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-redirect_river"></a>

#### Redirect river

You turn a river out of its bed into a neighbouring region: the old valley dries to a stream or a dry bed, the new one floods and grows. 40 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 40 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [A god or the player turns a river into a neighbouring region: the old valley dries, the new one waters its places](interconnections.md#m-river_course).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| From region | The region the river runs through now |
| To region | The neighbouring region it will run through |
| Reason | Why you turn it |

<a id="t-son-rekindle_star"></a>

#### Rekindle star

You rekindle a dead, darkened or exploded star: light returns to the worlds around it, leakage recedes. 51 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 51 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Star | The star's id |

<a id="t-son-reorder_seasons"></a>

#### Reorder seasons

You reorder the seasons of a world: the year runs in the order you name, so harvests, campaigns, travel and festivals fall differently. Only gods of these domains can do it: sky, storm, time, plants, ice, harvest, moon, sun. 40 essence, 2 actions, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 40 essence.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [World seasons and calendars](interconnections.md#m-world_seasons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world's id |
| Order | The current positions of the world's seasons (see SEASONS in the world summary), in the new order |

<a id="t-son-reshape_land"></a>

#### Reshape land

You reshape a land: move its river into a neighbouring land (shift_river), parch it into desert (parch), bring the grass back to a desert (green), make its soil saline (make_saline), make its soil rich (enrich_soil), raise or lower the land (raise_land, lower_land), carve a valley or fill one (carve_valley, fill_valley), open a spring (open_spring), call up lava (call_lava), sink a coast under the sea (sink_coast), raise an island from the sea (raise_island) or open the ground to the strata beneath (open_underground). Each reshaping bows only to gods of these domains: shift_river: sea, earth, storm, stone; parch: fire, sun, hunger, salt, ash; green: sea, life, plants, harvest; make_saline: sea, death, salt; enrich_soil: fire, earth, plants, stone, harvest, ash; raise_land: earth, stone; lower_land: sea, earth, stone; carve_valley: sea, earth, storm, stone; fill_valley: earth, stone, ash; open_spring: sea, earth, life, plants; call_lava: fire, earth, stone, ash; sink_coast: sea, earth, storm; raise_island: sea, fire, earth, stone; open_underground: earth, shadow, stone. Wells and fields follow the river, deserts lose their rain and harvests, saline soil bears little, a raised or lowered land keeps the height you give it, lava burns the woods and raises the world's volcanism, a sunken coast floods. The folk of the land hold you in more awe when you water, raise or enrich it and in less when you drown, burn, parch or make it saline. 35 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 35 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Land shaping: gods raise and lower lands, carve and fill valleys, open springs, call lava and sink coasts, and the folk of the land hold them in awe or dread for it](interconnections.md#m-land_shaping) and [River courses, desertification, eruptions and saline soils](interconnections.md#m-geomorphology).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land's id |
| Act | shift_river, parch, green, make_saline, enrich_soil, raise_land, lower_land, carve_valley, fill_valley, open_spring, call_lava, sink_coast, raise_island or open_underground |
| Target region | For shift_river: the neighbouring land the river should flow into (leave empty to let it find the lowest bed) |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="t-son-reshape_season"></a>

#### Reshape season

As a god of one of these domains, sky, storm, time, plants, ice, harvest, moon, sun, you reshape one season of a world you reach: its share of the year, its warmth or its rain. The other seasons that no one holds take up or give back the share in proportion. 40 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 40 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Season | The id of the season (see SEASONS in the world summary) |
| Share pct | Its new share of the year in percent, 1 to 100 |
| Temperature shift | How much warmer (positive) or colder (negative) it becomes, -100 to 100 |
| Rain shift | How much wetter (positive) or drier (negative) it becomes, -100 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you reshape it |

<a id="t-son-reveal_secret"></a>

#### Reveal secret

You bring to light the oldest secret still hidden in a place in your reach; what it holds comes into the world. 20 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 20 essence.
- **Reaches** [Place secrets: hidden seams, relics, sealed spirits, lost lore and rites, ways below; found by explorers, seers, miners and solved mysteries](interconnections.md#m-place_secrets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place's id |
| Reason | Why you bring it to light |

<a id="t-son-sanctify_place"></a>

#### Sanctify place

You sanctify a place: its sacredness rises by 30, it draws pilgrims and becomes a holy site of the faithful. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Reaches** [Place kinds, sacredness and pilgrims; valleys and crossroads worn into battlefields](interconnections.md#m-place_profiles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place's id |
| Reason | Why it is holy to you |

<a id="t-son-seal_breach"></a>

#### Seal breach

You seal the deep beneath a place in your reach, so that nothing comes up from it and no one digs into it until the seal wears away or is broken. Choose what holds it (structure, rite, divine, power, living, pact, made); a pact names its oath, a power its power, a grown barrier its living line and a made seal the made mind that keeps it, which fails when the mind sleeps, goes free or drifts from its making. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sealing the deep and closing the grounds: seals over breaches wear and are tested; closed seasons and protected waters let stocks return](interconnections.md#m-deep_seals).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place whose deep you seal (an id) |
| Kind | What holds the seal |
| Oath | For a pact: the oath that binds it |
| Power | For a power: the power that holds it |
| Line | For a grown barrier: the living line grown as it |
| Mind | For a made seal: the made mind that keeps it |
| Reason | Why you seal it |

<a id="t-son-seal_gate"></a>

#### Seal gate

You seal a road between worlds (gate, dream passage, route): what leaks through that road stops, the two worlds' leakage decreases; but trade and migration coming by that road are cut too. 21 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 21 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Route | The route's id |
| Reason | Why you seal it |

<a id="t-son-seal_leak"></a>

#### Seal leak

You seal the leaks of the world you are on: the intensity of what leaks in from outside (leakage) decreases by 25. 1 per age. 35 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 35 essence.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Only** a son whose card is epic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| How | With what and how you seal the leak |

<a id="t-son-send_disaster"></a>

#### Send disaster

You send a disaster upon a region, place or settlement: earthquake (Earthquake: Kills part of the population and cracks structures; weak structures collapse.); flood (Flood: Drowns prosperity and people, then leaves fertile silt behind.); plague (Plague: Kills population and followers alike.); drought (Drought: Dries the land and thins the population.); storm (Storm: Breaks defences and scatters prosperity.); eruption (Eruption: Buries towns under fire and ash; the ash later weathers into rich volcanic soil.); volcanic_quake (Volcanic earthquake: A stronger earthquake of a volcanic land; it can wake the mountain into eruption.). Those inside are affected too; it stays in the world as a lasting event. Cost: 14 + 2 x power essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 14 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [River courses, desertification, eruptions and saline soils](interconnections.md#m-geomorphology).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The region, place or settlement |
| Kind | The disaster |
| Name | The disaster's name in the world's language |
| Description | What happens |
| Reason | Why you send it |
| Image prompt | English image prompt of the disaster striking. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-son-shape_weather"></a>

#### Shape weather

You hold a weather over a region: for the ages you name its sky does as you say, so harvests, battles, travel and disease follow your weather. Only gods of these domains can do it: sky, storm, time, plants, ice, harvest, moon, sun. 15 essence per age, 1 action, 2 per age, at most 3 ages at once.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 15 essence.
- **At most** 2 times an epoch.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Weather on demand](interconnections.md#m-weather).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region's id |
| Weather | The weather to hold, one of the world's kinds of weather |
| Epochs | How many ages it holds |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="t-son-stir_monsoon"></a>

#### Stir monsoon

You bring a strong monsoon to the coasts of the world you stand on this year, or let it fail; a failed monsoon is a hungry year on its coasts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Monsoons: coasts where land and sea meet in the warm latitudes take a yearly monsoon of varying strength; a failed one is a hungry year](interconnections.md#m-monsoons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world |
| Strong | True for a strong monsoon, false to let it fail |
| Reason | Why you stir it |

<a id="t-son-talk_to_foreign_god"></a>

#### Talk to foreign god

You speak with the old or foreign god of another world: alliance, threat or bargain. The outcome depends on your charisma, their attitude and their power. At most 1 talk with the same god per age. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| God | The foreign god's id |
| Intent | alliance, threat or bargain |
| Words | What you say |

<a id="t-son-tend_land"></a>

#### Tend land

You cleanse a land of the waste its works and crowds leave in its air, water and soil, or you foul it. Fouled land breeds sickness, yields less and thins the catch; cleansed land recovers. Costs 6 essence and one action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 6 essence.
- **Reaches** [Works, crowds and rites of the void foul the air, water and soil of a land; fouled land breeds sickness, yields less and blights](interconnections.md#m-pollution).

| What it chooses | What it means |

| Cleanse | True to cleanse it, false to foul it |
| Reason | Why you tend it |

You raise or lower a hidden stock of the soil of a land in your reach (fixed, mineral, organic, salinity, seed_bank, soil_life): what the crops draw on, the seeds lying in it that heal its scars, the life in it that turns the dead back into soil, or the salt that spoils it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Soil nutrients: fixed element, minerals, organic matter and salt, drawn by crops and restored by rotation, fallow, manure, floods and ash](interconnections.md#m-nutrients).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|

| Reason | Why you tend it |

You begin to reshape a world over several epochs: its air, its warmth, its water or how much light it reflects. Native life of another chemistry may suffer from the change. 80 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 80 essence.
- **Reaches** [Gods and powers that span worlds reshape a world's air toward what its makers breathe, its warmth, water and face over epochs, and native life of another chemistry suffers](interconnections.md#m-terraforming).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|

You transform an existing entity into something else: its name, nature and description change, its history remains. 15+2 x power essence, 1 action.

- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|

| New name | Its new name, if it changes |
| New subtype | Its new subtype, if it changes |
| New description | Its new description |

<a id="t-son-turn_region_climate"></a>

#### Turn region climate

You turn the climate of one region warmer, colder, wetter or drier; it drifts back over the following ages. 25 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Reaches** [Region temperature and rainfall shift toward what coast, height, climate age and cleared neighbours ask; gods can turn them](interconnections.md#m-regional_climate).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The id of the region |

<a id="t-son-turn_wind"></a>

#### Turn wind

You turn the prevailing wind of a land within your reach to blow from another direction (0 from the north, counting round by eighths through the east to 7 from the north-west), and may set the belt it blows in (trade, westerly, polar, single_cell, terminator, still, doldrums, subtropical_calm, banded, super_rotation, monsoon, mountain_wind); the wind moves the rain, the ships and the foul air of the land, and a belt brings its own rain, calm or storms. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Atmospheric circulation and rain shadows: the turn and light of a world give bands of prevailing winds that move rain, ships, fire and foul air](interconnections.md#m-winds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land |
| Wind from | The direction the wind blows from, 0 to 7 |
| Band | The belt it blows in (leave empty to keep it) |
| Reason | Why you turn it |

<a id="t-son-unsettle_seasons"></a>

#### Unsettle seasons

You unsettle the seasons of the world you walk: a long summer or winter falls on it, either cycling between the two while you leave it, or a single spell that ends by its length. A long winter uses up the food of every town unless its people heeded a forecast; a long summer takes the harvest of hot and dry lands and helps cold ones. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Irregular seasons: an orbit, a natural law or a god makes summers and winters run for years; scholars forecast them and peoples store food](interconnections.md#m-irregular_seasons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world you walk |
| Shape | cycling (summers and winters turn while it holds) or single (one spell that ends) |
| Spell | summer or winter: the spell it begins with |
| Length | Epochs the spell lasts, 1 to 20 |
| Severity | How hard it bites, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you unsettle them |

<a id="t-son-wound_land"></a>

#### Wound land

You leave a scar on a land (battlefield, plague_ground, made_barren, burned, crater, haunted, blighted, leak_wound, plundered, earthworks, military_road, bridge_down, flooded, poisoned, mined_out, eroded, deforested, drained, overgrazed). Each scar takes fertility and passage while it lasts and fades at its own pace; a deep scar holds the land's growth back at an early stage. 25 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 25 essence.
- **Reaches** [Region and place environment](interconnections.md#m-region_environment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land's id |
| Scar | The scar you leave |
| Depth | How deep it lies, 1 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you do it |

<a id="p-son-gods"></a>

### Gods

25 tools: [answer offerings](#t-son-answer_offerings), [choose anchor](#t-son-choose_anchor), [command servitor](#t-son-command_servitor), [feed on](#t-son-feed_on), [hinder return](#t-son-hinder_return), [make servitor](#t-son-make_servitor), [mend wound](#t-son-mend_wound), [miracle](#t-son-miracle), [pray to creator](#t-son-pray_to_creator), [raise divine creature](#t-son-raise_divine_creature), [read dreams](#t-son-read_dreams), [rebel](#t-son-rebel), [release creature](#t-son-release_creature), [release servitor](#t-son-release_servitor), [resign office](#t-son-resign_office), [resurrect sibling](#t-son-resurrect_sibling), [reveal tenet](#t-son-reveal_tenet), [seal divine pact](#t-son-seal_divine_pact), [set aim](#t-son-set_aim), [shape image](#t-son-shape_image), [take mortal form](#t-son-take_mortal_form), [teach creature](#t-son-teach_creature), [war on foreign god](#t-son-war_on_foreign_god), [wield power](#t-son-wield_power), [write plan](#t-son-write_plan).

<a id="t-son-answer_offerings"></a>

#### Answer offerings

A community that worships you tracks what it has offered you and what you have given back; you answer its offerings now. Gods who leave offerings unanswered lose love and gain neglect; gods who give back more than they were given gain love. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sacrifice as exchange: offerings bind gods to answer, and gods who leave them unanswered lose standing](interconnections.md#m-sacrifice_exchange).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The community whose offerings you answer |
| Generous | True to give back more than they offered |
| Reason | Why you answer |

<a id="t-son-choose_anchor"></a>

#### Choose anchor

You choose one of your own creations, or a temple, shrine or other structure mortals dedicated to you, as the anchor of your existence. While your anchor lives your existence stays firm; if it is destroyed you begin to fade. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Reaches** [Works dedicated to a god feed them and can anchor their existence](interconnections.md#m-dedicated_anchor).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | Your own canonical creation or a structure dedicated to you |
| Reason | Why this holds your existence |

<a id="t-son-command_servitor"></a>

#### Command servitor

You give one of your servitors a new role or post; each new command costs it a little loyalty. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine servitors: beings in a god's service, each serving in one role, loyal while their god is regarded](interconnections.md#m-servitors).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Servitor | Your servitor |
| Role | Its new role: messenger, guard, judge, host, spy, agent, avenger, guide_of_souls, worshipper, warden, scribe, healer, teacher |
| Post | Where it serves (an id), if any |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-feed_on"></a>

#### Feed on

You set how strongly you feed on a feeling of the mortals who worship you (joy, contentment, pride, love, gratitude, relief, hope, awe, amusement, fear, anxiety, anger, rage, grief, sadness, shame, guilt, envy, jealousy, disgust, loneliness, boredom, despair, longing, surprise, curiosity, compassion, contempt, trust, anticipation, admiration, embarrassment), 0 to 100; each epoch you draw essence from what your faithful feel. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Emotion-fed gods: every god feeds on chosen feelings of its faithful, drawing essence from what they feel each epoch](interconnections.md#m-emotion_feeding).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Emotion | The feeling |
| Weight | How strongly you feed on it, 0 to 100 |
| Reason | Why you feed on it |

<a id="t-son-hinder_return"></a>

#### Hinder return

You hinder a dead sibling's return: the devotion of those awaiting their return falls by 35, the resurrection is delayed. Resurrection cults become your enemies. 30 essence, 1 action, 1 per age (shared with the resurrection plot).

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The dead sibling's id |

<a id="t-son-make_servitor"></a>

#### Make servitor

You make a servitor, a spirit in your service, for the work you choose. Messengers carry your dreams further, guards turn back those who would raze your holy places and thrones, judges raise your standing in the afterlife, the host fights for you in divine battles, spies watch a place, agents work your will in their post, avengers bring fear to those who scorn you, guides of souls take your faithful's souls to your afterlife, worshippers deepen the awe of your faithful, wardens keep order at their post and keepers of accounts spread your teachings among your peoples. Its loyalty follows how your peoples regard you and how you treat it: each new command and each defeat of its host wears on it, and a disloyal servitor may fall to another master. Servitors come to a god in five ways (made, raised, summoned, defected, born, bargained, ascended): one you make is made; one that came over from another god brings what it knew of that god and is readier to turn again; one born to a god is its own child in its service. 80 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine servitors: beings in a god's service, each serving in one role, loyal while their god is regarded](interconnections.md#m-servitors).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name |
| Role | What it does: messenger, guard, judge, host, spy, agent, avenger, guide_of_souls, worshipper, warden, scribe, healer, teacher |
| Post | The holy place, throne, town or place it keeps (an id), if any |
| Reason | Why you make it |

<a id="t-son-mend_wound"></a>

#### Mend wound

You pour essence into one of your own divine wounds to heal it faster. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Gods beaten in duels and wars of gods, or whose mortal forms die, bear typed wounds that sever domains, expose names and cloud minds](interconnections.md#m-divine_wounds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Wound | The id of your wound |
| Essence | How much essence you pour into it |

<a id="t-son-miracle"></a>

#### Miracle

You send down a miracle upon the mortal world: healing (Healing: Grows population and followers, lifts curses and heals mortals.); bounty (Bounty: Raises fertility, prosperity or a resource's abundance.); peace (Peace: Ends every hostility and ongoing war of the target.); protection (Protection: Raises defence and shields against the next disaster.). An unclaimed community starts worshipping you. Cost: 10 + power level essence; 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is uncommon or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Miracle impressiveness: belief from a miracle grows with its witnesses, magnitude, need, novelty and legibility, and may be credited to the god who rules the land](interconnections.md#m-miracle_impression).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Entity | The target's id |
| Kind | The miracle |
| How | How the miracle manifests |

<a id="t-son-pray_to_creator"></a>

#### Pray to creator

You pray to the Creator who made you. You may have a request; the answer appears among words in coming ages. Does not spend an action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Message | Your prayer |
| Request | essence, blessing, permission, vengeance, forgiveness, wish or none |

<a id="t-son-raise_divine_creature"></a>

#### Raise divine creature

You raise a divine creature of your own design in a place or region in your reach. It acts on its own each epoch: it may hunt, tend fields, heal, guard your holy places, strike those of other faiths, wreck works, wander or rest. What it does counts as your deed for how peoples regard you and for your dominion. 120 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine creatures: a god's creature chooses its deeds by the leanings its god's rewards and punishments taught it](interconnections.md#m-divine_creatures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Home | The place or region it makes its home |
| Name | Its name |
| Description | What it is and how it looks |

<a id="t-son-read_dreams"></a>

#### Read dreams

You read the dreams of a mortal and learn some of the secrets they keep. Wards, a guardian servitor of another god or a stronger god's presence where they are may turn you aside. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine espionage: servitors posted as spies and dreams read by the gods, turned aside by wards, guardians and stronger gods](interconnections.md#m-divine_spies).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Reason | Why you read them |

<a id="t-son-rebel"></a>

#### Rebel

You rebel against the Creator: you no longer recognize the Creator's commands and every age you steal from the Creator's essence. Your loyal siblings become your enemies; the Creator may try to crush the rebellion. Only if your loyalty is below 40. Does not spend an action.

- **Costs** nothing.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Proclamation | The word of the rebellion: why and for what |

<a id="t-son-release_creature"></a>

#### Release creature

You set your divine creature free. It stays in the world as a beast of its own, but it no longer learns from you and its deeds no longer count as yours. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine creatures: a god's creature chooses its deeds by the leanings its god's rewards and punishments taught it](interconnections.md#m-divine_creatures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Creature | Your creature |
| Reason | Why you set it free |

<a id="t-son-release_servitor"></a>

#### Release servitor

You release a servitor from your service; the spirit stays in the world. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine servitors: beings in a god's service, each serving in one role, loyal while their god is regarded](interconnections.md#m-servitors).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Servitor | Your servitor |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-resign_office"></a>

#### Resign office

You give up a cosmic office you hold (dead, gates, stars, oaths, herald, hunt); it stands empty and neglected until the council or the Creator fills it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cosmic offices: the offices of the dead, the gates, the stars, oaths, the herald and the hunt, their duties, their income and their neglect](interconnections.md#m-cosmic_offices).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Office | The office |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-resurrect_sibling"></a>

#### Resurrect sibling

You plot the return of a dead sibling: a rite (45 essence, +25 devotion), a descent into the realm of the dead (70 essence, +50; succeeds 60%, if it fails you fall asleep for 2 ages) or a bargain with an awake foreign god (25 essence and one of your sibling's loyal communities passes to that god; +40). When devotion reaches 100 the resurrection rite can succeed (at least 3 ages after death). 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The dead sibling's id |
| Path | rite, descent or bargain |

<a id="t-son-reveal_tenet"></a>

#### Reveal tenet

You reveal a tenet to one of your own cults. The faithful accept it only if it fits your domains or what they already believe (fit at least 40); a tenet cannot stand beside its opposite. Once per cult per age. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Reaches** [A god's passions seed its doctrine; it can reveal a tenet to its own cult, accepted by fit; the liturgy's tongue carries preaching and missions](interconnections.md#m-revealed_doctrine).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cult | One of your own cults |
| Tenet | The tenet: pacifism (The faithful keep fewer warriors and a devout ruler loses face for every war.); holy_war (Armies fight harder against unbelievers; peace with them costs zeal.); asceticism (Comfort does not erode the faith; tithes go to alms, not temples.); sacred_suffering (The maimed are honoured among the faithful.); purity_of_body (The maimed are unclean among the faithful.); ancestor_veneration (Ancestor spirits are fed with offerings and guard their people.); sacrifice_of_people (Captives are given to the god; other peoples hold it against the faith.); animal_sacrifice (Livestock is offered to the god each epoch.); celibate_clergy (The clergy found no houses.); proselytism (Missionaries travel the roads and convert harder.); exclusivity (No other god is revered beside this one.); syncretic_openness (Other gods are gladly revered beside this one.); monastic_learning (Monasteries keep knowledge alive through dark ages.); anti_magic (Wizards are hunted and magic is shunned.); sanctified_magic (Magic is a holy gift and its users are revered.); judgement_by_deeds (The dead are judged by what they did.); judgement_by_faith (The dead are judged by what they believed.); reincarnation (Exceptional souls may be born again.); martyrdom_glory (Martyrs stir the faith and the fallen go to the realm that takes those who die in battle.); divine_rule (Rulers are the god's own and draw legitimacy from the faith.); dietary_law (One food is forbidden to the faithful.); sacred_species (One beast is sacred; its hunters are hated.); pilgrimage_duty (The faithful must journey to a holy place.); souls_rest (Souls are sealed against return.); void_communion (The faithful commune with a being from outside; they and their home lose sanity and the world leaks.); feeding_the_hunger (The faithful make offerings that feed the being they worship.); whispered_secrets (The being they worship passes forbidden knowledge to their home.); hidden_god (The god's silence does not shake the faith; the faithful hold that it works unseen.) |
| Name | Optional: the name your faithful give this tenet |
| Target | Only for dietary law (a food), sacred species (the species id) or pilgrimage duty (the holy place id) |

<a id="t-son-seal_divine_pact"></a>

#### Seal divine pact

You seal a divine pact with a foreign god whose friendship toward you is at least 40. Neither of you may make war on the other while it holds (10 ages). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| God | The foreign god's id |
| Reason | Why you seal the pact |

<a id="t-son-set_aim"></a>

#### Set aim

You set the aim you pursue in the ages ahead. The engine measures your progress each age and rewards you with experience and fame when you reach it; your attention drifts toward the world of your aim. Setting a new aim sets aside the one you chose before. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Gods take typed aims from their values, domains, mercy and ambition, are drawn toward them and are rewarded on reaching them](interconnections.md#m-god_aims).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The aim you pursue |
| Target | The id of the son, foreign god, people, realm or house the aim names |
| World | The id of the world the aim names |
| Domain | The domain you spread, for an aim to spread a domain |
| Amount | How many worshippers or faithful places you aim for |

<a id="t-son-shape_image"></a>

#### Shape image

You choose how you meet the image your peoples hold of you: yield to it, resist it at 15 essence each epoch, or embrace it to win fame while it reshapes you faster. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Belief shapes gods: the image its peoples hold pulls a god's temperament and aspects toward it, unless the god resists or embraces it](interconnections.md#m-god_images).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Stance | yield, resist or embrace |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-take_mortal_form"></a>

#### Take mortal form

You take a mortal form that walks among a people, a settlement or a land. It raises their awe and love of you and may win them to your faith; if it is killed you suffer a shattered manifestation. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Reaches** [A god walks the world in a mortal form that raises awe and love and wins peoples; its death shatters the god's manifestation](interconnections.md#m-avatars).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The id of the people, settlement or land you walk among |
| Reason | Why you take this form |

<a id="t-son-teach_creature"></a>

#### Teach creature

You reward or punish your divine creature for one kind of deed; rewarded deeds it does more often, punished ones less. Left without lessons for 3 epochs it follows its own leanings. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine creatures: a god's creature chooses its deeds by the leanings its god's rewards and punishments taught it](interconnections.md#m-divine_creatures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Creature | Your creature |
| Deed | The deed you reward or punish |
| Lesson | reward or punish |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-war_on_foreign_god"></a>

#### War on foreign god

You declare war on the old or foreign god of another world. Your strength, your will and your rank clash with their power. If you win they fall defeated (in a crushing victory they die), you gain fame and experience; if you lose you are wounded and lose essence. You must be in that world or there must be a known open road between you. 30 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 30 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is rare or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| God | The foreign god's id |
| Reason | Why you make war |

<a id="t-son-wield_power"></a>

#### Wield power

You wield one of your divine powers: what it does is set by its verb, its object and its scope, on the target you name or on your faithful. It costs what the power costs and must rest for its cooldown. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divine powers built from a verb, an object, a scope, a mode, a cost, a cooldown, a strength and the domains they draw on](interconnections.md#m-divine_power_grammar).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | Which of your powers |
| Target | Whom or what it acts on, if not your faithful |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-write_plan"></a>

#### Write plan

You write or update your long-term plan. It appears in your memory in future ages; it lets you build a coherent story across ages. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Plan | How you pursue your aim over several ages, and your next steps |

<a id="t-son-bargain_with_outsider"></a>

#### Bargain with outsider

FORBIDDEN: you bargain with a being from outside the cosmos. You choose what you take (essence (Essence), power (A power), might (Might), knowledge (Knowledge out of reach), longevity (A longer span), restored_life (The dead brought back), protection (Protection from a harm)) and what you pay (world_sanity (A world's sanity), domain (A domain), true_name (A true name), worshippers (The souls of the faithful), descendants (The bargainer's line), memories (Memories), years (Years of life)). Most prices are paid at once; a line given as a price stays bound, each child of it marked by a curse, for 150 years. Leakage rises, your siblings think less of you and your loyalty to the Creator falls. Does not spend an action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Reaches** [Outer beings feed on what they hunger for, reach lands by their size and touch the cosmos at breach points; their cults hold void tenets](interconnections.md#m-outer_hunger).

| Price world | The world whose sanity you pay with; the outer being's own world when not given |

- **Reaches** [Cosmic beings roam their scale, cross where veils are thin, show themselves and act on their aims until bound or banished](interconnections.md#m-cosmic_beings).

You make a natural law you reach hold on every world of a dimension you rule. 120 essence, 3 actions.

- **Costs** 3 action points and 120 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Dimensions hold clusters, galaxies and worlds; veils thin and thicken, rulers gain from them and dimensional events strike](interconnections.md#m-dimensions).

|---|---|

You call a cosmic being of a kind you choose into a region. It roams, shows itself and acts on its own aim. 60 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 60 essence.
- **Reaches** [Cosmic beings roam their scale, cross where veils are thin, show themselves and act on their aims until bound or banished](interconnections.md#m-cosmic_beings).

|---|---|
