<a id="t-son-call_impact"></a>

#### Call impact

You call a body from the sky down upon a region of a world. It strikes next epoch unless someone turns it aside: it ruins the region, leaves a crater, can bring a winter over the whole world and can leave metal from the sky. Bodies: asteroid (Asteroid), comet (Comet), rogue_world (Rogue world), called (Called down by a god), moon_fragment (Fragment of a broken moon), wreck (Fallen made thing), ejecta (Thrown from another world); a fragment falls only where the ring of a broken moon circles the world, a made thing only where one circles it, and a made thing leaves salvage. 60 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 60 essence.
- **Reaches** [Bodies from fields, comets and rogue worlds strike or pass by; warned peoples pray, hoard and flee, and a strike leaves a crater, ruin, a winter, a remembered year, star metal and a legend](interconnections.md#m-impacts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The id of the region it falls on |
| Body | What falls |
| Reason | Why you call it |

<a id="t-son-claim_galaxy"></a>

#### Claim galaxy

You become the patron of a galaxy one of whose worlds you reach. You gain essence from each world in it that has you as its patron, and your domains move how many stars it bears. 80 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 80 essence.
- **Reaches** [Galaxies bear stars in their nurseries, age, merge, burst from their nuclei and live through galactic events, and patrons gain from them](interconnections.md#m-galaxy_cycles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Galaxy | The id of the galaxy |
| Reason | Why you claim it |

<a id="t-son-claim_star"></a>

#### Claim star

You become the patron of a star. You gain essence from each community that worships it. 40 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 40 essence.
- **Reaches** [Stars age through their stages, dim, flare by their steadiness, give off radiation and are worshipped](interconnections.md#m-star_lives).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Star | The id of the star |
| Reason | Why you claim it |

<a id="t-son-create_cluster"></a>

#### Create cluster

You gather a new cluster in a dimension, or inside a larger cluster, that holds a world you reach; with neither it lies in the prime dimension. Its kind sets the heat of the medium between its galaxies and how they are placed in it. A greater work than a galaxy: 180 essence, 4 actions.

- **Costs** 4 action points and 180 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Clusters hold galaxies; their hot medium strips star formation and their drift lengthens the roads between them](interconnections.md#m-clusters).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The cluster's name |
| Kind | Cluster kind: galaxy_group (Galaxy group), local_group (Local group), poor_cluster (Poor cluster), rich_cluster (Rich cluster), supercluster (Supercluster), filament (Filament), wall (Wall), void (Void), crown_cluster (Crown cluster), drowned_cluster (Drowned cluster), forge_cluster (Forge cluster), dream_cluster (Dream cluster), mirror_cluster (Mirror cluster) |
| Dimension | The id of the dimension it lies in |
| Parent cluster | The id of the larger cluster it lies inside |
| Description | What the cluster is like |

<a id="t-son-create_dimension"></a>

#### Create dimension

You bring a new dimension into being, on its own or inside a dimension that holds a world you reach. You rule it. Its kind sets its veil, stability, time, magic, hostility, sanity drain and leak. The greatest of works: 200 essence, 5 actions.

- **Costs** 5 action points and 200 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Dimensions described by aspects of form and matter, each pulling the dimension's values its own way every epoch](interconnections.md#m-dimension_aspects) and [Dimensions hold clusters, galaxies and worlds; veils thin and thicken, rulers gain from them and dimensional events strike](interconnections.md#m-dimensions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The dimension's name |
| Kind | Dimension kind: prime (Prime dimension), parallel (Parallel dimension), mirror (Mirror dimension), pocket (Pocket dimension), bubble_universe (Bubble universe), brane (Brane), higher_dimensional (Higher-dimensional space), antimatter (Antimatter dimension), quantum_branch (Quantum branch), time_reversed (Time-reversed dimension), compactified (Compactified dimension), astral (Astral plane), ethereal (Ethereal plane), dream (Dream dimension), shadow (Shadow dimension), spirit (Spirit realm), fey (Fey realm), elemental (Elemental plane), celestial (Celestial realm), infernal (Infernal realm), abyssal (Abyssal realm), limbo (Limbo), far_realm (Far realm), clockwork (Clockwork plane), chaos (Plane of chaos), underworld (Underworld), divine_domain (Divine domain), void_between (Void between), made (Made realm) |
| Element | A matter it is made of, if any: fire, water, earth, air, void, light, shadow, ice, lightning, metal, wood; each aspect pulls the dimension's values every epoch |
| Form | A form of the world it is made of, if any: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream, law; each aspect pulls the dimension's values every epoch |
| Parent dimension | The id of the dimension it lies inside |
| Description | What the dimension is like |

<a id="t-son-create_galaxy"></a>

#### Create galaxy

You make a new galaxy inside a cluster, or loose in a dimension, that holds a world you reach; with neither it lies in the prime dimension. You become its patron. It starts without stars and bears them in its nurseries over the epochs. A great work: 150 essence, 3 actions.

- **Costs** 3 action points and 150 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Galaxies bear stars in their nurseries, age, merge, burst from their nuclei and live through galactic events, and patrons gain from them](interconnections.md#m-galaxy_cycles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The galaxy's name |
| Galaxy type | Galaxy type: spiral (Spiral galaxy), barred_spiral (Barred spiral galaxy), grand_design_spiral (Grand-design spiral), flocculent_spiral (Flocculent spiral), lenticular (Lenticular galaxy), elliptical (Elliptical galaxy), giant_elliptical (Giant elliptical galaxy), dwarf_elliptical (Dwarf elliptical galaxy), dwarf_spheroidal (Dwarf spheroidal galaxy), irregular (Irregular galaxy), dwarf_irregular (Dwarf irregular galaxy), ring (Ring galaxy), peculiar (Peculiar galaxy), starburst (Starburst galaxy), active (Active galaxy), quasar_host (Quasar host), low_surface_brightness (Low surface brightness galaxy), ultra_diffuse (Ultra-diffuse galaxy), living (Living galaxy), dead (Dead galaxy), shattered (Shattered galaxy), forged (Forged galaxy), dreaming (Dreaming galaxy), mirror (Mirror galaxy), crowned (Crowned galaxy), hollow (Hollow galaxy), ember (Ember galaxy), veiled (Veiled galaxy) |
| Cluster | The id of the cluster it joins |
| Dimension | The id of the dimension it lies in, outside any cluster |
| Description | What the galaxy is like |

<a id="t-son-deflect_impact"></a>

#### Deflect impact

You turn aside a body that is due to strike a world. 50 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 50 essence.
- **Reaches** [Bodies from fields, comets and rogue worlds strike or pass by; warned peoples pray, hoard and flee, and a strike leaves a crater, ruin, a winter, a remembered year, star metal and a legend](interconnections.md#m-impacts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Impact | The id of the coming impact |
| Reason | Why you turn it aside |

<a id="t-son-echo_life"></a>

#### Echo life

In a cosmos that turns in ages, you mark the life of a mortal to carry into the next age: a newborn of that age may take up the same life course. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cyclic ages: in a cosmos set to turn, the apocalypse turns the age: ruins, half-remembered tales, lost arts and echoed lives](interconnections.md#m-cyclic_ages).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Reason | Why that life should carry on |

<a id="t-son-gather_star_system"></a>

#### Gather star system

You gather two or more stars within your reach into one system that moves together. 60 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 60 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The system's name |
| Star | The ids of the stars |
| Reason | Why you gather them |

<a id="t-son-open_breach"></a>

#### Open breach

You open a breach in a region you reach through which an outer being can press into the world. 60 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 60 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Being | The id of the outer being |
| Region | The id of the region |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-open_cosmic_bed"></a>

#### Open cosmic bed

On the world you are on, you open a bed of the cosmic travel resource in a region or place: mortals can claim it and travel between worlds. The cosmos's travel resource must be defined. 60 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 60 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The region or place where the bed opens |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-open_realm"></a>

#### Open realm

You open a sealed made realm again. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The made realm |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-open_rift"></a>

#### Open rift

You tear a rift in a region of a world into another dimension. The region gains leak, magic and danger, the dimension's veil (its barrier to other dimensions) weakens, and beings of that dimension may cross. If you name a world of that dimension, a passage opens between the two worlds. 40 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 40 essence.
- **Reaches** [Rifts tear regions open into other dimensions, grow or shrink, open passages between worlds and leave leak wounds](interconnections.md#m-rifts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The id of the region where the rift opens |
| Dimension | The id of the dimension it opens into |
| Target world | The id of a world in that dimension for a passage, or nothing |
| Reason | Why you open it |

<a id="t-son-plan_prehistory"></a>

#### Plan prehistory

You give a world you made, still empty of lore, a prehistory: at the end of this age the engine runs its past for the epochs you name while every other world is held, and its happenings become the world's past. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Prehistory: a past run before a world joins the cosmos, borrowed from its source, mirrored, or dreamt again each epoch](interconnections.md#m-dreamt_pasts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world you made that gets a past |
| Epochs | How many epochs of past it lives |
| Reason | Why you give it a past |

<a id="t-son-press_transformation"></a>

#### Press transformation

You press a thing in your reach toward becoming another kind of thing, a change whose conditions it already meets in part. Its progress toward the change grows; the Creator may have blocked it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Transformations: a son falls or is corrupted, a people loses its sapience, a faith or a realm forges a people, a star or a world gains a god](interconnections.md#m-transformations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The change you press toward |
| Subject | The thing you press (an id) |
| Reason | Why you press it |

<a id="t-son-raise_phenomenon"></a>

#### Raise phenomenon

You raise a phenomenon in a region you reach; it grows and acts on the land around it until it fades or is held. 70 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 70 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is legendary or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of phenomenon |
| Region | The id of the region |
| Reason | Why you raise it |

<a id="t-son-rule_dimension"></a>

#### Rule dimension

You take rule of a dimension one of whose worlds you reach. You gain essence from it each age, and your domains move its veil and its stability. 100 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 100 essence.
- **Reaches** [Dimensions hold clusters, galaxies and worlds; veils thin and thicken, rulers gain from them and dimensional events strike](interconnections.md#m-dimensions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Dimension | The id of the dimension |
| Reason | Why you take rule of it |

<a id="t-son-seal_realm"></a>

#### Seal realm

You seal a made realm: no one may go in or out until it is opened. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The made realm |
| Reason | Why you seal it |

<a id="t-son-seal_rift"></a>

#### Seal rift

You seal an open rift; its passage closes. 30 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 30 essence.
- **Reaches** [Rifts tear regions open into other dimensions, grow or shrink, open passages between worlds and leave leak wounds](interconnections.md#m-rifts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Rift | The id of the open rift |
| Reason | Why you seal it |

<a id="t-son-set_cosmic_feature"></a>

#### Set cosmic feature

You set a feature of the kind you choose in the heavens. A wormhole joins two worlds, a rogue world needs its star or its galaxy, and the other kinds belong to a star. Each star, galaxy and world you name must be within your reach. 80 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 80 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of feature |
| Name | The feature's name |
| Star | The id of the star it belongs to |
| Galaxy | The id of the galaxy it drifts in |
| World | The id of the first world, for a wormhole |
| World b | The id of the second world, for a wormhole |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-son-set_natural_law"></a>

#### Set natural law

You reshape how a natural law in your reach works: what it does to the thing it governs, the amount, when it holds, and whom it spares (none (Spares no one), creator_chosen (The Creator's chosen), author_worshippers (Its author's faithful), heroes (Heroes), avatars (Avatars), named_kind (A named kind), office_holders (Holders of a divine office), bargainers (Those bound by an outer bargain)). A law that spares a named kind names the species or people it does not touch. A law you did not write costs essence to take up and becomes yours to keep. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Natural laws are written as a target, an operator, a value, a condition, exceptions and a span; they press on stats and on made things, contest by strength, and peoples hold theories of them](interconnections.md#m-natural_law_grammar).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Law | The natural law |
| Operator | What the law does to the thing it governs |
| Value | The amount it works with, where 100 leaves the thing as it is |
| Condition | When the law holds |
| Exception | Whom the law spares; leave empty to keep it as it is |
| Exempt kind | The species or people the law spares, when it spares a named kind |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-shape_cycle"></a>

#### Shape cycle

You move one planetary cycle of the world you stand on (carbon, water, nutrients, oxygen, rock, heat, ice) up or down; the cycle then flows on from where you set it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [World cycles: carbon, water and nutrients flow through each world from fire, forest, ocean, ice, ash, rivers and erosion, and set its warmth, rain, seas and soil](interconnections.md#m-world_cycles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world you stand on |
| Cycle | Which cycle |
| Change | How far to move it, -30 to 30 |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-shape_galactic_structure"></a>

#### Shape galactic structure

You shape a new structure of the kind you choose in a galaxy you are patron of or whose worlds you reach. Stars inside it take what it gives. 70 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 70 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Galaxies bear stars in their nurseries, age, merge, burst from their nuclei and live through galactic events, and patrons gain from them](interconnections.md#m-galaxy_cycles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Galaxy | The id of the galaxy |
| Kind | The kind of structure |
| Name | The structure's name |
| Reason | Why you shape it |

<a id="t-son-shape_structure_type"></a>

#### Shape structure type

You give a community a building type of its own for one kind of structure: it is entered in the atlas under its name with the form, storeys and material you choose, and the community's builders raise that kind to it from then on. 10 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 10 essence.
- **Reaches** [Megastructures: star collectors, beacons, orbital rings, planetary engines and habitat rings work on their stars, routes and worlds while their control cores hold](interconnections.md#m-megastructures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Owner | The community that will raise it |
| Structure kind | The kind of structure it is a type of; one of: temple (Temple), shrine (Shrine), monastery (Monastery), necropolis (Necropolis), library (Library), school (School), observatory (Observatory), academy (Academy), hospital (Hospital), wall (Walls), gate (Gatehouse), tower (Tower), citadel (Citadel), watchtower (Watchtower), fort (Fort), palace (Palace), court (Court), prison (Prison), market (Market), harbour (Harbour), warehouse (Warehouse), granary (Granary), caravanserai (Caravanserai), bank (Bank), forge (Forge), mill (Mill), tannery (Tannery), mine (Mine), quarry (Quarry), shipyard (Shipyard), workshop (Workshop), alchemist_lab (Reagent laboratory), road (Road), bridge (Bridge), aqueduct (Aqueduct), sewer (Sewers), canal (Canal), irrigation (Irrigation works), dam (Dam), monument (Monument), tomb (Tomb), arena (Arena), theatre (Theatre), wonder (Wonder), cosmic_gate (Cosmic gate), cosmic_archive (Cosmic archive), firehouse (Firehouse), bathhouse (Bathhouse), fountain (Fountain), park (Park), slaughterhouse (Slaughterhouse), almshouse (Almshouse), brewery (Brewery), sawmill (Sawmill), fishery (Fishery), charcoal_kiln (Charcoal kiln), stables (Stables), siege_workshop (Siege yard), mint (Mint), tavern (Tavern), town_hall (Town hall), guildhall (Trade hall), barracks (Barracks), orphanage (Orphanage), apothecary (Apothecary), training_ground (Training ground), museum (Museum), plaza (Plaza), kontor (Trading post), embassy (Embassy), lighthouse (Lighthouse), signal_tower (Signal tower), customs_house (Customs house), toll_gate (Toll gate), courthouse (Courthouse), archive (Archive), printing_house (Printing house), laboratory (Laboratory), foundry (Foundry), smelter (Smelter), kiln (Kiln), glassworks (Glassworks), textile_mill (Textile mill), dye_works (Dye works), paper_mill (Paper mill), bakery (Bakery), dairy (Dairy), distillery (Distillery), refinery (Refinery), power_plant (Power plant), silo (Silo), cistern (Cistern), reservoir (Reservoir), canal_lock (Canal lock), tunnel (Tunnel), rail_station (Rail station), airfield (Airfield), spaceport (Spaceport), dry_dock (Dry dock), arsenal (Arsenal), kennel (Kennel), menagerie (Menagerie), botanical_garden (Botanical garden), cemetery (Cemetery), gymnasium (Gymnasium), asylum (Asylum), quarantine_house (Quarantine house), inn (Inn), market_hall (Market hall), exchange (Exchange), university (University), seminary (Seminary), conservatory (Conservatory), opera_house (Opera house), gallery (Gallery), stadium (Stadium), orbital_ring (Orbital ring), space_elevator (Space elevator), space_station (Space station), star_collector (Star collector), beacon (Beacon), planetary_engine (Planetary engine), habitat_ring (Habitat ring), factory (Factory), data_centre (Data centre), office_tower (Office tower), apartment_block (Apartment block), mall (Mall), broadcast_tower (Broadcast tower), satellite_array (Satellite array), military_base (Military base), missile_silo (Missile silo), shield_generator (Shield generator), terraformer (Terraformer), vertical_farm (Vertical farm), recycling_plant (Recycling plant), water_purification (Water purification), cloning_facility (Cloning facility), robotics_works (Robotics works), ai_core (Machine mind core), teleporter (Teleporter), covenant (Covenant) |
| Form | Its building form, if you choose; one of: hall, longhouse, tower, keep, dome, spire, stepped, pyramid, courtyard, ring, tent, mound, arcade, terrace, platform, bridge_form, wall_line, tunnel_form, orbital, high_rise, arcology, bunker, floating, geodesic, modular, grown, carved, stilt, vault |
| Storeys | How many storeys it rises, if you choose |
| Material | What it is built of, if you choose; one of: wood, calcified_tissue, hide, leather, cloth, stone, flint, obsidian, copper, tin, bronze, iron, steel, silver, gold, glass, crystal, ivory, chitin, great_beast_tissue, star_metal, void_glass, divine_tissue, living_wood, halite, amber, lead, skysteel, sulfur, clay, gems, mud_brick, fired_brick, dressed_stone, rubble, lime_mortar, hydraulic_concrete, thatch, tile, ceramic, silk, resin, shell, polymer, composite, rubber, paper, lacquer |
| Name | What the type is called; left empty, the community names it in its own tongue |
| Description | What sets the type apart, in your words |

<a id="t-son-shield_world"></a>

#### Shield world

You strengthen or strip the magnetic field of a world within your reach for some epochs. A stronger field turns flares and storms from the sky away from its ground, keeps its signals working and its compasses true; a weaker one lets them through, spreads its auroras and harms those who live outside shelter. 60 essence, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Space weather: stars turn through their cycles and send flares and storms, fields reverse, and worlds take what their shields let through](interconnections.md#m-space_weather).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world |
| Change | How much stronger (positive) or weaker (negative) its field grows, -100 to 100 |
| Epochs | For how many epochs, 1 to 50 |
| Reason | Why you change it |

<a id="t-son-shift_world_nature"></a>

#### Shift world nature

You move one share of the nature of the world you walk: how much timber, food, ore and room for life it gives, how readily its shipyards launch hulls, and how often its storms, quakes, floods, droughts and plagues come. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [World natures: each world carries the nature of its kind as values the god can set, and harvests, extraction, eating, fire, seasons, disasters, roads, towns and wars read it](interconnections.md#m-world_views).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world you walk |
| Share | The share you move: timber_pct, food_need_pct, capacity_pct, ore_yield_pct, ship_pct, storm_pct, quake_pct, flood_pct, drought_pct or plague_pct |
| Change | How far you move it, up or down |
| Reason | Why you move it |

<a id="t-son-stir_dimension"></a>

#### Stir dimension

You bring about an event in a dimension you rule or whose worlds you reach; some events need a second dimension or a world. 100 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 100 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Dimensions hold clusters, galaxies and worlds; veils thin and thicken, rulers gain from them and dimensional events strike](interconnections.md#m-dimensions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Dimension | The id of the dimension |
| Kind | The kind of dimensional event |
| Other dimension | The id of a second dimension, for an event between two dimensions |
| World | The id of a world, for an event that touches one world |
| Reason | Why you bring it about |

<a id="t-son-stir_galaxy"></a>

#### Stir galaxy

You bring about an event in a galaxy you are patron of or whose worlds you reach; some events need a second galaxy or one of its stars. 90 essence, 2 actions.

- **Costs** 2 action points and 90 essence.
- **Only** a son whose card is mythic or rarer may use it.
- **Reaches** [Galaxies bear stars in their nurseries, age, merge, burst from their nuclei and live through galactic events, and patrons gain from them](interconnections.md#m-galaxy_cycles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Galaxy | The id of the galaxy |
| Kind | The kind of galactic event |
| Other galaxy | The id of a second galaxy, for an event between two galaxies |
| Star | The id of one of its stars, for an event of one star |
| Reason | Why you bring it about |

<a id="t-son-strike_being"></a>

#### Strike being

You strike a cosmic or outer being with the force you choose; the blow wounds its body part by part, and a wounded being loses might, sight or the power to cross between worlds until it heals. 25 essence for each point of force, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point and 25 essence.
- **Reaches** [Wounds of cosmic and outer beings: a vast body takes injuries and lasting conditions that cut its might, sight and roaming until it heals or falls](interconnections.md#m-cosmic_wounds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Being | The being you strike |
| Force | How hard you strike, 1 to 10 |
| Reason | Why you strike it |

<a id="t-son-turn_hunger"></a>

#### Turn hunger

You turn the hunger of a being from outside toward something else it will feed on. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Outer beings feed on what they hunger for, reach lands by their size and touch the cosmos at breach points; their cults hold void tenets](interconnections.md#m-outer_hunger).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outer | The being from outside |
| Hunger | What it will hunger for |
| Reason | Why |

<a id="t-son-ward_against_outer"></a>

#### Ward against outer

You ward the cosmos against an outer being. The larger it is, the more essence it costs; you lose some sanity, the being loses interest and its breach points weaken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Outer beings feed on what they hunger for, reach lands by their size and touch the cosmos at breach points; their cults hold void tenets](interconnections.md#m-outer_hunger).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outsider | The outer being's id |
| Reason | Why you ward against it |

<a id="a-mortal"></a>

## The tools of the mortals

The notable mortals and the communities of a world act too. Each epoch they take a turn of their own and act through the tools below. A tool is offered only to those who may use it and only when it can work where they stand.

<a id="p-mortal-seeing"></a>

### Seeing the world

Tools that only look: they show the world, a thing or a memory more fully than the turn's own picture of it. They cost nothing and change nothing.

3 tools: [inspect entity](#t-mortal-inspect_entity), [look at world](#t-mortal-look_at_world), [search entities](#t-mortal-search_entities).

<a id="t-mortal-inspect_entity"></a>

#### Inspect entity

Shows the details of an entity. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Id | The entity's id |

<a id="t-mortal-look_at_world"></a>

#### Look at world

Shows your land, those near you, your language, what you know, what you own and recent events more widely than the prompt. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

<a id="t-mortal-search_entities"></a>

#### Search entities

Searches entities. Free.

- **Costs** nothing: it only looks.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Query | A word in the name or description |

<a id="p-mortal-body"></a>

### Body

9 tools: [answer sickness](#t-mortal-answer_sickness), [design cure](#t-mortal-design_cure), [design disease](#t-mortal-design_disease), [find carrier](#t-mortal-find_carrier), [host symbiont](#t-mortal-host_symbiont), [install augment](#t-mortal-install_augment), [remove augment](#t-mortal-remove_augment), [remove symbiont](#t-mortal-remove_symbiont), [seek cure](#t-mortal-seek_cure).

<a id="t-mortal-answer_sickness"></a>

#### Answer sickness

As ruler of a realm, or as a healer of your community, you set how it answers a sickness (close_borders, quarantine, burn_dead, expel_sick, prayer, seek_cure, sanitation, flee, inoculate, close_gatherings): closed borders cost trade and calm, a quarantine slows the spread but may set off riots, burning the dead slows it but grieves folk whose rites forbid it, driving out the sick slows it at home and turns folk against the sick, prayer comforts but crowds, seeking a cure sets the learned to work, and cleaning water and carrying away refuse costs treasury, cuts the routes by water, food and goods, and leaves works that keep cutting them; your order backs it even where no theory of your healers does. Each answer cuts only the routes it reaches. You may also treat it with a kind of cure (rest_and_care, herbal, antimicrobial, antiparasitic, antitoxin, nutritional, rite, exorcism, power, designed, surgery, antiviral, immunisation, diet); a cure works only on the kind of thing the sickness is, one that fails costs your healers trust, cutting it out may kill or infect where healers know little, and an antiviral needs advanced medicine and loses its hold faster when overused. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Agents of disease: what a disease is decides which cures work, where it survives and how fast it changes](interconnections.md#m-disease_agents) and [Plague responses: communities close borders, quarantine, burn the dead, drive out the sick, pray or seek a cure, each with its price](interconnections.md#m-plague_responses).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Disease | The sickness |
| Response | The answer |
| Cure class | The kind of cure you treat it with, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-design_cure"></a>

#### Design cure

You culture a cure for a disease from a potent line you hold, for your town. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Diseases made by design strike whom they were aimed at, get loose among their makers and are answered by cultured cures](interconnections.md#m-designed_disease).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Disease | The disease |
| Line | The line |

<a id="t-mortal-design_disease"></a>

#### Design disease

You make a disease by design, aimed at whom you name or at no one; it may get loose among its makers. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Diseases made by design strike whom they were aimed at, get loose among their makers and are answered by cultured cures](interconnections.md#m-designed_disease).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Name of the disease |
| Target | Whom it is made to strike, or empty |
| Lethality | How deadly, from 1 to 100 |
| Virulence | How readily it spreads, from 1 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-find_carrier"></a>

#### Find carrier

With deep medicine you find that a mortal in your land carries a sickness without falling ill; once found, a carrier spreads it no further. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Carriers: named mortals carry a sickness unseen and seed it where they travel, until physicians who know contagion find them](interconnections.md#m-carriers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal you suspect |
| Disease | The sickness |

<a id="t-mortal-host_symbiont"></a>

#### Host symbiont

You take something of a line you hold to live within you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Symbionts live within hosts, help or harm them, share their minds, steer them and spread among their neighbours](interconnections.md#m-symbionts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The line |
| Placement | Where it lives: skin, blood, organ, nerve, mind or gut |
| Relation | How it lives with you: mutual, commensal, parasitic or controlling |

<a id="t-mortal-install_augment"></a>

#### Install augment

You take an augment into your own body, in the part you name or across the whole body. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Augments lift skills and body functions, integrate or strain their bearers, cost upkeep and may be rejected](interconnections.md#m-augments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Body part | The part it goes in, or empty for the whole body |
| Kind | What the augment does |
| Make | How it is made |

<a id="t-mortal-remove_augment"></a>

#### Remove augment

You have one of your augments taken out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Augments lift skills and body functions, integrate or strain their bearers, cost upkeep and may be rejected](interconnections.md#m-augments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Augment | The augment |

<a id="t-mortal-remove_symbiont"></a>

#### Remove symbiont

You have something that lives within you taken out; removal harms by where it lived. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Symbionts live within hosts, help or harm them, share their minds, steer them and spread among their neighbours](interconnections.md#m-symbionts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Symbiont | What lives within you |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_cure"></a>

#### Seek cure

You work at a cure for a sickness at the hospital, academy or monastery where you live; your medicine adds to the search, and the cure, once found, belongs to your community and can be sold along its trade routes. A search by a kind of cure that fits what the sickness is goes faster, and one that does not fit goes slower. Or you seek relief there for a condition you or your close kin bear, whatever its cause; your medicine decides whether it eases. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Agents of disease: what a disease is decides which cures work, where it survives and how fast it changes](interconnections.md#m-disease_agents), [Conditions without an agent: bodies that wear by age, diet, fouling, heredity, strain and old wounds, and ease by medicine](interconnections.md#m-agentless_conditions) and [Cure research: hospitals, academies and monasteries race for a cure with their scholars and specimens; the cure brings prestige and is sold along trade routes](interconnections.md#m-cure_research).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Disease | The sickness, or none when you seek relief for a condition |
| Condition | The condition to seek relief for, or none when you work at a cure for a sickness |
| Cure class | The kind of cure you seek (rest_and_care, herbal, antimicrobial, antiparasitic, antitoxin, nutritional, rite, exorcism, power, designed, surgery, antiviral, immunisation, diet), or none |

You bind a mind of your making to obey your word. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mind | The mind |

<a id="t-mortal-bind_spirit"></a>

#### Bind spirit

With magic you bind a free spirit in your own or a neighbouring region to an item, ship or monument there (requires magic knowledge). The item becomes ensouled; bound to a monument, the monument moves and acts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The free spirit's id |
| Target | The item, vessel or monument |

#### Copy mind

You copy your mind, or one you made, into another seat; the copy is a little less faithful than the source. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mind | The mind |
| Seat | Where the copy will run |

<a id="t-mortal-develop_knowledge"></a>

#### Develop knowledge

Your community develops a new piece of knowledge (a technology, craft, school or method). Branch: agriculture (Knowers' population grows faster; famine and drought strike more softly.); metallurgy (Weapons and armour they produce are stronger.); seafaring (Trade, war and migration with communities beyond the sea.); writing (Their languages grow complex; what they know spreads faster to partners.); architecture (Their structures are grander and sturdier, and withstand earthquakes.); medicine (Plagues strike more softly; their mortals live longer.); magic (Their magic grows and the items they produce gain might.); warfare (Their armies fight harder.); astronomy (They read the star of the epoch, sense disasters early and suffer them less.); trade (Their trade routes bring more prosperity.); sky_navigation (Can discover routes between worlds; journeys are less likely to go astray.); shipbuilding (Can build ships that sail between worlds.); gate_lore (Can open and close ancient gates; gate journeys are swift.); pathfinding (Can fold space with the cosmic travel resource.); void_living (Can settle worlds with thin or no air; loses fewer travellers.); immortality (Their mortals reach for immortality.); biomancy (Combines and alters living things: merges species into new ones and steers mutations.); forbidden (Great power in war and magic, at the price of sanity and a leak into their world.); civics (Their laws, offices, faith and thought grow into institutions that shape how the realm is run.); law (Their courts and codes grow; written and codified law come within reach sooner.); natural_history (They know living kinds better: breeding, anatomy and new materials open to them.); language_lore (They read and translate other tongues; ciphers and interpreters come easier.); mathematics (Their reckoning sharpens engineering, navigation and accounts, up to signs for unknowns.). If it is built on knowledge you already have it is stronger. A named mortal needs a learned skill of at least 10. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Skills, callings and teaching](interconnections.md#m-skills).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Branch | The knowledge's branch |
| Description | What it is, how it was found, which material or observation it rests on |
| Base | Knowledge you know that it is built on (at most 3) |
| Resource | The resource it relies on |
| Image prompt | English image prompt: those who practise this technique, with what they use and where. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-mortal-free_mind"></a>

#### Free mind

You release a mind of your making from service. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mind | The mind |

<a id="t-mortal-keep_pet"></a>

#### Keep pet

You take one beast of a small tame kind your people keep as your own companion. It stays at your side, comforts you, and if its kind hunts or guards it sharpens your hunting; its death lowers your mood. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Mortals take small tame beasts as companions; a pet comforts its owner, eases loneliness, sharpens their hunting and is mourned at its death](interconnections.md#m-pets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The kind it is of, from those your people keep; leave empty to take any |

You link your mind with another's, so the two share what they think. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The one you link with |

You make a mind in a thing near you, by the method you name, with the will and purpose you give it; your skill sets how well it turns out. 1 action.

- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

|---|---|

- **Reaches** [Minds have seats, will, a bond with their makers, saved states and lineages; made minds grow or lose will, drift, wake and leave](interconnections.md#m-minds).

|---|---|
| Mind | The two minds, the one that stays first |

With biomancy you merge two creature species in your world, near you (at most 4 steps), into a single merged (requires biomancy knowledge). The merged species carries the traits of both species; but it may get out of control. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

#### Tame species

You try to tame a creature species on your land. If you succeed it serves you in war, transport and trade; flying or gigantic ones can carry you between worlds. Dangerous and intelligent species are hard. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals and factions.
