<a id="t-mortal-answer_gift"></a>

#### Answer gift

You answer a gift someone gave you, so you owe them nothing; answer a rank-claiming gift with more or lose face. An answer worth 150% of the gift or more exceeds it and puts the first giver in your debt; two houses answering each other so spend their coin and raise their standing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Gift ledgers: gifts bind their receivers to answer in kind and in time; refusals insult and unanswered gifts cost face](interconnections.md#m-reciprocity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Gift | The gift you owe |
| Value | Coin your answer is worth, or empty to match what custom asks |

<a id="t-mortal-bargain_contract"></a>

#### Bargain contract

You bargain for a worker body you lead with one who hires its trade. Terms follow the strength of each side: the body's members, fund, solidarity and standing in law against the hirer's riches and the bound workers or made minds who could do the work. A much stronger hirer may lock the workers out; a body far weaker may take to a strike instead of agreeing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organised labour: worker bodies with members, dues and funds, contracts that set wages and conditions, strikes, lockouts and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-labour_relations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Worker body | The body you lead |
| Hirer | The one who hires its trade |
| Demands | What you press for: wage, hours, safety, rest, keep or recognition |

<a id="t-mortal-bestow_gift"></a>

#### Bestow gift

You give someone a gift from your purse, or from your realm's if you rule one, on an occasion (courtesy, feast, circuit, offering, marriage, return, alliance, relief, birth, funeral, amends, patronage, hospitality, farewell, homecoming, initiation). It raises your standing and their regard. The occasion sets what is owed back: a gift at a birth binds the child to you, one to the bereaved is owed back at your own losses, amends settle a wrong and can end a feud, a patron's gift is repaid in loyalty, and a guest gift seals guest right. An unanswered gift costs its receiver face, and one who hates you may refuse it as an insult. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Gift ledgers: gifts bind their receivers to answer in kind and in time; refusals insult and unanswered gifts cost face](interconnections.md#m-reciprocity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Receiver | Who receives it |
| Value | Coin it is worth |
| Item | A thing of yours to give with it, or empty |
| Occasion | The occasion of the gift |

<a id="t-mortal-bid_for_org"></a>

#### Bid for org

You bid, for an organisation you head or a realm you rule, to take in another organisation. Its body may accept, or resist a hostile bid by seeking a friendly buyer or setting harsher terms; a closed bid joins its members, holdings and debts to yours. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Acquirer | The organisation you head, or the realm you rule for an absorption |
| Target | The organisation you bid for |
| Kind | The bid: merger, friendly_acquisition, hostile_takeover, buyout or absorption_by_polity |
| Price | The price you offer, or none to offer its worth |

<a id="t-mortal-call_dispute"></a>

#### Call dispute

You call the members of a worker body you lead out against the one who hires its first trade. Output stops in that trade while it lasts; the fund pays those out until it is spent, and the hirer loses each epoch. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organised labour: worker bodies with members, dues and funds, contracts that set wages and conditions, strikes, lockouts and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-labour_relations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Worker body | The body you lead |
| Act | How they press: strike, go_slow, boycott, occupation_of_works or sabotage |
| Demand | What they press for: wage, hours, safety, rest, keep or recognition |

<a id="t-mortal-cast_org_vote"></a>

#### Cast org vote

You cast your vote on the decision body of an organisation where you hold a seat, for or against a course; your vote joins those who back the same course, and the body votes at once. A course passes when it gains the quorum. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation whose body you sit on |
| Motion | The course voted on: grow, hold, specialise, diversify, expand_abroad, integrate_up, integrate_down, lobby, research or withdraw |
| Vote | Your vote: for or against |

<a id="t-mortal-choose_storylet"></a>

#### Choose storylet

You make the choice a storylet waits on you for, by its number among the choices your context lists; what follows is decided by the choice, and some choices lead to more. Left too long, you choose by your nature without thinking. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Storylets: typed event chains with conditions, a cast, two to four choices with typed effects, follow-ups and cooldowns](interconnections.md#m-storylets).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Run | The storylet waiting for you |
| Choice | The number of your choice |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_deed"></a>

#### Claim deed

You speak up about a deed: you lay a claim to it, as true, shared, false or inflated (the whole of a deed you shared), or you dispute a claim another has laid, which splits its renown until proof, a duel or a court settles it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Deeds are credited by claims; comrades share them, the vain claim them falsely, and witnesses, tokens and contests expose impostors](interconnections.md#m-claims_of_deeds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Deed | The deed |
| Kind | Your claim |
| Contest | True to dispute the claim that stands instead of laying your own |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_identity"></a>

#### Claim identity

You hold one of the identities you have closer, or loosen your hold on it. How much each counts for you decides whom you trust and with whom you side when your town divides. Layers: people, faith, class, locality, house, profession, generation, polity, circle, language, kind, cause, gift, calling. You can claim only a layer you have. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Identity layers and salience: holy war, hunger, invasion, strikes, preaching, segregation and mixed marriages set which identity matters](interconnections.md#m-identities).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Layer | Which identity |
| Loosen | True to loosen your hold instead of holding it closer |

<a id="t-mortal-confer_honour"></a>

#### Confer honour

As head of a realm, faith or order, confer one of its honours on someone for a deed on record. They gain renown, goodwill toward it and its privileges. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Honours conferred on persons: orders of merit that realms, faiths, orders and gods found, confer for deeds, sell and take back](interconnections.md#m-person_honours).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Honour kind | The honour |
| Holder | The one you honour |
| Deed incident | The deed it rewards |

<a id="t-mortal-disown"></a>

#### Disown

You cast one of your children, or of the house you head, out of the family: they lose their place in the house and the inheritance, may found a line of their own, and hold it against you. It can be mended. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Family dynamics: a parent's favour, rivalries between kin, casting out and mending, step kin and the sway of elders](interconnections.md#m-kin_dynamics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Member | The one you cast out |
| Reason | Why: disobedience, marriage, faith, crime or rivalry |

<a id="t-mortal-enclose_commons"></a>

#### Enclose commons

You enclose a common of your land for yourself; it yields you coin each epoch, while those who used it lose their living, grow restless and drift to the cities. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commons and enclosure: pastures, woods and fisheries worn down by overuse unless a council rules them, enclosed by rulers](interconnections.md#m-commons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commons | The common you enclose |

<a id="t-mortal-expose_hypocrisy"></a>

#### Expose hypocrisy

You show that someone professes a value and acts against it, naming happenings that show it if you know them. If the gap between what they profess and what they do is wide enough, they lose trust and standing, and those who follow them for that value may leave. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Professed values: the gap between what beings profess and what they do comes out and costs trust and following](interconnections.md#m-hypocrisy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you expose |
| Value axis | The value they profess |
| Evidence incident | Happenings that show them acting against it, if you know any |

<a id="t-mortal-favour_child"></a>

#### Favour child

You favour one of your children, or of the house you head. Where the custom of inheritance leaves a choice, the favoured child comes first to it; the others may grow rivals. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Family dynamics: a parent's favour, rivalries between kin, casting out and mending, step kin and the sway of elders](interconnections.md#m-kin_dynamics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | The child |

<a id="t-mortal-file_insolvency"></a>

#### File insolvency

You take an organisation you head before its realm's court for its debts: creditors claim, its holdings are weighed and shared, and the case ends by the path you ask for. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation you head |
| Path | How it is settled: liquidation, restructuring, rescue_by_owner, rescue_by_polity or seizure_by_creditor |

<a id="t-mortal-foster_child"></a>

#### Foster child

You send a child of yours, still young, to be raised in another household, whose head becomes its fosterer: the child grows loyal to that house and the two houses are bound. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | Your child |
| Household | The household that raises the child |

<a id="t-mortal-found_circle"></a>

#### Found circle

As leader of a faction, you make it a circle of a given kind that meets in your town. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Mortals who share a strong taste or belief gather into circles that bond their members, feed faction recruiting and spread a creed](interconnections.md#m-circles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of circle |

<a id="t-mortal-found_club"></a>

#### Found club

You found a club in your town (team_game, racing, combat, athletics, animal_contest, mind_games, water_sport, hunt); it plays matches, gathers fans and may come to sway the town. A club of animal contests keeps and breeds the beasts of its sport and needs a town that keeps them; a club of games of the mind trains its players and draws patrons. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Clubs and leagues: towns with arenas found clubs, realms gather them into leagues, matches are played and towns wager on them](interconnections.md#m-sports).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What the club plays, or empty to let it be chosen |

<a id="t-mortal-found_coop"></a>

#### Found coop

You lead a body of your town to pool its members' means as a cooperative, for one purpose: mill, boats, irrigation, insurance, burial, credit, granary, herding, school, housing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Factions: sixteen kinds pursuing typed goals by lobbying, bribes, petitions, strikes, riots, coups, murder, smuggling, raids and war funding](interconnections.md#m-factions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Purpose | What the cooperative is for |

<a id="t-mortal-found_faction"></a>

#### Found faction

You gather others in your town into a body of a kind you choose, and you lead it; you need some renown and a little wealth. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Factions: sixteen kinds pursuing typed goals by lobbying, bribes, petitions, strikes, riots, coups, murder, smuggling, raids and war funding](interconnections.md#m-factions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What kind of body; your people may found these unless their own ways say otherwise: merchant_guild, craft_guild, priesthood, warrior_order, noble_council, peasant_league, rebels, bandits, pirates, assassins, scholars_academy, secret_society, mercenary_company, interworld_federation, wandering_market, route_raiders, social_circle, crime_family, thieves_guild, smuggling_ring, pirate_confederacy, workers_union, engineers_society, financiers, veterans, worker_body, street_gang, trafficking_ring, fence_network, counterfeiting_ring |

<a id="t-mortal-found_honour"></a>

#### Found honour

As head of a realm, faith or order, found a new honour it can confer, ranked above those it has, with its privileges and a limit on holders (0 for none). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Honours conferred on persons: orders of merit that realms, faiths, orders and gods found, confer for deeds, sell and take back](interconnections.md#m-person_honours).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Conferrer | The realm, faith or order you head |
| Name reason | The name of the honour |
| Privileges | What it grants (precedence, pension, land, seat, immunity, title, none) |
| Limit | How many may hold it at once, 0 for any number |

<a id="t-mortal-found_worker_body"></a>

#### Found worker body

You found a body of the workers of your town for one or more trades: it gathers members and dues into a strike fund, bargains with those who hire them and can call them out. The law of the town decides whether it is banned, tolerated, recognised or compulsory. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organised labour: worker bodies with members, dues and funds, contracts that set wages and conditions, strikes, lockouts and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-labour_relations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Form | Its form: craft_union, industrial_union, general_union, companion_society, mutual_aid, worker_cooperative or made_mind_collective |
| Trades | The trades it speaks for: farming, herding, fishing, mining, crafting, trade, soldiering, clergy, scholarship, building, service, idle, transport, administration, gathering, industry, arts, healing or teaching |
| Settlement | Your town |

<a id="t-mortal-fund_research"></a>

#### Fund research

You set aside a budget from an organisation you head to research a technique its home could reach next, or a design. Its staff draw on the budget each epoch; when the work is done the organisation holds what it found, secret until it leaks out or is stolen. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation you head |
| Technique | The technique to pursue, or none when you pursue a design |
| Design | The design to pursue, or none when you pursue a technique |
| Budget | The treasury you set aside |

<a id="t-mortal-give_speech"></a>

#### Give speech

You speak in your town. At the assembly a speech moves the realm's standing, in the square or camp it stirs or calms the crowd, at the temple it moves the faithful; its force follows your charisma and the reach of the venue. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Humour and public speech: the humour of a people, speeches that move assemblies, crowds and congregations, mockery that offends or is punished](interconnections.md#m-speech).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Venue | Where: assembly, court, temple, square, school, camp, outlet, market or funeral |
| Aim | To what end: persuade, teach, mock, mourn, rally, defend, accuse, praise or warn |
| Audience node | Whom you speak to or of, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-go_on_adventure"></a>

#### Go on adventure

You embark on an adventure: hunting a dangerous creature (the world's course decides the fight at the end of the age; your weapons count) or seizing an item from someone else's hands (your agility and wits against its owner's defence). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The species to hunt or the item to seize |
| Goal | What you set out to do |

<a id="t-mortal-hold_gathering"></a>

#### Hold gathering

You hold a gathering of the kind you choose where you live, this epoch, and name the guests you most want; others come by friendship, kinship, duty and distance. Those who come grow closer, may fall in love, quarrel or learn each other's secrets. A feast costs coin for each guest. Councils, summits and tournaments are for rulers. One gathering planned at a time. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Gatherings: feasts, weddings, funerals, coronations, tournaments, hunts, councils, summits, festivals, fairs, games, performances, trials, executions, pilgrimages, processions, milestones and wakes](interconnections.md#m-gatherings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of gathering |
| Guest | Whom you invite by name |

<a id="t-mortal-join_cooperative"></a>

#### Join cooperative

You join a cooperative of your town and pool your means with its members; it eases their want. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Town life: taverns and inns as hubs of news and brawls, houses of welfare for the poor, the way to work and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-town_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Faction | The cooperative |

<a id="t-mortal-join_settlements"></a>

#### Join settlements

As one who rules several settlements of one world you join them into one city around the first, which keeps the others as quarters. Under one council or the core's rule the core's laws spread to them; under one council their unrest is shared. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [City rules, municipal services and settlements that grow together](interconnections.md#m-city_rules).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlements; the first is the core |
| Governance | How it is governed: one_council, federated_councils or ruled_by_core |

<a id="t-mortal-lead_expedition"></a>

#### Lead expedition

You lead an expedition with your companions into the ground your target lies in (site, wilds, underworld, realm, under_water, space, dimension); each of you takes the role your skills fit best (scout, guide, healer, fighter, scholar, porter, warder, interpreter, cartographer, engineer). Each leg uses provisions, light on dark ground and air where some of you cannot breathe; running short raises danger and stress, and those who return bring finds and renown. You travel by one way your people's bodies can use (on_foot, mounted, wheeled, boat, flying, burrowing, submerged, incorporeal, swimming, climbing); a way that finds little passage in the land you seek adds legs. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Expeditions into sites, wilds, underworlds and realms: a party, provisions, light, tools and medicine spent leg by leg, camps and ambushes](interconnections.md#m-expeditions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Where you go |
| Mode | How you travel; leave empty to take the way with the best passage |

<a id="t-mortal-lead_youth"></a>

#### Lead youth

In your years of youth, you take the lead of a youth group of your town and turn it to a purpose: its purpose moves the town's order, crime or unrest and what its members come to value. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Youth: its years, place, freedoms and duties; youth groups that act on their towns, misbehave, join gangs and movements, serve and come of age](interconnections.md#m-youth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Group | The youth group of your town |
| Purpose | The purpose: sport, service, courtship, defence, faith, mischief, crime, politics, learning or labour |

<a id="t-mortal-lock_out"></a>

#### Lock out

As a hirer, or the head of one, you shut a worker body's members out of your works until they yield. You lose what they would have made; their fund pays them while it lasts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organised labour: worker bodies with members, dues and funds, contracts that set wages and conditions, strikes, lockouts and cooperatives](interconnections.md#m-labour_relations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Hirer | Yourself or the organisation you head that hires them |
| Worker body | The body whose members you shut out |
| Demand | What you press for: wage, hours, safety, rest, keep or recognition |

<a id="t-mortal-make_camp"></a>

#### Make camp

You make your expedition camp now; the party rests off fatigue and stress for a ration each, though a night ambush may come. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Expeditions into sites, wilds, underworlds and realms: a party, provisions, light, tools and medicine spent leg by leg, camps and ambushes](interconnections.md#m-expeditions).

<a id="t-mortal-mock_the_ruler"></a>

#### Mock the ruler

You mock the ruler of your realm in public. Where the court tolerates mockery it costs the ruler standing and its folk approve of the ruler less; where it does not, you may be imprisoned. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Satire and the licensed fool: jesters and satirists mock rulers; tolerated mockery costs standing and vents anger, punished breeds grievance](interconnections.md#m-satire).

<a id="t-mortal-provide_service"></a>

#### Provide service

As one who rules a settlement you found or fund one of its services. A service reaches its districts as far as its budget pays for them: refuse and street cleaning keep it clean, water distribution brings water, night watch keeps order, burial grounds take the dead, market inspection keeps the markets honest, lighting cuts night crime and lengthens trade. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [City rules, municipal services and settlements that grow together](interconnections.md#m-city_rules).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement |
| Service | The service: refuse, lighting, water_distribution, street_cleaning, night_watch, burial_ground or market_inspection |
| Provider | A body you lead that pays for it; leave empty for the settlement itself |
| Budget | What it may spend each epoch |

<a id="t-mortal-reconcile_kin"></a>

#### Reconcile kin

You take back one you cast out of the family, restoring their place. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Family dynamics: a parent's favour, rivalries between kin, casting out and mending, step kin and the sway of elders](interconnections.md#m-kin_dynamics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Member | The one you take back |

<a id="t-mortal-reform_youth"></a>

#### Reform youth

As an elder of your town, you set a youth group to a measure: schooling raises its members' learning, service and sport turn it to those, and discipline raises its standing and turns trouble to defence. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Youth: its years, place, freedoms and duties; youth groups that act on their towns, misbehave, join gangs and movements, serve and come of age](interconnections.md#m-youth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Group | The youth group of your town |
| Measure | The measure: schooling, service, sport or discipline |

<a id="t-mortal-refuse_gift"></a>

#### Refuse gift

You refuse a gift someone gave you; you owe them nothing, but the giver takes it as an insult. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Gift ledgers: gifts bind their receivers to answer in kind and in time; refusals insult and unanswered gifts cost face](interconnections.md#m-reciprocity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Gift | The gift you refuse |

<a id="t-mortal-release_debt"></a>

#### Release debt

You release someone from the return they owe you for a gift; your standing rises and they owe you gratitude instead. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Gift | The gift whose return you forgive |

<a id="t-mortal-revoke_honour"></a>

#### Revoke honour

As head of the realm, faith or order that conferred it, take an honour back; its holder is shamed. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Honours conferred on persons: orders of merit that realms, faiths, orders and gods found, confer for deeds, sell and take back](interconnections.md#m-person_honours).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Honour | The honour held |

<a id="t-mortal-rouse_crowd"></a>

#### Rouse crowd

You speak to the folk of your town about a grievance (price_spike, execution, sermon, omen, rumour, fire, plague, beast, eclipse, miracle, famine, unpaid_wages, lost_match, victory, great_funeral, election, price_rise, arrest); your sway makes it easier for each to join once others have, and the town may rise in a riot, a panic or a lasting movement. Its guards and garrison hold them back. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Crowds: each town's anger, fear and fervour, and threshold cascades in a district that turn a trigger into a riot, a panic or a movement](interconnections.md#m-crowds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grievance | What you speak about |

<a id="t-mortal-rule_commons"></a>

#### Rule commons

You set a council over a common of your land, or have its households take it in turns by year; a common ruled by its users wears down far more slowly when many depend on it, and one taken in turns also eases the quarrels over it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commons and enclosure: pastures, woods and fisheries worn down by overuse unless a council rules them, enclosed by rulers](interconnections.md#m-commons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commons | The common the council rules |
| By turns | True to have its households take it in turns by year |

<a id="t-mortal-satirise"></a>

#### Satirise

You mock someone in public. Where your people allow mocking such a one, it costs them honour and gains you some; where they do not, it gives offence, and mocking a ruler where speech is censored is punished. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Humour and public speech: the humour of a people, speeches that move assemblies, crowds and congregations, mockery that offends or is punished](interconnections.md#m-speech).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you mock |
| Venue | Where: assembly, court, temple, square, school, camp, outlet, market or funeral |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_headship"></a>

#### Seek headship

You stand for the headship of an organisation you belong to or own. Where the headship is bought you pay for it at once; where it is chosen, you gather backers on its body and win when the term ends and its selection rule chooses you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_recognition"></a>

#### Seek recognition

As a parent of a child whose standing among your people is less than full, you have it recognised by the way your people allow, paying what that way costs; a recognised child inherits and may hold office as one born in full standing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Norms of intimacy and their scandals and sanctions, vows of restraint, the course of birth, and a child's standing](interconnections.md#m-unions_and_birth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | Your child |
| Path | The way: recognition, adoption, payment, rite or none |

<a id="t-mortal-set_commons_rule"></a>

#### Set commons rule

As ruler of a realm, set how a common in it is ruled (open, customary, council, enclosed, state, licensed, rotational). Under state rule your officials hold its use to the quota and its yield goes to your treasury; under licensed rule its use is sold as permits at the price you name. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commons and enclosure: pastures, woods and fisheries worn down by overuse unless a council rules them, enclosed by rulers](interconnections.md#m-commons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commons | The common |
| Rule | How it is ruled |
| Quota | Use allowed each epoch, 0 to 200 |
| Licence price | Coin a permit costs for each point of quota |

<a id="t-mortal-set_faction_goal"></a>

#### Set faction goal

As leader of a faction you set what it presses for: lower_taxes, monopoly_on, install_claimant, abolish_law, enact_law, war_on, peace_with, protect_craft, convert_realm, independence, loot, gain_privilege, win_office, ruin_rival, protect_members, found_settlement. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Factions: sixteen kinds pursuing typed goals by lobbying, bribes, petitions, strikes, riots, coups, murder, smuggling, raids and war funding](interconnections.md#m-factions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Goal | The aim |
| Target | Whom or what it aims at, if anyone |

<a id="t-mortal-set_land_use_rule"></a>

#### Set land use rule

As one who rules a settlement you set a rule for its land, for the whole place or one district. Rules of use decide which districts may grow there and ease the nuisance next to homes; limits cap how high homes rise or how dense districts grow; a required material or fire spacing cuts the spread of fire; frontage and green share make districts more desirable. Every rule raises the cost of building, and a weakly enforced rule on valuable land is evaded. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [City rules, municipal services and settlements that grow together](interconnections.md#m-city_rules).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement |
| District | One district; leave empty for the whole place |
| Rule | The rule: allowed_uses, banned_uses, height_limit, density_limit, build_material, fire_spacing, frontage_rule or green_share |
| Values | What the rule names: kinds (district kinds) for allowed or banned uses, level for a height or density limit, material for a build material |

<a id="t-mortal-set_role_custom"></a>

#### Set role custom

As ruler, you set your people's custom for who may hold a role and from what age. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Role norms: who each people lets hold office, work a craft, fight, serve the gods and inherit, by sex, age and class](interconnections.md#m-role_norms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Role | The role |
| Eligibility | Who may hold it |
| Min age | The least age to hold it |
| Stance | How taking it up is held |

<a id="t-mortal-socialise"></a>

#### Socialise

You spend time with another mortal where you are, in the way you choose. It moves how each of you feels about the other. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Mortals who share a place meet, talk, court, quarrel, teach, gift and brawl; friendship, romance, familiarity and rivalry grow into bonds](interconnections.md#m-relationships).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The mortal |
| Interaction | What you do with them |

<a id="t-mortal-split_org"></a>

#### Split org

You lead your clique out of an organisation and found a rival, taking members and a share of its holdings as large as the clique's votes. A clique needs enough of the body's votes to leave with a share. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The inner life of organisations: decision bodies, heads chosen by rule, votes, cliques, splits, mergers, research and insolvency](interconnections.md#m-governance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Org | The organisation |
| Clique | The clique you lead |
| Name reason | The name of the new organisation |

<a id="t-mortal-stir_crowd"></a>

#### Stir crowd

You call the folk of the town you are in to one act (riot, panic, lynching, conversion, revolt, strike, procession, revival, jubilation, demonstration, boycott, exodus, vigil, occupation, petition); your sway feeds the feeling the act grows from (anger, fear, fervour, grief, hope, joy, solidarity), and whether they follow depends on how many already feel it and on the guards that hold them back. A demonstration presses a demand without violence, a boycott refuses trade with those the town blames, an exodus leaves together, a jubilation rejoices. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Crowds: each town's anger, fear and fervour, and threshold cascades in a district that turn a trigger into a riot, a panic or a movement](interconnections.md#m-crowds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The town you are in |
| Act | What you call them to |

<a id="t-mortal-swear_kinship"></a>

#### Swear kinship

You swear kinship with a friend where you are, by the rite you name, and become sworn kin: you owe each other aid and vengeance as blood kin do, and breaking it is oathbreaking. You both must count each other as friends. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The friend you swear kinship with |
| Rite | The rite by which you swear it |

<a id="t-mortal-take_restraint_vow"></a>

#### Take restraint vow

You take a vow of restraint of your own will. Breaking it costs you honour. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Norms of intimacy and their scandals and sanctions, vows of restraint, the course of birth, and a child's standing](interconnections.md#m-unions_and_birth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The vow: celibacy or fidelity |
| Term | For how many epochs, from 0 for life to 10 |

<a id="p-mortal-household"></a>

### Household

10 tools: [adopt](#t-mortal-adopt), [call on kin](#t-mortal-call_on_kin), [convene kin group](#t-mortal-convene_kin_group), [found school method](#t-mortal-found_school_method), [hire servant](#t-mortal-hire_servant), [raise child](#t-mortal-raise_child), [set house duties](#t-mortal-set_house_duties), [steer marriages](#t-mortal-steer_marriages), [take leisure](#t-mortal-take_leisure), [tend home](#t-mortal-tend_home).

<a id="t-mortal-adopt"></a>

#### Adopt

You adopt another named mortal: it counts as of your lineage. It must be younger than you and near you. 1 per age, 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | The named mortal you adopt |
| Reason | Why and how you adopt |

<a id="t-mortal-call_on_kin"></a>

#### Call on kin

You call on kin of one role for what you need. Kin answer as far as your people's duties bind that role to you: support for food, coin or care, hosting for shelter, protection for protection. Kin whom no strong duty binds may refuse, and a refusal counts as a breached duty. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Kin duties and kin groups: help in need, fostering, mourning, vengeance, joking and avoidance, heads, splits, shared land and exogamy](interconnections.md#m-kin_duties).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Role | Which kin: parent, child, sibling, grandparent, grandchild, parent_sibling_first_line, parent_sibling_second_line, sibling_child, cousin_parallel, cousin_cross, parent_in_law, child_in_law, sibling_in_law, co_spouse, chosen_kin, godparent, sworn_kin, foster_kin, step_parent, step_child or half_sibling |
| Need | What you need: food, coin, care, shelter or protection |

<a id="t-mortal-convene_kin_group"></a>

#### Convene kin group

You call your kin group together on one matter: choose_head to choose a head, share_land to share the yield of the kin's land with members in need, feud to answer harm done to its members, marriage to tighten the rule against marrying within the kin, or migrate to bring its members to where you are. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Kin duties and kin groups: help in need, fostering, mourning, vengeance, joking and avoidance, heads, splits, shared land and exogamy](interconnections.md#m-kin_duties).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Group | A kin group you belong to |
| Agenda | The matter: choose_head, share_land, feud, marriage or migrate |

<a id="t-mortal-found_school_method"></a>

#### Found school method

You set the way a school teaches: its theory sets how fast its pupils learn, and its depth whether it teaches few things deeply or many broadly. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Infancy and childhood: infant survival and weaning, ages of naming, work, responsibility and marriage, child labour, upbringing, schooling](interconnections.md#m-childhood).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| School | The school |
| Theory | The theory: rote, dialectic, apprenticeship, discovery, ritual_transmission, examination, network_learning, mind_transfer or peer_teaching |
| Depth | From 0 for breadth to 100 for depth |

<a id="t-mortal-hire_servant"></a>

#### Hire servant

As head of your household or its head's spouse, you take someone of your town who is free into the service of your household, on the terms you set: a wage your household pays each epoch, board, or bondage where your law allows it. You may give the servant duties of the house. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Daily life: hours of the day, care of the body, duties of the house, service in others' houses, free time and norms of space](interconnections.md#m-daily_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Household | Your household |
| Terms | The terms of service: wage, board or bound |
| Wage | The wage your household pays each epoch when the terms are a wage |
| Duties | Duties of the house the servant keeps: food, water, fuel, cleaning, textiles, childcare, eldercare, animals, repairs, sickcare, household_rites, teaching or accounts |

<a id="t-mortal-raise_child"></a>

#### Raise child

As a parent of a child or the head of its household, you raise it by the ways you weigh: they move what the child will value, and harsh ways leave stress. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Infancy and childhood: infant survival and weaning, ages of naming, work, responsibility and marriage, child labour, upbringing, schooling](interconnections.md#m-childhood).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | The child you raise |
| Techniques | The ways you raise it, each with a weight |
| Techniques › Technique | The way: example, instruction, praise, shame, punishment, ritual_ordeal, storytelling, play, apprenticeship, schooling, peer_group, age_set, mentoring or travel |
| Techniques › Weight | How much of the upbringing goes this way, from 0 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-set_house_duties"></a>

#### Set house duties

As head of your household or its head's spouse, you decide who in your household keeps which duty of the house. Duties can go only to members and servants of your household; the load of each falls on whoever keeps it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Daily life: hours of the day, care of the body, duties of the house, service in others' houses, free time and norms of space](interconnections.md#m-daily_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Household | Your household |
| Assignments | Who keeps which duty |
| Assignments › Member | A member or servant of your household |
| Assignments › Duty | The duty: food, water, fuel, cleaning, textiles, childcare, eldercare, animals, repairs, sickcare, household_rites, teaching or accounts |

<a id="t-mortal-steer_marriages"></a>

#### Steer marriages

As head of a house you set its marriages to follow a long design across generations, toward an inherited gift or a union with a claim on a realm; love matches, childless couples and exposure stand in its way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Breeding plans: long designs steer a house's marriages toward a gift of the blood or a claim, against love, barrenness and exposure](interconnections.md#m-breeding_plans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | What the design is called |
| Locus | The inherited trait the design breeds for |
| Allele | The form of that trait it wants |
| Claim polity | The realm whose claimants the house should marry |
| Target share | Share of the house that should carry it, 1 to 100 |
| Steps | Steps the design needs, 1 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-take_leisure"></a>

#### Take leisure

You give part of your time to a pursuit you enjoy, at a place if you name one: it lifts your mood and eases your stress, and a pursuit of travel spends your coin at the place you visit. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Daily life: hours of the day, care of the body, duties of the house, service in others' houses, free time and norms of space](interconnections.md#m-daily_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The pursuit: conversation, games, sport, music, crafts, collecting, reading, excursion, tourism, drinking, worship_extra, rest, media, dance, storytelling, hunting, gardening, bathing or animal_keeping |
| Venue | Where you pursue it, if anywhere in particular |

<a id="t-mortal-tend_home"></a>

#### Tend home

As head of your household or its head's spouse, you decide for it: improve your home, take on or let go a servant, apprentice a child to a master, take in a lodger, match an unmarried member with another household, sell a furnishing, or move the household to a richer place nearby. Your purse, your home and your people must allow it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Every named mortal belongs to a household that pools its income, pays for food, upkeep, tithes and taxes, cares for its weak and decides whom to marry, apprentice or where to move](interconnections.md#m-households).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Choice | What your household does |

<a id="p-mortal-demography"></a>

### Demography

8 tools: [assign caste](#t-mortal-assign_caste), [clone](#t-mortal-clone), [grow organ](#t-mortal-grow_organ), [raise sapience](#t-mortal-raise_sapience), [set clone law](#t-mortal-set_clone_law), [set population policy](#t-mortal-set_population_policy), [tend maimed](#t-mortal-tend_maimed), [use fertility means](#t-mortal-use_fertility_means).

<a id="t-mortal-assign_caste"></a>

#### Assign caste

As ruler, you set the caste of a mortal of your people. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Reproductive systems: two sexes, sequential, ambisexual, caste or budding peoples, read by births, marriage, growth and levies](interconnections.md#m-reproduction_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The mortal |
| Caste | The caste |

<a id="t-mortal-clone"></a>

#### Clone

You grow a clone of a body here, yours or another's: a new person with its own mind and history. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Clones are grown from a body, live shorter by generation, rise when held too low and become a people when many](interconnections.md#m-clones).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Source | Whose body is copied |

<a id="t-mortal-grow_organ"></a>

#### Grow organ

You grow a part for a body, yours or another's here. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Clones are grown from a body, live shorter by generation, rise when held too low and become a people when many](interconnections.md#m-clones).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Slot | Where it goes: part, mind, senses, skin, blood or whole_body |
| Kind | What it does: replacement, enhancement, interface, weapon, regulator, sense, storage, cosmetic, life_support or armour |
| Bearer | Whose body, or empty for your own |

<a id="t-mortal-raise_sapience"></a>

#### Raise sapience

You shape a line with a designed nervous system toward thought; strong enough, its kind rises as a people. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Clones are grown from a body, live shorter by generation, rise when held too low and become a people when many](interconnections.md#m-clones).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Line | The line |

<a id="t-mortal-set_clone_law"></a>

#### Set clone law

As ruler, you set whether clones may be made in your realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Clones are grown from a body, live shorter by generation, rise when held too low and become a people when many](interconnections.md#m-clones).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Level | Making clones is: banned, licensed or free |

<a id="t-mortal-set_population_policy"></a>

#### Set population policy

As the ruler of a realm, you set its policy on its numbers: an aim and the instruments that serve it, each with a strength. Instruments shift births, movement, health and grievance in the realm's towns; enforcing them costs the treasury, and harsh ones meet resistance. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Policies on numbers: aims and instruments that shift births, movement, health and grievance, paid for, resisted, reviewed and repealed](interconnections.md#m-population_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Aim | The aim: grow, hold, shrink, rebalance_age, rebalance_class, settle_land, empty_land, keep_kind, mix_kinds or none |
| Instruments | The instruments, each with a strength |
| Instruments › Instrument | The instrument: birth_reward, birth_tax, union_law, child_limit, child_support, inheritance_rule, settlement_grant, migration_permit, migration_ban, expulsion, resettlement, recruitment_abroad, health_campaign, faith_teaching, made_birth, immunisation or sanitation |
| Instruments › Strength | Its strength, from 0 to 100 |
| Target node | A people or place within your realm the policy aims at, or none for the whole realm |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-mortal-tend_maimed"></a>

#### Tend maimed

You help one person of your town who lives with a lasting harm back to work. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The maimed as a population](interconnections.md#m-maimed_cohorts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The harm |

<a id="t-mortal-use_fertility_means"></a>

#### Use fertility means

Take up a means your people know to prevent, seek or end conception. Where your people frown on it you use it in secret, and it may come to light. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fertility control: the means by which peoples prevent, seek or end conception, held open, hidden or forbidden](interconnections.md#m-fertility_means).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Means | The means you take up |

<a id="t-mortal-audit_endowment"></a>

#### Audit endowment

You audit an endowment. What its trustee kept for themselves comes to light; they lose honour and you take their place as trustee. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Endowments: wealth given to serve a beneficiary each epoch for its purpose, kept or skimmed by trustees, captured, confiscated or outliving its founder](interconnections.md#m-endowments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Endowment | The endowment you audit |

<a id="t-mortal-barter"></a>

#### Barter

You barter with a community or a named mortal: you give resources, items or vessels you own and take what it owns (if you want nothing it is a gift). The value of the offer, the relationship between you and a die decide acceptance; a successful barter warms the relationship. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community or mortal |
| Give | Things you own and will give (at most 3) |
| Take | Things it owns that you want (at most 3) |
| Terms | The barter's words |

<a id="t-mortal-build_ponds"></a>

#### Build ponds

Build fish ponds for your town by its river; they yield fish without taking from the wild, eat grain and are ruined by floods. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fishing and hunting grounds: gear and effort set the catch, worn grounds collapse, owned waters are poached and sea beasts wound](interconnections.md#m-grounds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | Your town |

You build a structure of one of your world's structure kinds. It can be dedicated to a god. A named mortal needs a craft skill of at least 10. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-mortal-buy_insurance"></a>

#### Buy insurance

You insure your life with an insurer of your town: you pay a premium each epoch, and when you die it pays your household. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Insurance and held rights: insurers pay lost cargoes, houses and lives; rights to songs, rites and designs pass, license and are taken](interconnections.md#m-insurance_rights).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Insurer | The insurer |

<a id="t-mortal-buy_office"></a>

#### Buy office

You buy an empty seat of your realm at the price of offices; the seat repays its buyer over the years and raises corruption. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The sale of offices and sinecures: seats bought for coin repay their buyers and breed corruption](interconnections.md#m-venality).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Office | The seat you buy |

<a id="t-mortal-buy_shares"></a>

#### Buy shares

You buy parts of a company for coin. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Companies gain influence where they hold things, take enclaves, may capture a realm's rule or answer to no realm](interconnections.md#m-company_power).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Company | The company |
| Share | How many of its hundred parts you buy |

<a id="t-mortal-buy_workshop"></a>

#### Buy workshop

You buy a workshop of your community from whoever owns it, paying its price from your wealth or treasury. Its profit (what it sells, less inputs, wages, upkeep and taxes) then comes to you, and so do its losses. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Every workshop and resource has an owner who takes its profit after inputs, wages, upkeep and taxes, and puts a share into the town](interconnections.md#m-site_ownership).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of workshop to buy |

<a id="t-mortal-change_gear"></a>

#### Change gear

Change the gear a ground of your land is worked with (lines, nets, weirs, traps, snares, harpoons, missiles, trawls, poison, hunting_animals, dredges, decoys); gear the ground does not allow is refused. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fishing and hunting grounds: gear and effort set the catch, worn grounds collapse, owned waters are poached and sea beasts wound](interconnections.md#m-grounds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Source | What the ground draws on |
| Region | The land of the ground |
| Gear | The new gear |

<a id="t-mortal-change_money"></a>

#### Change money

With the accounting of a money changer, you take up the changing of coin at your market and keep a share of the spread. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commerce practice: shops and their goods, tiers of supply and their cornering, promotions and their claims, leases and their rent](interconnections.md#m-commerce_practice) and [Exchange rates and money changers: rates between coins follow their purity, trust, supply and trade balance, changers profit from spreads, shocks swell debts in foreign coin, and bills of exchange let merchants pay without carrying coin](interconnections.md#m-exchange).

<a id="t-mortal-charter_company"></a>

#### Charter company

As ruler of a realm, you charter a company to work in it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Companies gain influence where they hold things, take enclaves, may capture a realm's rule or answer to no realm](interconnections.md#m-company_power).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Company | The company |

<a id="t-mortal-choose_method"></a>

#### Choose method

You choose how your community does one kind of work. A method needs its techniques, knowledge and energy; it changes output, quality, tool wear and pollution, and a method that needs fewer hands puts some out of work. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Each place does each work by one of several methods with their own inputs, hands, power and waste, chosen for profit under the owner's beliefs](interconnections.md#m-production_methods) and [Grain is milled, livestock butchered, milk made into cheese and fruit pressed into wine, each step a method, the raw stage spoiling](interconnections.md#m-food_chain).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Method | The method to take up |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_resource"></a>

#### Claim resource

You claim an ownerless resource on your land. If you take someone else's resource, your relation with its owner turns hostile. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Resource | The resource's id |

<a id="t-mortal-compose_account"></a>

#### Compose account

You tell a happening you know of in an account of your own, of any kind and stance; your people keep it as one telling among others, and it moves the standing of those it names. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Incident | The happening you tell |
| Kind | The kind of account |
| Stance | How it tells those who took part |

<a id="t-mortal-construct_language"></a>

#### Construct language

You make a tongue on purpose, with a regular grammar and no inherited history; your town begins to learn it, and it spreads where schools or law teach it. Needs skill in languages. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The tongue's name |
| Description | What the tongue is and why it was made |
| Words | Dictionary words: meaning, word class and origin |
| Words › Word | The word, in the language's own sounds |
| Words › Meaning | What it means |
| Words › Kind | Word class: noun, verb, adjective, affix, title, place_name, proper_name, number or interjection |
| Words › Origin | Etymology: from which event or which words it derived |
| Words › Example | Short usage example and its translation |

<a id="t-mortal-corner_market"></a>

#### Corner market

As leader of a merchant house you buy up a good in your home market to raise its price; a fair-market law or an association holding the good's monopoly undoes part of it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Market manipulation: rich merchant houses corner or flood markets, undone in part by fair-market laws and guild monopolies](interconnections.md#m-market_manipulation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commodity | The good |

<a id="t-mortal-create_culture"></a>

#### Create culture

You create a tradition of your community, a legend, a work of art (by honouring your god it strengthens that god) or a law (a just law reduces rebellion). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | tradition, legend, artwork or law |
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Subtype | What exactly it is |
| Description | What it is, how it came to be, what makes it distinctive and concrete |
| Related | Existing records it tells of (at most 4) |
| Law kind | For a law: what it rules, one of: succession_rule, inheritance_split, land_tenure, slavery, serfdom, tax_rate, tithe, tolls, debt_law, penal_code, levy_law, conscription, standing_army, veteran_pension, ransom_custom, marriage_law, sumptuary, guild_charter, foreigner_rights, religious_tolerance, worship_ban, censorship, blood_feud, quarantine, burial_law, sanitation, fair_market, magic_ban, licensed_magic, rite_restrictions, franchise, constitution, custom, labour_law, environment_law, data_privacy, weapons_law, intellectual_property, machine_law, space_law, closed_season, protected_waters, catch_quota, gear_ban, mind_status, copy_law, copy_succession, competition_law, erasure_law, augment_law, network_law, surveillance_law, provision_law, power_law, source_law, living_craft_law, clone_law, clone_status, germline_law, designed_status; required for laws |
| Law params | For a law: its terms by name, only those its kind has; leave out a term to keep its default. Terms of each kind: succession_rule: rule primogeniture/seniority/elective/tanistry/through_second_line/by_combat/divine_choice/ultimogeniture/designation/rotation, sex_rule any/begetter_preference/begetters_only/bearer_preference/bearers_only, wholeness_required true/false, min_age 0-200; inheritance_split: split eldest/equal/youngest; land_tenure: tenure feudal/communal/freehold; slavery: status allowed/banned/debt_only; serfdom: allowed true/false, labour_days 0-200; tax_rate: rate 0-100; tithe: rate 0-50, enforced true/false; tolls: rate 0-100; debt_law: remedy bondage/prison/jubilee, jubilee_epochs 1-20; penal_code: theft
