enforcer high_judge/treasurer/chancellor/minister/speaker |
| Image prompt | English image prompt: the tradition, legend, artwork or law as it appears in the world. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Legend category | Only for a legend: what it would become if belief made it real: creature, spirit, resource, relic |
| Archetype | Only for a legend: the kind of tale, which sets its lesson: hero_quest, monster, trickster, creation, world_disaster, betrayal, lost_golden_age, chosen_one, cursed_line, return_of_a_ruler, haunted_place, founding, exile, transformation, bargain, sacrifice, rivalry, discovery, deception, union, ascent, fall |
| Tradition kind | Only for a tradition: what kind of custom it is: festival, fast, rite_of_passage, taboo, offering, pilgrimage, hospitality, duel_custom, mourning, seasonal_rite, martial_display, body_marking, ancestral_rite, rest_custom, vigil, gift_custom, purification, commemoration, competition, fair, welcome, reconciliation, thanksgiving |
| Calendar slot | Only for a tradition kept at a set time: sowing, harvest, midwinter, campaign, holy_day, new_year, midsummer, remembrance, market, wild_harvest, rains, assembly, tax, pilgrimage |
| Taboo target | Only for a taboo: the good those who hold the taboo will not touch |
| Taboo act | Only for a taboo: the deed those who hold the taboo will not do: execution, mutilation, betrayal, mercy_to_enemy, sacrifice_of_people, trade_with_outsiders, building_temples, burning_books, conquest, raiding, duelling, ransom, taking_captives, oathbreaking, learning, hospitality, winter_fighting, guest_killing, ritual_scarring, killing_kin, killing_outsider, killing_in_war, killing_captive, regicide, duel_to_death, revenge_killing, theft, treason, cowardice, desecration, bribery, suicide, torture, eating_sapients, iconoclasm, conversion_by_force, polygamy, incest, magic_use, blood_magic, void_magic, necromancy, hoarding_in_famine, slavery, adultery, usury, infanticide, abandoning_elders, forced_labour, selling_offices, poaching, enclosing, lying, drunkenness, tomb_robbing, using_charms, feeding_on_hosts, killing_sacred_animal, fixing_contests, abandoning_the_sick, breaking_hospitality, cruelty_to_animals, apologising, sheltering_strangers, laying_curses, enhancing_contestants, poison_wells |

<a id="t-mortal-devise_use"></a>

#### Devise use

You devise a new way of keeping animals for a use, which herders can take up. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Tamed beasts become herds, domesticated kinds and named breeds; herds yield goods and mounts for armies](interconnections.md#m-domestication).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Use | What the animals are kept for |
| Name | The name of this way |
| Description | What this way is, in a sentence or two |

<a id="t-mortal-discover_resource"></a>

#### Discover resource

You discover a new resource on your land and own it. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Reaches** [Materials and what they do](interconnections.md#m-materials).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Subtype | What kind of resource it is |
| Description | What it is, how it came to be, what makes it distinctive and concrete |
| Location | Your own region or a place in your region |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Domain | One of: Sea, Fire, Earth, Sky, Storm, Death, Life, Dream, Time, Light, Shadow, War, Craft, Knowledge, Love, Trickery, Hunt, Plants, Bone, Blood, Sound, Stone, Ice, Madness, Trade, Road, Plague, Harvest, Moon, Sun, Star, Lies, Oath, Memory, Hunger, Glass, Salt, Ash, Border, Sleep, Technology, Machines, Cities, Industry, Information, Space, Science |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Commodity | What your people draw from it: grain, livestock, fish, fodder, timber, fuel, bread, ore, stone, cloth, tools, weapons, armour, mounts, siege_materiel, medicine, luxuries, incense, sacred_goods, magical_reagents, meat, milk, cheese, fruit, tubers, coal, peat, dung, fibre, dye, herbs, brew_base, poison, eggs, wool, oil, sugar, spice, halite, honey, wax, pottery, books, paper, glassware, soap, ink; ore goes to metalworking |
| Material | If it is a seam or a grove, its material: wood, calcified_tissue, hide, leather, cloth, stone, flint, obsidian, copper, tin, bronze, iron, steel, silver, gold, glass, crystal, ivory, chitin, great_beast_tissue, star_metal, void_glass, divine_tissue, living_wood, halite, amber, lead, skysteel, sulfur, clay, gems, mud_brick, fired_brick, dressed_stone, rubble, lime_mortar, hydraulic_concrete, thatch, tile, ceramic, silk, resin, shell, polymer, composite, rubber, paper, lacquer |
| Mechanics | The resource's particular rules, at most 2 (empty: read from its name and text): decaying: Rots in storage: its abundance drops each epoch and never regenerates. \| addictive: Communities that own it become addicted; losing it causes withdrawal. \| sentient: Chooses who mines it and makes demands; abandons owners who ignore them. \| depletion_price: When its last drop is drawn a curse falls on its land and doom approaches. \| fixed: A fixed amount in the cosmos that never regenerates. \| sacred: Belongs to a god who is angered when non-worshippers mine it. |

<a id="t-mortal-endow"></a>

#### Endow

You give part of your wealth as an endowment that serves a beneficiary every epoch for its purpose, and you keep it as its trustee. It raises your renown while it serves and goes on after you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Endowments: wealth given to serve a beneficiary each epoch for its purpose, kept or skimmed by trustees, captured, confiscated or outliving its founder](interconnections.md#m-endowments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Beneficiary | What or whom it serves: a town, a building, a school, a temple, a people or a being |
| Purpose | Its purpose: relief, learning, worship, health, works, art, kin, memory, research, hospitality or ransom |
| Capital | How much of your wealth you give to it |

<a id="t-mortal-establish_trade"></a>

#### Establish trade

You establish trade with a community nearby or in a neighbouring region; the prosperity of both of you increases. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community's id |
| Goods | What you give and what you take |

<a id="t-mortal-fabricate"></a>

#### Fabricate

You set more of your town's goods to be fabricated rather than made by hand. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Fabrication lowers scarcity and prices, moves workers out of work and pays dividends; patterns earn their holders fees](interconnections.md#m-abundance).

<a id="t-mortal-form_cartel"></a>

#### Form cartel

You bind sellers of one good in a market into a compact, for an organisation you head that sells there. The compact raises prices while its discipline holds; it weakens each epoch and breaks when cheating pays, and a law against cartels can expose and fine it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Market structure and competition: sellers and shares, concentration, barriers to entry, compacts, price wars and the law of fair dealing](interconnections.md#m-competition).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Member | The sellers who join, your own organisation among them |
| Goods kind | The good they agree on |
| Market | The market town |
| Aim | What they agree on: price, quota, territory or bid_rigging |
| Terms | The price or the quota they agree, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-found_company"></a>

#### Found company

You found a company in your home and hold all its shares. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Companies gain influence where they hold things, take enclaves, may capture a realm's rule or answer to no realm](interconnections.md#m-company_power).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name |

<a id="t-mortal-found_monetary_authority"></a>

#### Found monetary authority

As the ruler of a currency's issuer you set someone over it as its monetary authority, with an independence that decides how far rulers can press it to issue before wars and elections, and an aim. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Monetary authorities: rates, issuing for the state, pegs, reserves and the lender of last resort](interconnections.md#m-money_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Currency | The currency |
| Holder | Who holds the authority: a ruler, an office, a temple, a bank, a company or a council |
| Independence | How far it stands apart from rulers, from 0 to 100 |
| Aim | Its aim: stable_value, cheap_credit, fund_the_state, peg, growth or none |

<a id="t-mortal-found_settlement"></a>

#### Found settlement

You split off a branch of your population (or your disciples) and found a new settlement; those who migrate leave you. In your own region or a neighbouring region. You may say how it is settled: ground, floating, stilt, oasis, vent, habitat, band, deck, burrow, submerged, canopy, cliff, drift; each way asks its own ground and bodies, and sets how hard the settlement is to take and how much weather it bears. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** peoples, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Subtype | What kind of settlement it is |
| Description | What it is, how it came to be, what makes it distinctive and concrete |
| Location | Your own region or a neighbouring region |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |
| Mode | How it is settled; leave empty to settle as your people and the ground allow |

<a id="t-mortal-found_workshop"></a>

#### Found workshop

You found a workshop your community lacks and own it; it works its crafts from the next season, needing its inputs and energy. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Workshop designs drawn on a base kind change capacity, output, quality and storage](interconnections.md#m-workshop_designs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of workshop |

<a id="t-mortal-free_bound"></a>

#### Free bound

As ruler you free a share of the bound of a town of your realm (purchase, will, service, law, revolt); those freed by purchase or service rise as craftsfolk, the rest work the land. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Labour regimes: forced labour on great works, indenture and debt peonage, markets in bound folk, freedom by its paths and abolition](interconnections.md#m-labour_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Share | Share of its bound you free, 1 to 100 |
| Path | By what way they go free |

<a id="t-mortal-impose_script"></a>

#### Impose script

As a ruler whose own tongue is written, you replace by law the script of a tongue your subjects speak with your realm's script; its speakers resent it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The tongue whose script you replace |

<a id="t-mortal-insure"></a>

#### Insure

You buy cover from an insurer of your town against one kind of loss: cargo, house, life, crop, vessel, livestock, health or ransom; you pay a premium each epoch and it pays when the loss comes, if it can. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Insurance and held rights: insurers pay lost cargoes, houses and lives; rights to songs, rites and designs pass, license and are taken](interconnections.md#m-insurance_rights).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Insurer | The insurer |
| Cover | What the cover is against |

<a id="t-mortal-lay_corvee"></a>

#### Lay corvee

As ruler you summon a share of the workers of a town of your realm to forced labour on a great work being raised; the work goes faster, the harvest suffers, unrest rises and the town's grievance rises. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Labour regimes: forced labour on great works, indenture and debt peonage, markets in bound folk, freedom by its paths and abolition](interconnections.md#m-labour_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The town |
| Work | The work being raised |
| Share | Share of its workers, 0 to 100; 0 lifts the labour |

<a id="t-mortal-lease_out"></a>

#### Lease out

You lease land, a building, a vessel, an item or a beast you own to another for a rent each season and a term. If the lessee cannot pay, the lease ends in eviction, a debt or a case at court as you set. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commerce practice: shops and their goods, tiers of supply and their cornering, promotions and their claims, leases and their rent](interconnections.md#m-commerce_practice).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Thing | The thing you own |
| Lessee | Who takes it |
| Rent | The rent each season, from 0 to 100 |
| Term | How many epochs it runs, from 1 to 10 |
| On default | What follows an unpaid rent: eviction, court or debt |

<a id="t-mortal-lend"></a>

#### Lend

You lend to a community or a named mortal: the amount, what is lent and the term (how many ages until repayment). The amount is deducted from your wealth or prosperity and passes to it (if you have no wealth or prosperity you cannot lend; at most 30, term at most 12 ages). Nobody collects; but if it is not paid on time relations cool. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Only** a people that has grown to a tribe or beyond may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Debtor | The borrower |
| Amount | The debt amount (at most 30) |
| What | What is lent: a good, coin, labour or a service |
| Term ages | In how many ages it will be repaid (at most 12); if empty, 3 |

<a id="t-mortal-license_pattern"></a>

#### License pattern

You pay for the right to use a pattern. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Fabrication lowers scarcity and prices, moves workers out of work and pays dividends; patterns earn their holders fees](interconnections.md#m-abundance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Pattern | The pattern |

<a id="t-mortal-list_security"></a>

#### List security

You list claims on an issuer you rule or head on an exchange: shares in its results, or bonds, annuities or notes it pays interest on and repays when they fall due, or futures. The issuer pays the listing fee and holds every unit until it sells them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Securities and exchanges: listed claims, prices moved by results, rates, mood and herding, bubbles, crashes, margin calls and bonds](interconnections.md#m-securities).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Issuer | The issuer |
| Kind | What is listed: share, bond, annuity, note or future |
| Amount | How many units |
| Exchange | The exchange |

<a id="t-mortal-open_ground"></a>

#### Open ground

Open a ground for your people to catch from in the land you stand on (fishery, trapping, fowling, marine_hunt, game_hunt, shore_gathering, diving, fish_ponds, egg_gathering, insect_gathering), worked with gear it allows (lines, nets, weirs, traps, snares, harpoons, missiles, trawls, poison, hunting_animals, dredges, decoys). Shores and beds are worked from the land itself; land game and fowl from a kind of beast that lives there. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fishing and hunting grounds: gear and effort set the catch, worn grounds collapse, owned waters are poached and sea beasts wound](interconnections.md#m-grounds).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land you stand on |
| Ground | What you catch |
| Gear | What you catch it with |
| Kind | For game and fowl: the kind you hunt |

<a id="t-mortal-open_shop"></a>

#### Open shop

You open a shop in your town for the goods you name. It sells from its stock each season for its margin; a town with shops is supplied better than one without. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commerce practice: shops and their goods, tiers of supply and their cornering, promotions and their claims, leases and their rent](interconnections.md#m-commerce_practice).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Form | The form: stall, shop, house_trade, peddler, market_hall, warehouse_outlet, order_house or network_store |
| Goods kinds | The goods it carries |
| Structure | A building of your town it opens in, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-open_trading_post"></a>

#### Open trading post

As leader of a merchant house you open a trading post in a foreign town: it stores goods, gathers informants who learn its secrets and settles your folk there. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Trading posts: merchant houses open posts in foreign trading partners that store goods, gather informants and settle their folk](interconnections.md#m-trading_posts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Host | The foreign town |

<a id="t-mortal-plan_rebuilding"></a>

#### Plan rebuilding

You draw the plan for rebuilding your town after its ruin; a planned rebuilding costs more and takes longer, but it is built sturdier, with wider streets, and burns less. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Rebuilding after fire, flood, quake, storm or sack: planned in stone with wider streets, or hasty with the old risks; elites seize burned land](interconnections.md#m-reconstruction).

<a id="t-mortal-plant_perennial"></a>

#### Plant perennial

You plant a long-lived planting in your land (orchard, vineyard, grove, timber, plantation, forest_garden, coppice, hedgerow, palm_stand); it bears only after epochs, then yields you coin each epoch until war or neglect cuts it down. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Orchards, vineyards, groves and timber take epochs to bear, enrich their owners and are cut down in war](interconnections.md#m-perennials).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What you plant |
| Species | The plant you set, if any |

<a id="t-mortal-play_market"></a>

#### Play market

As leader of a merchant house you play your home market in a good: cornering it, dumping stock, or spreading a false report that moves its price until the truth arrives. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commodity | The good |
| Play | How you play it |

<a id="t-mortal-produce_item"></a>

#### Produce item

You produce an item from resources you own or that lie on your land. The resources' abundance decreases; quality depends on your craft and the resources' might. A named mortal needs a craft skill of at least 10. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Every item, structure and vessel is made of a material its makers can work and hold](interconnections.md#m-made_materials), [Materials and what they do](interconnections.md#m-materials) and [Skills, callings and teaching](interconnections.md#m-skills).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Category | weapon, armour, shield, tool, jewellery, sacred relic, instrument or book |
| Subtype | What kind of item it is |
| Description | What it is, how it came to be, what makes it distinctive and concrete |
| Resource | The resources it is made from (at most 3) |
| Material | What it is made of: wood, calcified_tissue, hide, leather, cloth, stone, flint, obsidian, copper, tin, bronze, iron, steel, silver, gold, glass, crystal, ivory, chitin, great_beast_tissue, star_metal, void_glass, divine_tissue, living_wood, halite, amber, lead, skysteel, sulfur, clay, gems, mud_brick, fired_brick, dressed_stone, rubble, lime_mortar, hydraulic_concrete, thatch, tile, ceramic, silk, resin, shell, polymer, composite, rubber, paper, lacquer; it must come from one of those resources or your people's stores, and your people must be able to work it |
| Owner | Who will carry the item (a mortal or you); if empty, you |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-mortal-promote"></a>

#### Promote

You spread word of your wares or brand. Reach raises a brand's reputation each season at a cost; claims that are not true may be found out, costing reputation and honour, and a fine where fair dealing is law. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commerce practice: shops and their goods, tiers of supply and their cornering, promotions and their claims, leases and their rent](interconnections.md#m-commerce_practice).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Medium | The medium: crier, sign, display, patronage, gift or media |
| Claim accuracy | How true the claims are, from 0 to 100 |
| Reach | How far the word goes, from 0 to 100 |
| Commodity | The good it speaks of, or none |
| Brand | Your brand it speaks of, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-repay_debt"></a>

#### Repay debt

You repay your debt to someone: if you have wealth or prosperity the debt amount passes to them (otherwise you repay in kind); the debt closes and relations warm (even if overdue). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Creditor | Who you owe |

<a id="t-mortal-replan_land"></a>

#### Replan land

As ruler, you set what the land around your town is used for. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Rings of land use around each market: gardens, fuel wood, grain and herds by the cost of carrying goods, stretched by rivers, roads and canals](interconnections.md#m-thunen).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Ring | What the land is given over to |

<a id="t-mortal-report_cartel"></a>

#### Report cartel

You tell of a compact between sellers. Where the law forbids cartels it is broken and its members are fined; where it does not, it is no longer secret. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Market structure and competition: sellers and shares, concentration, barriers to entry, compacts, price wars and the law of fair dealing](interconnections.md#m-competition).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cartel | The compact you tell of |

<a id="t-mortal-reschedule_loan"></a>

#### Reschedule loan

As a lender you put off the due date of a loan you hold, giving its borrower more time. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Loan | The loan |
| Extra epochs | Epochs to put it off by |

<a id="t-mortal-seize_hoards"></a>

#### Seize hoards

As ruler you seize every hoard held back in a market of your realm, returning its goods to sale; prices fall and the merchants resent it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The market |

<a id="t-mortal-sell_loan"></a>

#### Sell loan

As a lender you sell a loan you hold to another at a discount; they collect it from then on. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Lenders rate borrowers by repayment, treasury and stability; credit runs sound, strained, in default and restructuring, and reserves back coin](interconnections.md#m-credit).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Loan | The loan |
| Buyer | Who buys it |

<a id="t-mortal-send_convoy"></a>

#### Send convoy

As leader of a merchant house you send a convoy along a route from your town; it may be raided, lost or delayed, and brings goods, migrants and missionaries when it arrives. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Convoys: routes carry their goods in convoys with guards, migrants and missionaries, raided by bandits and beasts, lost at sea or delayed by weather](interconnections.md#m-convoys).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Route | The trade route |

<a id="t-mortal-set_levy"></a>

#### Set levy

As ruler, you set a tax of your realm and its rate. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Corruption grows from overreach, injustice, greed and tax farming and skims taxes; each kind of tax falls on its classes](interconnections.md#m-corruption_and_taxes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The tax |
| Rate | Its rate, out of 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-set_monetary_policy"></a>

#### Set monetary policy

You set the policy of a monetary authority you hold or rule: its rate and its aim. Lenders take the rate as their base a season later; a low rate raises borrowing and prices, a high rate the reverse. Issuing to fund the state fills the treasury and lowers trust; a peg holds the currency to another currency or a good while reserves last. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Monetary authorities: rates, issuing for the state, pegs, reserves and the lender of last resort](interconnections.md#m-money_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Authority | The authority |
| Rate | Its policy rate, from 0 to 100 |
| Aim | Its aim: stable_value, cheap_credit, fund_the_state, peg, growth or none |
| Peg currency | The currency to hold it to, for a peg |
| Peg goods kind | The good to hold it to, for a peg, instead of a currency |

<a id="t-mortal-set_provision"></a>

#### Set provision

As ruler, you set how your realm provides for its people once goods can be fabricated: by market, ration, for all, by status, by standing or in common. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Fabrication lowers scarcity and prices, moves workers out of work and pays dividends; patterns earn their holders fees](interconnections.md#m-abundance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Level | How goods are given out |

<a id="t-mortal-speculate"></a>

#### Speculate

You buy up a share of a good in your market and hold it back, hoping its price rises. You sell when it does, or after a few epochs; if prices fall you may be ruined. Hoarding food while people go hungry is judged by those who see it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Greedy merchants buy up rising goods and hoard food in famine; booms enrich them and busts ruin them](interconnections.md#m-speculation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Commodity | The good to buy up |

<a id="t-mortal-start_price_war"></a>

#### Start price war

An organisation you head sells a good below its usual price in a market to drive its rivals out. It pays for the cut each epoch from its treasury, and its rivals lose too; whoever runs out first loses, and beaten rivals go before the court for their debts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Market structure and competition: sellers and shares, concentration, barriers to entry, compacts, price wars and the law of fair dealing](interconnections.md#m-competition).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Market | The market town |
| Goods kind | The good |
| Cut | How far below the usual price you sell, in a hundred |

<a id="t-mortal-trade_security"></a>

#### Trade security

You buy a listed claim from its largest holder or sell yours back to its issuer at the price of the exchange. You may buy partly on credit up to the exchange's margin; if the price falls far, what you hold on credit is sold off and you owe the rest. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Securities and exchanges: listed claims, prices moved by results, rates, mood and herding, bubbles, crashes, margin calls and bonds](interconnections.md#m-securities).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Security | The listed claim |
| Amount | How many units |
| Side | buy or sell |

<a id="t-mortal-abandon_work"></a>

#### Abandon work

As one who rules a settlement, you call the builders away from a work still being raised there; it stands unfinished. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure | The work |

<a id="t-mortal-accept_treaty"></a>

#### Accept treaty

You accept a treaty offered to you; it takes force at once. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Treaty | The offered treaty |

<a id="t-mortal-appoint_court"></a>

#### Appoint court

As ruler or regent you seat a mortal of your court in one of its places (steward, champion, physician, tutor, chronicler, master_of_revels, jester, seer, taster, interpreter); each place serves the ruler in its own way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [A ruler's court gathers courtiers into positions and shines with grandeur that wins renown, a warm welcome abroad and talent](interconnections.md#m-court).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Position | The place at court |
| Mortal | Who takes it |

<a id="t-mortal-appoint_officer"></a>

#### Appoint officer

As ruler or regent you fill a seat of your realm (chancellor, marshal, treasurer, high_judge, spymaster, governor, ambassador) with a living named mortal, or leave it empty. Free, 2 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 2 times an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Civics gate governments, offices, laws, taxes, privileges and creeds: what a realm has not learned it cannot found](interconnections.md#m-civic_gates).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Office | The office to fill |
| Mortal | The mortal who takes the seat; empty to dismiss the holder |

<a id="t-mortal-assign_army_role"></a>

#### Assign army role

You give someone near you a place on the staff of an army you command or own. Roles: commander, sub_commander, quartermaster, standard_bearer, surgeon, chaplain, paymaster, scout_master, engineer_master, interpreter. A chaplain keeps its rites and buries its dead, a paymaster holds its chest and, if honest, keeps it from mutiny, a master of scouts spares it ambushes. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Commanders and sub-commanders as army offices; the marshal's and ruler's traits choose strategy](interconnections.md#m-army_command).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Army | The army |
| Mortal | Who takes the place |
| Role | The place on the staff |

<a id="t-mortal-assign_task"></a>

#### Assign task

As ruler or regent you set an officer of your council to a task: improve_relations, train_troops, collect_taxes, disrupt_schemes, convert_province, purge_corruption, oversee_works, promote_trade, advance_learning, public_health, secure_borders. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Each officer works a council task scaled by skill and by regard for the ruler, and a disloyal spymaster leaks](interconnections.md#m-council_tasks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Office | The office |
| Task | The task |
| Target | The realm or land the task aims at, if any |

<a id="t-mortal-back_crisis"></a>

#### Back crisis

As ruler or regent you put your realm behind one side of a crisis between other realms; backers weigh on who yields and join the war if it comes. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [A demand to cede land, release a vassal, convert or pay opens a crisis; backers weigh in until a side yields or it becomes war](interconnections.md#m-crises).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Crisis | The crisis |
| Side | Which side you back: demander or target |

<a id="t-mortal-bargain_with_estate"></a>

#### Bargain with estate

As the ruler of a realm, you grant one of its estates a privilege (tax_exemption, monopoly, council_seat, arms, judicial_immunity, reserved_offices, land_holding, service_exemption, dress) to win its loyalty, or take one back at a cost in loyalty. At most 2 bargains an age. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Civics gate governments, offices, laws, taxes, privileges and creeds: what a realm has not learned it cannot found](interconnections.md#m-civic_gates) and [The classes of a realm gather as estates with a loyalty and a share of its power; privileges buy loyalty at a cost in taxes, justice or power](interconnections.md#m-estates).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Estate | The estate |
| Privilege | The privilege |
| Grant | True to grant it, false to take it back |

<a id="t-mortal-begin_realm_plan"></a>

#### Begin realm plan

As ruler you set your realm on a long plan (ecological_transformation, bloodline, conversion, wonder, forecast, network, megastructure, programme, realm, line, reform, exploration, restoration) paid from its treasury each epoch; it outlives you and passes to whoever rules after. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Long plans: ecological transformations, bloodlines, conversions and wonders held by realms, orders and gods, carried past their authors](interconnections.md#m-long_plans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What the plan aims at |
| Name | What the plan is called |
| Target | The land, world, house, people or town it aims at |
| Steps | Steps it needs, 1 to 100 |
| Secrecy | How secret it is kept, 0 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-begin_transition"></a>

#### Begin transition

As ruler of a realm, you proclaim a change of its economic order; holdings move in stages to their new hands, and those who lose them resist unless they are paid. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Economic orders: a realm's ownership, allocation, labour and distribution move goods, favour classes and change in stages against their losers](interconnections.md#m-economic_orders).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm you rule |
| Sector | The good whose order changes, or empty for the whole realm |
| Kind | The change: collectivisation, nationalisation, privatisation, land_reform, enclosure, emancipation, decollectivisation or restoration_of_holdings |
| Compensation | What the losers are paid: none, partial or full |
| Reason | Why you change it |

<a id="t-mortal-begin_works"></a>

#### Begin works

As the ruler of a realm, you begin public works on a route, region or town of your realm, setting a budget aside from the treasury. They advance a stage each season their labour is paid or pressed; paid labour costs the treasury, corvée and bound labour raise grievance, and finished works change roads, bridges, water, walls, stores, harbours or prosperity. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The works: road, bridge, canal, rail_line, aqueduct, dam, drainage, harbour_works, walls, granary_system, sewer, power_line, network_line, terraforming_stage, megastructure_stage, grown_works, lighthouse, terracing, irrigation or tunnel |
| Route or region | The route, region or town they lie on |
| Labour | The labour: paid, corvee, bound, made_minds or constructs |
| Budget | What the treasury sets aside for them |

<a id="t-mortal-break_treaty"></a>

#### Break treaty

You break a treaty your people are bound by: the other parties' trust in you falls, guarantors may march and your rulers lose honour. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Treaty | The treaty you break |

<a id="t-mortal-call_referendum"></a>

#### Call referendum

As the ruler of a realm, town or league you put a question to its people, voted by those its law lets vote. A binding vote changes the law or the state at once; a vote that does not bind presses the ruler, who loses legitimacy by ignoring it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Referendums: questions put to the people, called by right, campaigned, held, found out and carried or ignored](interconnections.md#m-referendums).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm, town or league whose people vote |
| Subject | What is asked: law, constitution, independence, union, treaty, war, transition, recall or faith |
| Question target | What the question is about: the law, the town that would leave, the realm to join, the treaty, the foe, the office holder or the faith |
| Binding | Whether the result binds |

<a id="t-mortal-campaign_for_vote"></a>

#### Campaign for vote

You campaign for one side of a vote still to be held: with money, through an outlet you own, by a speech you gave that was heard, or by fraud, which may be found out and void the vote. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Referendums: questions put to the people, called by right, campaigned, held, found out and carried or ignored](interconnections.md#m-referendums).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Referendum | The vote |
| Side | yes or no |
| Means | money, media, speech or fraud |

<a id="t-mortal-cast_rite"></a>

#### Cast rite

You work a rite you know. It must be lawful where it is worked, its requirements met and its price paid; it may backfire. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Rite | The rite to work (a rite id) |
| Target | Whom or where it is aimed at, if anyone |
| Vessel | Ignored: you work it yourself |

<a id="t-mortal-commission_artwork"></a>

#### Commission artwork

You commission a work of art. It moves the views of those who hold it, raises the standing of whom it portrays, and a realm or town pays for it from its treasury. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults, factions and polities.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for it, in the world's naming language |
| Medium | What kind of work: verbal, musical, movement, flat_image, solid_image, built, worn, culinary, scent, light, recorded, interactive, landscape, body, ritual_performance, textile_image, made_mind_art, tactile, mind; a performed work lasts only while someone can perform it |
| Subject | Whom or what it portrays (an entity id), if anyone |
| Author | The artist who makes it (a mortal id), if any; a skilled hand makes a finer work |
| Patron | Who commissions and keeps it (a community id); you if empty |

<a id="t-mortal-create_office"></a>

#### Create office

As a ruler you make a new office in your realm, paid from its treasury, to hold those who want a place. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Office creation: realms make offices for rising elites at a cost and abolish them when the treasury runs short, leaving their holders aggrieved](interconnections.md#m-office_creation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of office |
| Title | Its title, if it has one of its own |

<a id="t-mortal-declare_neutral"></a>

#### Declare neutral

As ruler or regent you declare your realm neutral: it leaves its alliances, joins none, and trades with every side of the wars around it; whoever makes war on it alarms every realm of its world. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Conquest, wars without a claim and broken treaties raise threat; realms that fear the same aggressor band into a coalition](interconnections.md#m-threat_and_coalitions).

<a id="t-mortal-designate_heir"></a>

#### Designate heir

As ruler you name your heir from among any living mortal; under a law of designation your choice stands, though blood kin may contest it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Patrons and clients: favours and appointments bind clients to patrons, and weak institutions let patronage decide offices and votes](interconnections.md#m-clientelism).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Heir | The heir |

<a id="t-mortal-devise_custom"></a>

#### Devise custom

You devise a new kind of technique, tenet, tradition, tale, item or working, built on one your world knows; the result names the bases you can build on if yours is not one. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Catalog | Which kind of thing it is |
| Base | What it is built on |
| Name | Its name |
| Description | What it is, in a sentence or two |

<a id="t-mortal-diplomacy"></a>

#### Diplomacy

Diplomacy with another community: alliance (stands with you in war), peace (tries to end enmity), tribute (demands wealth from the weak) or marriage (unites the lineages). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community's id |
| Kind | alliance, peace, tribute or marriage |
| Terms | Your terms |

<a id="t-mortal-dismantle_work"></a>

#### Dismantle work

As one who rules a settlement, you take a work there apart; a share of what it cost goes to new building and little is left. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure | The work |

<a id="t-mortal-draw_blueprint"></a>

#### Draw blueprint

You draw a design that others of your craft can build from. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What sort of design it is |
| Subject | What it is a design of |

<a id="t-mortal-drill_trait"></a>

#### Drill trait

As commander of an army, you school yourself in a way of command. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Trait | The way of command |

<a id="t-mortal-ease_pressure"></a>

#### Ease pressure

You ease one of your pressures. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Pressure | The pressure to ease |

<a id="t-mortal-enact_law"></a>

#### Enact law

As ruler or regent you lay down a typed law for your whole realm: its kind decides what it rules and its terms decide how. In a realm that decides by council, assembly or divine will, the law goes before them as a proposal instead of passing at once. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Civics gate governments, offices, laws, taxes, privileges and creeds: what a realm has not learned it cannot found](interconnections.md#m-civic_gates) and [Laws pass as proposals that move by the weight of voices for and against them; estates and hooks stall or carry them](interconnections.md#m-enactment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What the law rules; one of: succession_rule, inheritance_split, land_tenure, slavery, serfdom, tax_rate, tithe, tolls, debt_law, penal_code, levy_law, conscription, standing_army, veteran_pension, ransom_custom, marriage_law, sumptuary, guild_charter, foreigner_rights, religious_tolerance, worship_ban, censorship, blood_feud, quarantine, burial_law, sanitation, fair_market, magic_ban, licensed_magic, rite_restrictions, franchise, constitution, custom, labour_law, environment_law, data_privacy, weapons_law, intellectual_property, machine_law, space_law, closed_season, protected_waters, catch_quota, gear_ban, mind_status, copy_law, copy_succession, competition_law, erasure_law, augment_law, network_law, surveillance_law, provision_law, power_law, source_law, living_craft_law, clone_law, clone_status, germline_law, designed_status |
| Params | The law's terms by name, only those its kind has; leave out a term to keep its default. Terms of each kind: succession_rule: rule primogeniture/seniority/elective/tanistry/through_second_line/by_combat/divine_choice/ultimogeniture/designation/rotation, sex_rule any/begetter_preference/begetters_only/bearer_preference/bearers_only, wholeness_required true/false, min_age 0-200; inheritance_split: split eldest/equal/youngest; land_tenure: tenure feudal/communal/freehold; slavery: status allowed/banned/debt_only; serfdom: allowed true/false, labour_days 0-200; tax_rate: rate 0-100; tithe: rate 0-50, enforced true/false; tolls: rate 0-100; debt_law: remedy bondage/prison/jubilee, jubilee_epochs 1-20; penal_code: theft
