enforcer high_judge/treasurer/chancellor/minister/speaker |
| Name | The law's name, if it should have one of its own |

<a id="t-mortal-face_fear"></a>

#### Face fear

You face one of your fears; it weakens, and may leave you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Fear | The fear |

<a id="t-mortal-flee"></a>

#### Flee

If you are hunted, or bound and would run, you flee to a refuge that will take you in where you are: a temple, a host, an embassy, a city whose charter frees those who stay, or the wilds. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Sanctuary | The kind of refuge you seek, or leave it empty to take the first that will have you |

<a id="t-mortal-found_enterprise"></a>

#### Found enterprise

As the ruler of a realm, you found a state enterprise: it earns for the treasury each season, a monopoly earns more and starves rival shops, and a corrupt manager may skim it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Sector | The good it deals in, or none |
| Form | The form: monopoly, royal_works, state_company, licence_holder, arsenal, mint, granary_board or post |
| Manager | Who manages it: an official, a company or a made mind, or none |

<a id="t-mortal-found_league"></a>

#### Found league

As the ruler of a realm you found a standing league with other realms at peace with each other. Members pay dues, vote by the basis you choose and are bound by what passes; the aims decide its organs and what it does for its members. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organisations of polities: leagues with charters, dues, votes and vetoes, sanctions, courts, common force, shared money and federation](interconnections.md#m-leagues).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The founding realms, yours among them |
| Aims | What it is for: defence, trade, customs_union, currency_union, law, faith, peace, research or migration |
| Vote basis | How it decides: one_member_one_vote, by_population, by_dues or by_veto_holders |

<a id="t-mortal-found_school"></a>

#### Found school

You found a school of thought, a creed that spreads from person to person and town to town, and can move a realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Civics gate governments, offices, laws, taxes, privileges and creeds: what a realm has not learned it cannot found](interconnections.md#m-civic_gates).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for the school or creed |
| Tenets | What it teaches, 1 to 3 tenets: legalism, restraint, egalitarianism, divine_right, mercantilism, militarism, asceticism, rationalism, nature_harmony, xenophobia, cosmopolitanism, anti_god, creatorism |
| Founder | Ignored: you found it yourself |

<a id="t-mortal-fund_works"></a>

#### Fund works

You give from your own wealth to public works being built. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Public works built stage by stage by paid, pressed or made labour, kept up or left to decay; state enterprises that earn, skim and are sold](interconnections.md#m-public_works).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Works | The works |
| Amount | How much you give |

<a id="t-mortal-give_relief"></a>

#### Give relief

As the ruler of a realm you set what relief it gives and what it spends on it each epoch; as anyone else, you give coin to the relief it already gives. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Relief diplomacy: realms and cults send goods, healers and labour to stricken towns for gratitude, favours, gifts owed and love for their god](interconnections.md#m-relief).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of relief |
| Budget | The coin it costs each epoch, or that you give |

<a id="t-mortal-hunt_fugitive"></a>

#### Hunt fugitive

You hunt down a fugitive your realm pursues; those in sanctuary are out of your reach. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Fugitive | The fugitive |

<a id="t-mortal-issue_demand"></a>

#### Issue demand

As ruler or regent you demand something of another realm: cede_region, release_vassal, convert, pay, hand_over, disarm, open_trade, apology, release_captives. A person to hand over is named as the subject. A crisis opens: other realms back a side, the weaker side backs down or concedes, and if neither yields it becomes war. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [A demand to cede land, release a vassal, convert or pay opens a crisis; backers weigh in until a side yields or it becomes war](interconnections.md#m-crises).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The realm you make the demand of |
| Demand | What you demand: cede_region, release_vassal, convert, pay, hand_over, disarm, open_trade, apology or release_captives |
| Subject | The land to cede, the vassal to release or the person to hand over, if the demand names one |

<a id="t-mortal-issue_mandate"></a>

#### Issue mandate

As ruler or regent you issue a mandate: produce, export_ban, build, famine_relief, curfew, slum_clearance, prepare, enclosure, ration, corvee, quarantine, conscription. Unmet mandates cost you legitimacy, and harsh ones weigh on your people. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Rulers issue mandates, from production orders to famine relief from the state granaries, and nobles hold demands](interconnections.md#m-mandates).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | produce, export_ban, build, famine_relief, curfew, slum_clearance, prepare, enclosure, ration, corvee, quarantine or conscription |
| Settlement | The settlement it is laid on, if any |
| Commodity | The good to produce, ban or send |
| District | The quarter to build |
| Amount | How much to produce or send |

<a id="t-mortal-join_league"></a>

#### Join league

As the ruler of a realm you ask a league to take it in; its assembly votes at once. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organisations of polities: leagues with charters, dues, votes and vetoes, sanctions, courts, common force, shared money and federation](interconnections.md#m-leagues).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| League | The league |
| Polity | Your realm |

<a id="t-mortal-lay_trade_policy"></a>

#### Lay trade policy

You lay or change a trade policy of the realm you rule: a tariff on a good (raising its price and your treasury, guarding your own makers), a subsidy on a good your people make (paid from your treasury), or an embargo on a partner or a good (smugglers may run it). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Realms lay tariffs, subsidies and embargoes by good and partner; smugglers run bans and embargoes through outlaws](interconnections.md#m-trade_policies).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | Tariff, subsidy or embargo |
| Commodity | The good, or none for every good |
| Partner | The realm or market it is aimed at, or none for all |
| Rate pct | The rate for a tariff or subsidy |

<a id="t-mortal-leave_office"></a>

#### Leave office

You leave an office of a realm you hold, or the headship of an organisation, by your own choice, handing it to one you name or to the rule of succession, on the conditions you ask. One who leaves keeps some standing and may come back while the office stands empty or weak. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Leaving office: abdication, retirement, resignation, withdrawal and exile by choice, with pensions, rallying points and returns](interconnections.md#m-departures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Office | The office of a realm you hold, if you leave one |
| Org | The organisation you head, if you leave its headship |
| Kind | How you leave: abdication, retirement, resignation, withdrawal_to_religion or exile_by_choice |
| In favour of | Whom you hand it to, or none to leave it to succession |
| Conditions | What you keep: keep_title, keep_lands, pension or safe_conduct |

<a id="t-mortal-mediate"></a>

#### Mediate

As ruler or regent you offer your realm as mediator: of a treaty in force between other realms, which then holds your standing on it and lets you settle disputes under it, or of an open crisis between two other realms, which you settle by terms both accept if both trust you. Name either a treaty or a crisis. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Treaties: clauses executed, breaches, guarantors, renewal and shared wars](interconnections.md#m-treaties).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Treaty | The treaty you stand behind |
| Crisis | The crisis you settle |

<a id="t-mortal-open_district"></a>

#### Open district

As one who rules a settlement, you open or enlarge a district of it of any of the kinds its world knows; each kind changes what the city stores, houses, keeps in order and pays for. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement you rule |
| Kind | The kind of district |
| Material | What it is built of |

<a id="t-mortal-press_claim"></a>

#### Press claim

You press a claim you hold to rule a realm, named by its basis (inheritance, marriage, conquest, divine, fabricated, de_jure, election, adoption, prophecy); a pressed claim grows stronger and may be fought for. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The realm |
| Basis | The basis of your claim |

<a id="t-mortal-press_demand"></a>

#### Press demand

As one of noble birth, you press a demand on your ruler. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Want | What you demand |

<a id="t-mortal-proclaim_age"></a>

#### Proclaim age

As ruler, you proclaim what age your realm lives in. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Deeds give a realm era score: golden, dark and heroic ages follow; a world enters the next era when its leaders know enough of it](interconnections.md#m-eras).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Age | The age proclaimed |

<a id="t-mortal-proclaim_tongue"></a>

#### Proclaim tongue

As ruler of a realm you set a policy on a tongue across it (official, liturgical, suppressed, revival, protected, standardised, bilingual): an official tongue spreads among your subjects, a suppressed one fades but its speakers lose cohesion and grow restless, a revived one is taught again. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Language policy: realms and faiths make tongues official or sacred, suppress them or revive them](interconnections.md#m-language_policy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The tongue |
| Policy | The policy |

<a id="t-mortal-promote_cult"></a>

#### Promote cult

You promote the cult of yourself, or of the ruler of the realm you live or serve in, by instruments. The cult lends the ruler legitimacy and quiets dissent at a cost to the treasury and to the public's sincerity, which falls when the cult runs ahead of the realm's results; a ban on criticism punishes mockery. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cults of living leaders: instruments, legitimacy and sincerity, banned criticism, and the cult's fate at the leader's death](interconnections.md#m-leader_cults).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Leader | The leader |
| Instruments | The instruments: images_in_public, official_biography, holidays, oaths_of_loyalty, naming_of_places, teaching_in_schools, staged_gatherings or ban_on_criticism |

<a id="t-mortal-propose_treaty"></a>

#### Propose treaty

You offer a typed treaty to another community or realm; it takes force when they accept. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community or realm you offer it to |
| Kind | peace, truce, alliance_defensive, alliance_offensive, non_aggression, vassalage (you become the vassal), tribute, marriage_pact, trade_pact, condotta, guarantee, interworld, coalition, divine_pact, naval_limit or arms_limit |
| Clauses | Typed clauses with who owes, who gains and their terms by name, as each clause kind requires |
| Epochs | How many ages it should hold |
| Clauses › Kind | The clause's kind |
| Clauses › Obligor | Who owes it |
| Clauses › Beneficiary | Who gains by it |
| Clauses › Params | Its terms by name, as the clause kind requires |

<a id="t-mortal-purge_circle"></a>

#### Purge circle

As ruler you cast a member of your ruling circle out of the circle and of every seat they hold. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Selectorate and winning coalition: who chooses the ruler sets the spending mix between public goods and private rewards](interconnections.md#m-selectorate).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The member you purge |

<a id="t-mortal-raise_movement"></a>

#### Raise movement

As leader of a faction, you turn it into a movement for a cause, or renew its cause; it gains support. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Movements for abolition, independence, revival, land reform or a wider franchise grow from pressures, protest, become parties where there is a council or assembly and rise where there is none](interconnections.md#m-movements).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cause | What the movement stands for |

<a id="t-mortal-raise_restoration"></a>

#### Raise restoration

From the old lands of a fallen realm, or by your claim to it, you begin a movement to raise it again under its old name, laws and rights. Its memory fades unless it is kept, grows with grievance and rises when strong enough. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Restoration of fallen realms: memory, movements, risings, and the old realm raised again under its name, laws and rights](interconnections.md#m-restoration).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Fallen polity | The fallen realm |
| Bases | The grounds: bloodline, faith, law, memory or foreign_backing |
| Territory | The old lands it seeks; empty seeks them all |

<a id="t-mortal-rebuild"></a>

#### Rebuild

As one who rules a settlement that waits to be rebuilt after a disaster, you choose how: planned, as it comes, on a safer site nearby, or not at all, its people leaving. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The settlement you rule |
| Plan | How it is rebuilt |

<a id="t-mortal-repudiate_debts"></a>

#### Repudiate debts

As ruler you declare your realm's debts void; its lenders lose them, your realm is shut out of credit for years, and the lenders' realms may take it as cause for war. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Banks lend to realms at war or in debt; realms repay from the treasury or default into standing debt](interconnections.md#m-realm_loans).

<a id="t-mortal-rescue_polity"></a>

#### Rescue polity

As ruler you carry another realm's debts on terms: it keeps paying under your terms, gives you that share of its treasury each epoch, and owes you standing. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Banks lend to realms at war or in debt; realms repay from the treasury or default into standing debt](interconnections.md#m-realm_loans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm you rescue |
| Terms | Share of its treasury it pays you each epoch, out of 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-restore_polity"></a>

#### Restore polity

As the leader of a restoration whose memory is strong enough, you raise the fallen realm again, unless the realms that hold its old lands are steady enough to put the rising down. Old laws that no longer fit cost the new realm stability. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Restoration of fallen realms: memory, movements, risings, and the old realm raised again under its name, laws and rights](interconnections.md#m-restoration).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Restoration | The restoration |

<a id="t-mortal-return_to_office"></a>

#### Return to office

You come back to an office you left, while it stands empty or weak; one who retired does not come back. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Leaving office: abdication, retirement, resignation, withdrawal and exile by choice, with pensions, rallying points and returns](interconnections.md#m-departures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Departure | The leaving you take back |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_citizenship"></a>

#### Seek citizenship

As a foreigner in your realm, you pay to become its citizen and gain the rights citizens hold. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Civics: evidence before the courts, citizenship and its rights, borders on trade, poor relief and standard measures](interconnections.md#m-civics).

<a id="t-mortal-seek_patron"></a>

#### Seek patron

You become the client of a patron; where institutions are weak, patrons place their clients in office and sway votes for them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Patrons and clients: favours and appointments bind clients to patrons, and weak institutions let patronage decide offices and votes](interconnections.md#m-clientelism).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Patron | The patron you seek |

<a id="t-mortal-seize_title"></a>

#### Seize title

As the ruler of a realm, you take up a title whose lands your realm mostly holds by right and whose seat you hold: you usurp it from its holder, at a cost in legitimacy, or restore it if it lies dormant. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Titles from local to imperial tiers rule their de jure lands by right; the ruler of a realm holds its primary title, lands held long under another title drift into it, and realms claim, usurp, create and restore titles](interconnections.md#m-titles).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Title | The title to take |

<a id="t-mortal-send_aid"></a>

#### Send aid

As ruler you send aid to a town (goods, healers, labour, money, credit, food, shelter, medicine, soldiers, knowledge, transport); the town owes your realm a favour and a gift in return and its folk love your god more, but a rival may turn the aid away. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Relief diplomacy: realms and cults send goods, healers and labour to stricken towns for gratitude, favours, gifts owed and love for their god](interconnections.md#m-relief).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Settlement | The town you aid |
| Kind | What you send |

<a id="t-mortal-set_economic_order"></a>

#### Set economic order

As ruler of a realm, you set its economic order, or that of one good it makes: who owns, how goods are allotted, how labour is bound and how the product is shared. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Economic orders: a realm's ownership, allocation, labour and distribution move goods, favour classes and change in stages against their losers](interconnections.md#m-economic_orders).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | The realm you rule |
| Sector | The good whose order you set, or empty for the whole realm |
| Ownership | Who owns land and works: household, kin_group, community, temple, ruler, state, private, cooperative, company, common or none |
| Allocation | How goods are allotted: market, redistribution, command, obligation, gift, need, abundance or lottery |
| Labour | How labour is bound: free, kin, corvee, serf, bound, enslaved, conscripted, cooperative or made |
| Distribution | How the product is shared: by_holding, by_office, by_need, by_work, by_share, equal or by_prestige |
| Reason | Why you set it |

<a id="t-mortal-set_power_law"></a>

#### Set power law

As ruler, you set how your realm treats those with powers and powers over minds. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Peoples judge the gifted, realms register, license, conscript or hunt them, the gifted band together and lines pass powers on](interconnections.md#m-powered_society).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Level | Those with powers are: free, registered, licensed, conscripted, hunted or banned |
| Mind powers | Powers over minds are: allowed, licensed or banned |

<a id="t-mortal-set_rights"></a>

#### Set rights

As ruler or regent you grant or take away a right (office, marriage, arms, courts, property, movement, vote, worship, assembly, education) for one standing of your realm (citizen, subject, resident_foreigner, protected_minority, bound, stateless). Those who lack a right grow restless. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Category | The standing |
| Right | The right |
| Granted | True to grant it, false to take it away |

<a id="t-mortal-set_surveillance"></a>

#### Set surveillance

As ruler, you set what your realm's watch looks for. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Realms watch their towns as far as their networks, stores, spies and laws allow; watching cuts crime and chills liberty](interconnections.md#m-surveillance).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Focus | What the watch looks for |

<a id="t-mortal-set_tax"></a>

#### Set tax

As ruler or regent you set the tax your realm's cities pay (0 to 50%); high taxes fill the treasury and stir unrest. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Rate | The share of the cities' output taken in tax (%) |

<a id="t-mortal-suspend_treaty"></a>

#### Suspend treaty

As ruler or regent you suspend a treaty your realm is party to: its clauses rest without a breach until it resumes or lapses by its term; the other parties trust you less, though less than for a breach. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Treaties: clauses executed, breaches, guarantors, renewal and shared wars](interconnections.md#m-treaties).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Treaty | The treaty |

<a id="t-mortal-swear_fealty"></a>

#### Swear fealty

As ruler, you bind your realm to another as its subject. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Vassals pay tribute and send levies; disloyal vassals of weak lords revolt or change lords; realm diplomacy and pacts](interconnections.md#m-vassal_duties).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Overlord | The realm you bind yourself to |
| Kind | How your realm is bound |

<a id="t-mortal-table_league_motion"></a>

#### Table league motion

As the ruler of a voting member you lay a motion before an organ of the league; it is voted at once by the league's basis, and a veto blocks it. What passes binds the members as far as they obey. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Organisations of polities: leagues with charters, dues, votes and vetoes, sanctions, courts, common force, shared money and federation](interconnections.md#m-leagues).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| League | The league |
| Organ | Which organ hears it: assembly, council, court, secretariat or force |
| Kind | What is moved: admit, expel, sanction, common_tariff, common_force, ruling, budget or mediation |
| Subject | Whom or what it concerns: a realm to admit, expel, sanction or mediate, a foe for the common force, or a law to rule on |

<a id="t-mortal-take_arms"></a>

#### Take arms

As head of a house, or ruler of a realm if you head no house, you choose the arms it bears: field, division, line, charges, rim, supporters and motto. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Heraldry: arms for houses, realms, titles, armies and guilds, drawn from their values, differenced for cadets and quartered by marriage](interconnections.md#m-heraldry).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Field | The main colour of the field |
| Shape | The shape of the shield |
| Division | How the field is divided |
| Line | The line of the division |
| Second | The second colour of a divided field |
| Charge | The figure set on the field |
| Charge tincture | The colour of that figure |
| Rim | The border around the field |
| Arrangement | How several figures are set out |
| Supporter | The figure that holds up the arms |
| Supporter pose | How the supporters stand |
| Motto | The words under the arms |

<a id="t-mortal-take_client"></a>

#### Take client

You take a mortal where you are as your client, in one of these roles: household, retainer, official, commander, broker, artist, informant, scholar. Clients serve their patron, and a patron speaks for them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Patrons and clients: favours and appointments bind clients to patrons, and weak institutions let patronage decide offices and votes](interconnections.md#m-clientelism).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Client | The mortal |
| Role | The role they take |

<a id="t-mortal-take_up_want"></a>

#### Take up want

You take up a want of your own, toward something or no one in particular. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What you want |
| Target | What or whom the want is toward, if anything |

<a id="t-mortal-tear_down_cult"></a>

#### Tear down cult

As the ruler of a realm you tear down the cult of a leader in it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cults of living leaders: instruments, legitimacy and sincerity, banned criticism, and the cult's fate at the leader's death](interconnections.md#m-leader_cults).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cult | The cult |

<a id="t-mortal-test_for_gifts"></a>

#### Test for gifts

As ruler, you have the people of your town tested for hidden gifts. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Peoples judge the gifted, realms register, license, conscript or hunt them, the gifted band together and lines pass powers on](interconnections.md#m-powered_society).

<a id="p-mortal-law"></a>

### Law

20 tools: [accuse](#t-mortal-accuse), [accuse of witchcraft](#t-mortal-accuse_of_witchcraft), [appeal conviction](#t-mortal-appeal_conviction), [bind with charm](#t-mortal-bind_with_charm), [choose path](#t-mortal-choose_path), [claim bounty](#t-mortal-claim_bounty), [claim courts](#t-mortal-claim_courts), [grant pardon](#t-mortal-grant_pardon), [lay geas on kin](#t-mortal-lay_geas_on_kin), [lift affliction](#t-mortal-lift_affliction), [make will](#t-mortal-make_will), [open reckoning](#t-mortal-open_reckoning), [post bounty](#t-mortal-post_bounty), [propose law](#t-mortal-propose_law), [register deed](#t-mortal-register_deed), [seek sanctuary](#t-mortal-seek_sanctuary), [swear geas](#t-mortal-swear_geas), [swear oath](#t-mortal-swear_oath), [testify](#t-mortal-testify), [try servant](#t-mortal-try_servant).

<a id="t-mortal-accuse"></a>

#### Accuse

You accuse a mortal of your settlement of a crime and stand as a witness against them. The case is tried in a later epoch in the way the realm's penal code sets, and a true charge is easier to prove than a false one. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Crimes become cases with witnesses, an investigation that may torture or blame a scapegoat, and a trial by judge, jury, combat or ordeal](interconnections.md#m-trials).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Accused | Whom you accuse |
| Crime | The crime you charge them with |

<a id="t-mortal-accuse_of_witchcraft"></a>

#### Accuse of witchcraft

You accuse a neighbour of your town of witchcraft; the court tries them in a later epoch by the way your realm tries cases, and the charge may be true or false. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Witchcraft: blamed misfortune brings accusations the courts try, real casters may be accused, and stress lights witch hunts](interconnections.md#m-witchcraft).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Accused | Whom you accuse |

<a id="t-mortal-appeal_conviction"></a>

#### Appeal conviction

You carry your latest conviction to the next court above the one that judged you, paying its fee; it may overturn the conviction and free you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Courts and appeals: a conviction climbs from the local to the provincial and high courts for a fee; advocates sway the outcome](interconnections.md#m-appeals).

<a id="t-mortal-bind_with_charm"></a>

#### Bind with charm

You make a charm that someone where you are takes in, binding them to you in love, obedience or devotion until it fades; if it is found, trust in you breaks. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Who takes it in |
| Kind | What it binds them to feel |

<a id="t-mortal-choose_path"></a>

#### Choose path

You take up a new path in life among those open to you where you are; each trains its own skills and leads its own way. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Path | The path |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_bounty"></a>

#### Claim bounty

As the best hunter of the realm that outlawed someone, you hunt them down for the bounty; you bring them to prison or kill them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Outlawry: those who flee the courts lose the law's protection, carry a bounty, are hunted, gather in bands and may become folk heroes](interconnections.md#m-outlawry).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outlaw | The outlaw |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_courts"></a>

#### Claim courts

As ruler, you claim a right of judgement over another realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Jurisdiction: churches, guilds and foreign realms claim their own courts, sparing their members and struggling with the crown](interconnections.md#m-jurisdictions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The other realm |
| Claim | The right you claim |

<a id="t-mortal-grant_pardon"></a>

#### Grant pardon

As ruler you pardon someone held in prison or outlawed; pardoning one wrongly condemned earns renown, pardoning a friend is read as corruption. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Pardons and amnesties: rulers free friends at a cost in corruption, the wrongly condemned to renown, and all at a new reign](interconnections.md#m-pardons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | Who you pardon |

<a id="t-mortal-lay_geas_on_kin"></a>

#### Lay geas on kin

By your word as kin you lay a geas on a child of your own: a taboo it must never break or a duty it must keep doing (taboo: oathbreaking, guest_killing, cowardice, theft, killing_kin, desecration, betrayal, torture, magic_use, hoarding_in_famine; duty: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning; condition: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning). Where your people hold to their lines it may pass on to the child's own children. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Geasa: taboos and duties laid on mortals by gods, seers and binding rites, favour while kept and doom when broken](interconnections.md#m-geasa).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Child | Your child |
| Mode | taboo, duty, condition |
| Act | The act it forbids or asks |

<a id="t-mortal-lift_affliction"></a>

#### Lift affliction

As a healer or a priest, you lift an affliction from someone where you are, by one of the cures it yields to. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Affliction | The affliction |
| Cure | The cure you use |

<a id="t-mortal-make_will"></a>

#### Make will

You write your will: the heirs who receive what you own when you die, and the share of it each receives. The shares add up to 100. Kin you leave out may contest it; where your people do not pass property by will, the law may set it aside. A new will replaces the old. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Heirs | The living heirs you name |
| Shares | The share each heir receives, in the same order, adding up to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-open_reckoning"></a>

#### Open reckoning

As the ruler of a realm you open a reckoning with the servants of a fallen regime by the measures you choose. Harsh measures may drive the old servants to revolt or flight; weak ones leave them in power and the victims aggrieved. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Reckoning after a fallen regime: tribunals, inquiries, bars from office, amnesties, restitution, reparation, memorials and open records](interconnections.md#m-reckoning).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Polity | Your realm |
| Fallen regime | The fallen ruler or regime, if one is named |
| Measures | The measures: tribunal, truth_inquiry, office_bar, amnesty, amnesty_for_confession, restitution, reparation_to_victims, memorial or record_opening |

<a id="t-mortal-post_bounty"></a>

#### Post bounty

You pay coin onto the bounty of an outlaw; whoever hunts them down is paid it with the realm's own bounty. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outlaw | The outlaw |
| Amount | The coin you put up |

<a id="t-mortal-propose_law"></a>

#### Propose law

As a ruler or a holder of a council office, you bring a typed law (or a new wording of one already in force) before your realm. It does not pass at once: each epoch it moves by the weight of those for it against those against it, under the realm's way of deciding, and it fails if it stalls. Hooks you hold over council members may win their votes. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Laws pass as proposals that move by the weight of voices for and against them; estates and hooks stall or carry them](interconnections.md#m-enactment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What the law rules, by its kind |
| Params | The law's terms by name, only those its kind has; leave out a term to keep its default. Terms of each kind: succession_rule: rule primogeniture/seniority/elective/tanistry/through_second_line/by_combat/divine_choice/ultimogeniture/designation/rotation, sex_rule any/begetter_preference/begetters_only/bearer_preference/bearers_only, wholeness_required true/false, min_age 0-200; inheritance_split: split eldest/equal/youngest; land_tenure: tenure feudal/communal/freehold; slavery: status allowed/banned/debt_only; serfdom: allowed true/false, labour_days 0-200; tax_rate: rate 0-100; tithe: rate 0-50, enforced true/false; tolls: rate 0-100; debt_law: remedy bondage/prison/jubilee, jubilee_epochs 1-20; penal_code: theft <a id="t-mortal-register_deed"></a>

#### Register deed

You register a contract with another party in your realm's registry (debt, sale, union_payment, indenture, patent, pattern, mind_state, line, company, birth, death, citizenship, will, licence); where the realm keeps a written code its courts make the other party pay what is owed, and elsewhere it rests on who is stronger. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Registries: titles to land and contracts of debt, sale, union portions and indenture are recorded, enforced by courts under a written code](interconnections.md#m-registries).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Counterparty | The other party |
| Kind | The kind of contract |
| Amount | What they owe you |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_sanctuary"></a>

#### Seek sanctuary

If you are hunted as an outlaw or accused before a court, you claim sanctuary in a temple of your town, or with a town whose custom welcomes guests. While you are sheltered no one may seize or punish you; your pursuer may wait, bargain, surround the refuge or break in. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sanctuary and asylum: the hunted shelter in temples, welcoming towns and foreign realms; pursuers wait, bargain, besiege or break in](interconnections.md#m-sanctuary).

<a id="t-mortal-swear_geas"></a>

#### Swear geas

You swear a geas upon yourself: a taboo you will never break or a duty you will keep doing (taboo: oathbreaking, guest_killing, cowardice, theft, killing_kin, desecration, betrayal, torture, magic_use, hoarding_in_famine; duty: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning; condition: hospitality, mercy_to_enemy, building_temples, learning). Breaking it is breaking an oath, judged as your people judge oathbreakers. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Geasa: taboos and duties laid on mortals by gods, seers and binding rites, favour while kept and doom when broken](interconnections.md#m-geasa).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mode | taboo, duty, condition |
| Act | The act it forbids or asks |

<a id="t-mortal-swear_oath"></a>

#### Swear oath

You swear an oath (fealty, vengeance, celibacy, pilgrimage, protection, silence, sworn_kinship, service, poverty, nonviolence, fidelity, secrecy, testimony, office); keeping it wins honour, breaking it costs honour and a god's favour. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The oath |
| Recipient | To whom or against whom, if anyone (for a pilgrimage: the holy place) |
| Words | The words of the oath |

<a id="t-mortal-testify"></a>

#### Testify

You give evidence in an open case of your town, of one kind: witness, trace, document, confession, divination, magic, expert, hearsay, record, body. Sworn evidence counts for more, and a false oath is broken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Crimes become cases with witnesses, an investigation that may torture or blame a scapegoat, and a trial by judge, jury, combat or ordeal](interconnections.md#m-trials).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Case | The case |
| Kind | The kind of evidence |
| Sworn | True to swear before you speak |

<a id="t-mortal-try_servant"></a>

#### Try servant

As the ruler of the realm you bring one to trial in an open reckoning for deeds the record ties to them; the tribunal convicts or acquits at once. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Reckoning after a fallen regime: tribunals, inquiries, bars from office, amnesties, restitution, reparation, memorials and open records](interconnections.md#m-reckoning).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Reckoning | The reckoning |
| Accused | The accused |
| Incident | The recorded deeds you name |

<a id="p-mortal-intrigue"></a>

### Intrigue

15 tools: [bribe official](#t-mortal-bribe_official), [build spy network](#t-mortal-build_spy_network), [claim turf](#t-mortal-claim_turf), [defend system](#t-mortal-defend_system), [hunt moles](#t-mortal-hunt_moles), [intrude](#t-mortal-intrude), [leak records](#t-mortal-leak_records), [pick lock](#t-mortal-pick_lock), [plot scheme](#t-mortal-plot_scheme), [press leverage](#t-mortal-press_leverage), [recruit agent](#t-mortal-recruit_agent), [run operation](#t-mortal-run_operation), [run racket](#t-mortal-run_racket), [sabotage](#t-mortal-sabotage), [set crime stance](#t-mortal-set_crime_stance).

<a id="t-mortal-bribe_official"></a>

#### Bribe official

As leader of a criminal organisation you put an official on your payroll: you pay a bribe each epoch and the realm's pressure on you is turned aside. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Criminal organisations: rackets bring income, officials on the payroll turn suppression aside, informers are killed by the code, and realms suppress or co-opt them](interconnections.md#m-crime_orgs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Official | The official |

<a id="t-mortal-build_spy_network"></a>

#### Build spy network

You build a spy network inside a nearby community: you learn its secrets and gain an advantage in war; if caught, relations sour and war may break out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community's id |
| Cover | The spies' cover |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_turf"></a>

#### Claim turf

As leader of a criminal organisation you claim a district of your town: your organisation collects there, and rivals on the same streets may fight you for them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Turf: criminal organisations hold districts, grow under weak watch and fight turf wars that kill and disorder the town](interconnections.md#m-turf).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| District | The district of your town |

<a id="t-mortal-defend_system"></a>

#### Defend system

You harden a network or store against intrusion. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Foes break into each other's networks, stores and made minds to read, alter, deny, seize, plant or extort, and are sometimes found](interconnections.md#m-intrusions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| System | The network or store |

<a id="t-mortal-hunt_moles"></a>

#### Hunt moles

As spymaster you hunt for moles at your court; you may catch a foreign agent, clear an official, or wrongly accuse one, who may then be purged or defect. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Mole hunts and defectors: spymasters hunt at their own courts, paranoid ones accuse the innocent, who are purged or defect with secrets](interconnections.md#m-mole_hunts).

<a id="t-mortal-intrude"></a>

#### Intrude

You try to break into a network, store, mind or made thing for the aim you name; you may be found and identified. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Foes break into each other's networks, stores and made minds to read, alter, deny, seize, plant or extort, and are sometimes found](interconnections.md#m-intrusions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | What kind of thing you break into |
| Target | The thing |
| Aim | What you want from it |

<a id="t-mortal-leak_records"></a>

#### Leak records

You let out the secrets a store holds, so they become known. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Foes break into each other's networks, stores and made minds to read, alter, deny, seize, plant or extort, and are sometimes found](interconnections.md#m-intrusions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Store | The store |

<a id="t-mortal-pick_lock"></a>

#### Pick lock

You try to get past the lock and guards of a store or vault where you are; some locks yield only to the working or the skill that made them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Criminal organisations: rackets bring income, officials on the payroll turn suppression aside, informers are killed by the code, and realms suppress or co-opt them](interconnections.md#m-crime_orgs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure | The store or vault |

<a id="t-mortal-plot_scheme"></a>

#### Plot scheme

You begin a scheme against someone (murder, abduct, seduce, sway, fabricate_claim, fabricate_hook, steal_relic, coup, poison_well, frame, elope, convert_secretly, sabotage_wedding, free_prisoner, ecosabotage, learn_true_name, forge_letter, geas_trap, hide_fleet, forge_work, fix_contest, incite_crowd). Friends join as agents, power gathers from skill in intrigue and deception, and it strikes against the target's spymaster and intuition. Each epoch it may be discovered. One scheme at a time. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Ambitious, cruel and deceitful mortals plot schemes against rivals: they recruit friends as agents, gather power from skill in intrigue and deception and strike against the target's spymaster and intuition; each epoch a scheme may be discovered](interconnections.md#m-schemes) and [Fog, blizzards and storms, festivals, plague and siege change a scheme's odds and witnesses; schemes may blight a land in the night](interconnections.md#m-intrigue_conditions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The scheme |
| Target | Whom or what it is aimed at |

<a id="t-mortal-press_leverage"></a>

#### Press leverage

You lean on someone you hold a hook on, or who owes you a favour, with one demand: marriage, step down, support, vote, law, appointment, silence, payment, release or information. Release frees those they hold of yours; information tells you what they know. Appointment puts you in a vacant office of the realm they rule; silence keeps them from testifying before a court that tries you. A weak hook is spent and a strong one may wear weak; a favour called in may be refused. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Gifts, rescues, support in war or succession and aid in schemes leave favours owed; honouring one wins trust, refusing costs trust and honour](interconnections.md#m-favours) and [Things keep typed secrets born of affairs, hidden births, hidden conditions, debts, hidden faiths, heretical and void rites, broken oaths, flight in battle and schemes; whoever learns one holds a hook, and a secret brought to light shames its keeper](interconnections.md#m-secrets_and_hooks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you press |
| Demand | What you demand |

<a id="t-mortal-recruit_agent"></a>

#### Recruit agent

As a realm's spymaster, you win someone living among those you spy on as an agent. Motives: money, ideology, compromise, ego, love, revenge, faith. Money always serves; the others must truly move them. Love holds while their bond to the handler holds; revenge holds while its target stands. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Typed spy networks owned by realms, factions and cults, run by named agents under cover](interconnections.md#m-spy_agents).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | Whom you recruit |
| Motive | What wins them |

<a id="t-mortal-run_operation"></a>

#### Run operation

As a realm's spymaster, you set its network on a rival realm or community. Operations: steal_knowledge, sabotage, assassinate, incite, intel, blackmail, counterfeit, infiltrate, exfiltrate, disinform, recruit_agent, surveil. Each can fail by its difficulty against your network's depth and your skill. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spy networks with cover, depth, exposure and intelligence; typed operations and counter-intelligence](interconnections.md#m-spy_networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you work against |
| Operation | The operation |

<a id="t-mortal-run_racket"></a>

#### Run racket

As leader of a faction, you run a racket in your town for steady income. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Criminal organisations: rackets bring income, officials on the payroll turn suppression aside, informers are killed by the code, and realms suppress or co-opt them](interconnections.md#m-crime_orgs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The racket |

<a id="t-mortal-sabotage"></a>

#### Sabotage

You secretly harm a community: poison (population or life decreases), arson (prosperity, defence, wealth decrease) or discord (you break off one of its allies from it). If you are caught you become enemies. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community's id |
| Kind | poison, arson or discord |
| How | How you do it |

<a id="t-mortal-set_crime_stance"></a>

#### Set crime stance

As the ruler of a realm, you set how it treats an organisation of crime in it: hunt it down, use it, look away for its bribes or license its trade under terms. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Criminal organisations: rackets bring income, officials on the payroll turn suppression aside, informers are killed by the code, and realms suppress or co-opt them](interconnections.md#m-crime_orgs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Faction | The organisation |
| Stance | How the realm treats it |

<a id="p-mortal-communication"></a>

### Communication

14 tools: [build network](#t-mortal-build_network), [censor outlet](#t-mortal-censor_outlet), [cover up](#t-mortal-cover_up), [cut network](#t-mortal-cut_network), [expel envoy](#t-mortal-expel_envoy), [found outlet](#t-mortal-found_outlet), [investigate](#t-mortal-investigate), [jam signal](#t-mortal-jam_signal), [join network](#t-mortal-join_network), [keep secret of](#t-mortal-keep_secret_of), [persuade](#t-mortal-persuade), [publish item](#t-mortal-publish_item), [recount](#t-mortal-recount), [send dispatch](#t-mortal-send_dispatch).

<a id="t-mortal-build_network"></a>

#### Build network

You build a network from your home, carried the way you name, open to whom you choose, and bring the members you name onto it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Networks carry messages, records, control, money, minds and opinion; members come to depend on them and suffer when they go down](interconnections.md#m-networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Carrier | How it carries what it carries |
| Access | Who may use it |
| Member | Who else joins at once |

<a id="t-mortal-censor_outlet"></a>

#### Censor outlet

As the ruler of a realm, you act on an outlet in it: censor halves its reach, license also costs it trust, seize takes it for the realm, close ends it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Recorded and mass media: media and their fidelity, outlets that reach, earn, report, sway opinion and are found false, censored or seized](interconnections.md#m-media).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outlet | The outlet |
| Measure | The measure: censor, license, seize or close |

<a id="t-mortal-cover_up"></a>

#### Cover up

As one who holds it, you cover up a hidden truth in your town so fewer believe what they heard. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Truths are hidden from whole peoples, kept, sought, breached, covered up, revealed and in time forgotten](interconnections.md#m-concealment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Truth | The hidden truth |

<a id="t-mortal-cut_network"></a>

#### Cut network

You cut one member off a network, or bring the whole network down for an epoch. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Networks carry messages, records, control, money, minds and opinion; members come to depend on them and suffer when they go down](interconnections.md#m-networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Network | The network |
| Node | The member to cut off, or empty to bring it all down |

<a id="t-mortal-expel_envoy"></a>

#### Expel envoy

As ruler or regent you send home an envoy received at your court: a mark of anger short of war. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Messages between the living travel by envoy, letter, herald, bird, signal fire, dream or magic, taking time by distance, medium, season and signal links; spies read or seize them, outlaws waylay them, forgers replace them and tongues misread them](interconnections.md#m-dispatches).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Envoy | The envoy |

<a id="t-mortal-found_outlet"></a>

#### Found outlet

You found an outlet in your town that puts out word to its audience each season. Its reach grows with its staff and medium; its stance and lean colour what it tells, and its trust falls when what it spreads is found false. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Recorded and mass media: media and their fidelity, outlets that reach, earn, report, sway opinion and are found false, censored or seized](interconnections.md#m-media).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The outlet's name |
| Medium | A recorded medium it uses, or none |
| Stance | A value it leans on, or none |
| Lean | How far it leans, from -100 against to 100 for |
| Funding | How it is paid for: sales, patronage, advertising or state |

<a id="t-mortal-investigate"></a>

#### Investigate

You look into a hidden truth you have heard of, for an aim of your own. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Truths are hidden from whole peoples, kept, sought, breached, covered up, revealed and in time forgotten](interconnections.md#m-concealment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Truth | The hidden truth |
| Aim | What you want: expose, exploit, destroy_the_hidden, join_the_hidden, understand or protect |

<a id="t-mortal-jam_signal"></a>

#### Jam signal

You jam a signal station where you are, by interference, a working or sabotage: it carries nothing until the next season and is not taken. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Realms that know cryptography write in ciphers that codebreakers break over seasons by the traffic they intercept; signal links shorten journeys and fall to those who take their lands](interconnections.md#m-ciphers_and_signals).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Link | The station |

<a id="t-mortal-join_network"></a>

#### Join network

You bring your home onto a network. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Networks carry messages, records, control, money, minds and opinion; members come to depend on them and suffer when they go down](interconnections.md#m-networks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Network | The network |

<a id="t-mortal-keep_secret_of"></a>

#### Keep secret of

You take up the keeping of a hidden truth you know of. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Truths are hidden from whole peoples, kept, sought, breached, covered up, revealed and in time forgotten](interconnections.md#m-concealment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Truth | The hidden truth |

<a id="t-mortal-persuade"></a>

#### Persuade

You try to bring another being round by an appeal of one kind: belief, self_interest, authority, evidence, fear, feeling, bond or tradition. You may aim at a belief, a decision, a faith, a vote, a treaty, a surrender or a confession: a surrender is sought from the ruler of a town your side besieges and works only once its defenders falter; a confession is sought from one you hold accused. An appeal to a bond fails where none ties you. Your oratory, how well the appeal fits them, their stubbornness and their passions decide it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Typed arguments sway beliefs, sermons, council votes and envoy talks by oratory, the appeal's fit, stubbornness and passions](interconnections.md#m-persuasion).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The being you try to persuade |
| Appeal | How you argue |
| Aim | What you seek from them |
| Axis | For a belief: the belief at stake |
| Toward | For a belief: how far toward it (-100 to 100) |
| Subject | For a surrender: the town besieged |

<a id="t-mortal-publish_item"></a>

#### Publish item

Your outlet puts out an item. When it reports a happening, its accuracy follows what happened and your slant; slanted items move the opinion of its audience on their subject. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Recorded and mass media: media and their fidelity, outlets that reach, earn, report, sway opinion and are found false, censored or seized](interconnections.md#m-media).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Outlet | Your outlet |
| Kind | The item: news, opinion, record_of_event, entertainment, instruction, promotion, devotion, official, satire, sport or notices |
| Subject incident | The happening it tells of, or none |
| Subject node | Whom or what it speaks of, or none |
| Slant | How it leans, from -100 against to 100 for |

<a id="t-mortal-recount"></a>

#### Recount

Tells the people you live among what you know of a happening you saw or took part in, step by step as you know it; they pass it on as your account. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Those present at a happening witness it; retellings change its parts, truer tellings set them right and contrary ones cost trust](interconnections.md#m-witnesses_and_tellings).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Incident | The happening's id |
| Step offset | Where to start in its steps when they run past one page |

<a id="t-mortal-send_dispatch"></a>

#### Send dispatch

You send a message to someone: proposal, demand, threat, insult, gift notice, declaration, report, plea, intelligence, order, warning, condolence or congratulation, by one of these: bird, envoy, herald, letter, network, signal_fire. It takes time by distance, medium and season; spies may read or seize it, outlaws may waylay it, and in another tongue it may be misread. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults, factions, spirits and polities.
- **Reaches** [Messages between the living travel by envoy, letter, herald, bird, signal fire, dream or magic, taking time by distance, medium, season and signal links; spies read or seize them, outlaws waylay them, forgers replace them and tongues misread them](interconnections.md#m-dispatches).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Recipient | To whom |
| Content | What it says |
| Medium | How it travels |
| Army | For an order to a commander: the army it moves |
| Region | For an order to a commander: the land the army is to go to |

<a id="p-mortal-war"></a>

### War

38 tools: [appoint commander](#t-mortal-appoint_commander), [begin mass weapon](#t-mortal-begin_mass_weapon), [challenge to duel](#t-mortal-challenge_to_duel), [clear remnants](#t-mortal-clear_remnants), [collaborate](#t-mortal-collaborate), [contest orbit](#t-mortal-contest_orbit), [decide captives](#t-mortal-decide_captives), [decide fall](#t-mortal-decide_fall), [declare posture](#t-mortal-declare_posture), [declare war](#t-mortal-declare_war), [defend siege](#t-mortal-defend_siege), [draft unit type](#t-mortal-draft_unit_type), [draw design](#t-mortal-draw_design), [escape](#t-mortal-escape), [expose collaborator](#t-mortal-expose_collaborator), [forge model](#t-mortal-forge_model), [grant veterans](#t-mortal-grant_veterans), [guard throne](#t-mortal-guard_throne), [impose measure](#t-mortal-impose_measure), [launch campaign](#t-mortal-launch_campaign), [lead migration](#t-mortal-lead_migration), [man station](#t-mortal-man_station), [negotiate surrender](#t-mortal-negotiate_surrender), [offer terms](#t-mortal-offer_terms), [order formation](#t-mortal-order_formation), [order siege work](#t-mortal-order_siege_work), [parley](#t-mortal-parley), [pay ransom](#t-mortal-pay_ransom), [raise army](#t-mortal-raise_army), [refit section](#t-mortal-refit_section), [rescue captive](#t-mortal-rescue_captive), [set army stance](#t-mortal-set_army_stance), [set occupation rule](#t-mortal-set_occupation_rule), [shore up](#t-mortal-shore_up), [suborn garrison](#t-mortal-suborn_garrison), [train herd](#t-mortal-train_herd), [trim sails](#t-mortal-trim_sails), [use mass weapon](#t-mortal-use_mass_weapon).

<a id="t-mortal-appoint_commander"></a>

#### Appoint commander

You appoint a named mortal (bound to you or from your land) at the head of your army; the commander's strength and charisma reflect on the army. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Mortal | The named mortal's id |

<a id="t-mortal-begin_mass_weapon"></a>

#### Begin mass weapon

As the leader of a realm, organisation, faith or league you begin making a weapon whose harm reaches a whole settlement, region or world. It needs the means its harm and reach call for, costs the treasury each epoch, and passes through stages its rivals may see and answer by making their own. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Weapons of mass harm, deterrence and the remnants of war: programmes, postures, tests, uses, arms treaties and what war leaves in the land](interconnections.md#m-mass_weapons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | Whose arsenal it joins |
| Harm | Its harm: blast, fire, poison, disease, radiation, swarm, field, rite or divine_relic |
| Reach | Its reach: settlement, region or world |

<a id="t-mortal-challenge_to_duel"></a>

#### Challenge to duel

You challenge a mortal to single combat. Kinds of duel: war, honour, trial, champion, god, ritual, sport; a duel of war, of champions or of gods needs a war between your homes, the others someone where you are. A duel may end by: yield, disarm, crippled, death, exhaustion, unconscious, first_wound, ring_out, stopped; leave the ending empty to fight by the custom of your people. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Duels of exchanges: reach, armour, stamina, yielding and mercy; war, honour and champion duels](interconnections.md#m-duels).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you challenge |
| Context | What kind of duel it is |
| Ending | How it ends; leave empty for your people's custom |

<a id="t-mortal-clear_remnants"></a>

#### Clear remnants

You pay to clear what war left in a land, which harms those who live there until it fades or is cleared. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Weapons of mass harm, deterrence and the remnants of war: programmes, postures, tests, uses, arms treaties and what war leaves in the land](interconnections.md#m-mass_weapons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land |
| Budget | What you spend, from 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-collaborate"></a>

#### Collaborate

Living under an occupation, you serve the occupier for a reward. Those who serve cut the cost of ruling and, informing or policing, the partisans' raids; the partisans may come to know and kill them, and a liberated land may judge them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Occupation regimes: rule of occupied lands, collaborators, measures against resistance, annexation, settlers and liberation](interconnections.md#m-occupation_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Occupation | The occupation |
| Kind | How you serve: administration, police, informing, trade, militia or propaganda |

<a id="t-mortal-contest_orbit"></a>

#### Contest orbit

As the leader of a realm whose host is at a world you fight for the space around it, by the host's vessels and numbers. Who holds it may strike the enemy below, take a toll of every way that reaches the world, and cut the enemy's signals. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [War and migration between worlds: campaigns, orbits, landings and supply, bombardment, blockade, and peoples moving between worlds](interconnections.md#m-interworld).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| World | The world |
| Fleet | The host that fights |

<a id="t-mortal-decide_captives"></a>

#### Decide captives

You decide what becomes of a band of captives your side holds. Fates: held, ransomed, enslaved, sacrificed, labour, exchanged, released, executed, recruited, adopted. Each is judged by the peoples who hear of it; the recruited join your army with little loyalty and desert easily. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Captives taken in battle: ransom, release, sacrifice, labour, enslavement and exchange; wards and torture](interconnections.md#m-captive_cohorts).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cohort | The band of captives |
| Fate | What becomes of them |

<a id="t-mortal-decide_fall"></a>

#### Decide fall

As a besieger, you decide what becomes of the place if it falls. Choices: mercy, sack, raze, ransom_city, deport, resettle. A ransom spares it for a payment; deportation moves its people to your lands. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Wall material, works, relief, taboo by values, masons, sanitation, hunger betrayal, mercy or sack and its spoils](interconnections.md#m-siege_depth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Siege | The siege |
| Choice | What becomes of it |

<a id="t-mortal-declare_posture"></a>

#### Declare posture

As the leader of an arsenal's holder you declare how it would use its weapons, toward one rival or all. A credible posture holds rivals back from attacking; a posture with no ready weapon behind it may be exposed as a bluff. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Weapons of mass harm, deterrence and the remnants of war: programmes, postures, tests, uses, arms treaties and what war leaves in the land](interconnections.md#m-mass_weapons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Holder | Whose posture it is |
| Toward | Toward whom; leave empty for all |
| Posture | The posture: none, declared, ambiguous, first_use, retaliation_only or shared_with_league |

<a id="t-mortal-declare_war"></a>

#### Declare war

You declare war on a community or a creature. The world's course decides the war; your weapons and your god lend you strength. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community or creature |
| Reason | Why you go to war |

<a id="t-mortal-defend_siege"></a>

#### Defend siege

As a defender, you choose how your besieged place holds out this season. Defences: sally, repair, ration, seek_relief, flood_approaches, scorched_earth, expel_mouths, burn_works, evacuate_by_sea. Flooding the approaches needs a river or water works; driving out those who cannot fight is judged by every people that hears of it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Wall material, works, relief, taboo by values, masons, sanitation, hunger betrayal, mercy or sack and its spoils](interconnections.md#m-siege_depth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Siege | The siege |
| Defence | The defence |

<a id="t-mortal-draft_unit_type"></a>

#### Draft unit type

As one who knows war, you set out a new kind of soldier for your realm or town: a named type for a battlefield role, with the loadout, doctrine and formation its makers can give it; its armies muster it from then on. Needs tactics or strategy of 30. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Kinds of soldier: each realm draws its own named types for its battlefield roles, refits them as better equipment is known and teaches them to allies](interconnections.md#m-unit_types).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Role | The battlefield role it fills; one of: levy (Levy), line_infantry (Line infantry), heavy_infantry (Heavy infantry), shield_infantry (Shield infantry), pike_infantry (Pike infantry), light_infantry (Light infantry), shock_infantry (Shock infantry), guard_infantry (Guard infantry), marines (Marines), slingers (Slingers), archers (Archers), crossbow_infantry (Crossbow infantry), javelineers (Javelineers), gunners (Gunners), grenadiers (Grenadiers), light_cavalry (Light cavalry), heavy_cavalry (Heavy cavalry), mounted_archers (Mounted archers), mounted_gunners (Mounted gunners), beast_riders (Beast riders), sky_riders (Sky riders), chariots (Chariots), vehicle_crew (Vehicle crew), siege_crew (Siege crew), artillery_crew (Artillery crew), naval_crew (Naval crew), sky_crew (Sky crew), void_crew (Void crew), war_beasts (War beasts), bound_beasts (Bound beasts), sappers (Sappers), engineers (Engineers), battle_casters (Battle casters), support_casters (Support casters), summoners (Summoners), gifted (Gifted), dampeners (Dampeners), detectors (Detectors), constructs (Constructs), summoned_host (Summoned host), mechanised_infantry (Mechanised infantry), special_forces (Special forces), partisans (Partisans), peacekeepers (Peacekeepers), medics (Medics), drone_operators (Drone operators), signals_troops (Signals troops), powered_troopers (Powered troopers), drop_troops (Drop troops), pilots (Pilots) |
| Name | What the soldiers are called; left empty, your people names them in its own tongue |
| Description | What sets these soldiers apart, in your words |

<a id="t-mortal-draw_design"></a>

You draw a new vehicle type for your realm or town on a frame it knows how to build, giving more weight to one part (hull, propulsion, hold, quarters, armour, weapon, ward, navigation, frame, counterweight, steering, lift, power, troops, command, life_support); it is entered in the atlas under its name, its sections, mounts, speed, reach, cargo, arms and crew follow from the frame, its parts and their materials, and new vehicles are built to it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Component designs: vessels and engines built from parts and materials, with lineage, refits, wear, failures and shipyard repair](interconnections.md#m-designs), [Vehicle types and groups: frames, sections of differing criticality, weapon mounts and techniques; yards build groups that march, sail and fight with armies](interconnections.md#m-vehicles) and [War machine designs: siege engines built to a realm's design, whose frame, counterweight and arms strengthen its batteries](interconnections.md#m-engine_designs).

| What it chooses | What it means |

| Frame | The frame it is built on; one of: riding_mount (Riding mount), pack_mount (Pack mount), travois (Travois), sledge (Sledge), handcart (Handcart), cart (Cart), wagon (Wagon), covered_wagon <a id="t-mortal-escape"></a>

#### Escape

If you are a captive you try to escape your captors; your agility and wits decide. If caught you are wounded. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| How | How you try to escape |

<a id="t-mortal-expose_collaborator"></a>

#### Expose collaborator

Living under an occupation, you expose one who serves the occupier, so that the partisans know them. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Occupation regimes: rule of occupied lands, collaborators, measures against resistance, annexation, settlers and liberation](interconnections.md#m-occupation_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Collaboration | The service to expose |

<a id="t-mortal-forge_model"></a>

#### Forge model

You make a new equipment model for your realm or town: a named weapon, armour, shield or piece of materiel, as good as your metalworking or engineering and your craft allow, made of your town's own material; its metalworkers make it from then on. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Equipment models: each holder forges named weapons, armour and materiel of its own families and materials into stores its armies draw on](interconnections.md#m-equipment_models).

| What it chooses | What it means |

polearm (Polearm), staff (Staff), whip (Whip), fist_weapon (Fist weapon), sling (Sling), javelin (Javelin), throwing_weapon (Throwing weapon), blowgun (Blowgun), bow (Bow), longbow (Longbow), composite_bow (Composite bow), crossbow (Crossbow), repeating_crossbow (Repeating crossbow), handgun (Handgun), musket (Musket), rifle (Rifle), pistol (Pistol), repeating_gun (Repeating gun), grenade
