<a id="t-mortal-grant_veterans"></a>

#### Grant veterans

As a ruler, you set what your realm grants its veterans when a war ends. Grants: land, pension, none, membership, title, tax_exemption, office. Each costs the treasury and wins the loyalty of the towns by its own measure. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Veterans after war: realms grant their veterans land in a colony on the frontier or pensions from the treasury, unpaid pensions breed grievance, and veterans band into associations that press for their due and back their old commanders](interconnections.md#m-veterans).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grant | The grant |

<a id="t-mortal-guard_throne"></a>

#### Guard throne

As ruler you choose how you guard your rule against your own army: rotating commanders lowers their power and the army's morale, a parallel guard costs upkeep, promotion by loyalty favours your clients, hostages hold the ambitious back. Each measure lowers the chance of a coup. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Coup-proofing: rotation, a parallel guard, promotion by loyalty and hostages lower coup risk and military quality at once](interconnections.md#m-coup_proofing).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Rotation | Rotate commanders and governors |
| Parallel guard | Keep a guard of your own beside the army |
| Loyalty promotion | Promote by loyalty over merit |
| Hostages | Keep hostages from the powerful |

<a id="t-mortal-impose_measure"></a>

#### Impose measure

As the ruler of an occupier you impose a measure on an occupied land. Measures cut the partisans' raids while they last; most raise resentment, and hostages, collective punishment and deportation are remembered against you. Deportation carries people off as captives; requisition takes from the towns' treasuries; an amnesty offer lowers resistance. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Occupation regimes: rule of occupied lands, collaborators, measures against resistance, annexation, settlers and liberation](interconnections.md#m-occupation_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Occupation | The occupation |
| Measure | The measure: curfew, hostages, collective_punishment, deportation, requisition, ban_on_gathering or amnesty_offer |
| Strength | How hard it is pressed, from 1 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-launch_campaign"></a>

#### Launch campaign

As the leader of a realm you send one of its hosts along a way to another world. Its carriers set how many cross; some are lost on the crossing; on arrival it must win the space around the world before it can land, and once landed it is supplied along the way, starving if the space is lost. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [War and migration between worlds: campaigns, orbits, landings and supply, bombardment, blockade, and peoples moving between worlds](interconnections.md#m-interworld).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Attacker | The realm that sends it |
| Target world | The world it goes to |
| Route | The way it crosses |
| Army | The host |

<a id="t-mortal-lead_migration"></a>

#### Lead migration

You lead the people of the settlement you live in or rule to another world joined to yours by an open way. They leave over epochs, as many as the way carries and the world they seek lets in. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [War and migration between worlds: campaigns, orbits, landings and supply, bombardment, blockade, and peoples moving between worlds](interconnections.md#m-interworld).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Community | The settlement |
| To world | The world they seek |
| Cause | Why they go: flight, land_hunger, faith_call, exile, labour_draw, world_dying or resettlement |

<a id="t-mortal-man_station"></a>

#### Man station

Aboard a vessel, you set how many hands work a station. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Crew stations and gunnery: gunners, riggers, marines, officers and rowers; the rate of fire from gunners' skill, losses and command](interconnections.md#m-gunnery).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Vessel | The vessel |
| Station | The station |
| Count | How many hands |

<a id="t-mortal-negotiate_surrender"></a>

#### Negotiate surrender

As a besieger, you offer terms to a besieged place; if it yields, the siege ends in surrender on terms you may keep or break. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Wall material, works, relief, taboo by values, masons, sanitation, hunger betrayal, mercy or sack and its spoils](interconnections.md#m-siege_depth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Siege | The siege |

<a id="t-mortal-offer_terms"></a>

#### Offer terms

As a commander besieging a town you offer it terms: it may surrender with its honour, and you are bound not to sack it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Field diplomacy: parleys, truces, surrender terms, exchanges of captives and the dead, safe conducts and fraternisation](interconnections.md#m-field_diplomacy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Town | The besieged town (an id) |

<a id="t-mortal-order_formation"></a>

#### Order formation

As the commander of a formation or of its army, you give it an order (attack, manoeuvre, resupply, hold, standing, screen); a formation on standing orders loses organisation, one ordered to attack fights harder, to hold digs in, to manoeuvre regains order and to resupply is fed first. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Order of battle: army groups, armies, corps, divisions and brigades, each with a headquarters, its commander, staff and place](interconnections.md#m-order_of_battle).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Echelon | The formation |
| Order | The order |

<a id="t-mortal-order_siege_work"></a>

#### Order siege work

As a besieger, you set your army to raise a siege work now. Works: circumvallation, towers, rams, mines, countermines, ladders, bombardment, sapping, blockade_lines, air_assault, network_attack, rite_breach, ramp, dam. Assault from the air needs fliers, an attack on the networks needs knowledge of computation on both sides, a breaching rite needs casters. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Wall material, works, relief, taboo by values, masons, sanitation, hunger betrayal, mercy or sack and its spoils](interconnections.md#m-siege_depth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Siege | The siege |
| Work | The work |

<a id="t-mortal-parley"></a>

#### Parley

As a commander you parley with the commander of an enemy army in your land before battle; it may end in a truce. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Field diplomacy: parleys, truces, surrender terms, exchanges of captives and the dead, safe conducts and fraternisation](interconnections.md#m-field_diplomacy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Foe | The enemy army you parley with (an id) |

<a id="t-mortal-pay_ransom"></a>

#### Pay ransom

You pay a ransom to the captors for a captured member of yours (bound to you or a mortal from your land): your wealth or prosperity decreases, theirs increases, the captive returns. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Captive | The captive's id |

<a id="t-mortal-raise_army"></a>

#### Raise army

You raise an army from your population (or your disciples); the soldiers leave the population (one without population hires mercenaries and pays from its wealth). It joins your ongoing war and protects you. Its equipment depends on your craft and what you know. One army per community. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | The army's name, from your own language |
| Percent | What percent of the population takes up arms (at most 15); default 5 |

<a id="t-mortal-refit_section"></a>

#### Refit section

Aboard a vessel, you refit one of its sections to sound condition. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Ship damage by location: round shot holes the hull, chain shot the rigging, grape the crew, fire the sails, and raking runs the length](interconnections.md#m-ship_damage).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Vessel | The vessel |
| Section | The section |

<a id="t-mortal-rescue_captive"></a>

#### Rescue captive

You try to rescue a captive by force: your strength against the captors' defence. Failure breeds enmity. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Captive | The captive's id |
| Plan | How you mean to free them |

<a id="t-mortal-set_army_stance"></a>

#### Set army stance

You set the stance of an army you command or own. Stances: march, forage, entrench, besiege, garrison, raid, retreat, winter_quarters, pursue, escort, disband. Winter quarters spare it attrition in a closed season and feed it on the land it holds; pursuit turns a beaten enemy's rout into losses and risks ambush; an escort guards its convoys and siege trains against raids. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Armies as unit stacks with equipment, experience, morale, fatigue and wounds](interconnections.md#m-army_stacks).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Army | The army |
| Stance | The stance |

<a id="t-mortal-set_occupation_rule"></a>

#### Set occupation rule

As the ruler of an occupier you set how an occupied land is ruled. Each rule sets what you take, how fast your control grows, how much the locals resent it and what ruling them costs; civil rule and annexation extend your laws over the land and bring settlers, and annexation takes the land once your control is high enough. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Occupation regimes: rule of occupied lands, collaborators, measures against resistance, annexation, settlers and liberation](interconnections.md#m-occupation_regimes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Occupation | The occupation |
| Rule | The rule: military, civil_by_occupier, local_puppet, annexation or protectorate |

<a id="t-mortal-shore_up"></a>

#### Shore up

You shore up a part of a building where you stand. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Structures stand in sections: gates, walls, keeps, sanctums and foundations fail by criticality, fire spreads by material and collapse runs down from the foundation](interconnections.md#m-structure_integrity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure | The building |
| Section | The part |

<a id="t-mortal-suborn_garrison"></a>

#### Suborn garrison

As a besieger, you work on someone inside a besieged place with bribes, hooks or a shared grudge to open a gate or postern; if the plot is found out, the defence grows firmer. It costs coin. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Wall material, works, relief, taboo by values, masons, sanitation, hunger betrayal, mercy or sack and its spoils](interconnections.md#m-siege_depth).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Siege | The siege |
| Target | Whom inside you work on |

<a id="t-mortal-train_herd"></a>

#### Train herd

You train a herd your people keep, for war, for the hunt or for guarding, with your own beast handling (at least 20). Trained war beasts join your people's armies, hunters bring home more and guards keep raiders off. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Master handlers train herds for war, the hunt or guarding; war beasts join armies, hunters add to the hunt, guards keep raiders off](interconnections.md#m-herd_training).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Species | The kind of the herd, from those your people keep |
| Training | What you train it for |

<a id="t-mortal-trim_sails"></a>

#### Trim sails

Aboard a vessel, you set its point of sail and the shot it loads. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Points of sail: speed by the angle to the wind, tacking and being caught in irons; galleys row against the wind at a cost to rowers](interconnections.md#m-points_of_sail).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Vessel | The vessel |
| Point of sail | How it sails to the wind |
| Shot | The shot it loads |

<a id="t-mortal-use_mass_weapon"></a>

#### Use mass weapon

As the leader of an arsenal's holder you loose a ready weapon of mass harm on a target within its reach. It kills across its reach, may burn, sicken or leave its harm in the land, is remembered by those it struck and brings the end of the world nearer. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Weapons of mass harm, deterrence and the remnants of war: programmes, postures, tests, uses, arms treaties and what war leaves in the land](interconnections.md#m-mass_weapons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Weapon | The weapon |
| Target | What it strikes: a settlement, or a place within its region or world |

<a id="p-mortal-faith"></a>

### Faith

19 tools: [argue doctrine](#t-mortal-argue_doctrine), [consult diviner](#t-mortal-consult_diviner), [deny god](#t-mortal-deny_god), [guard grave](#t-mortal-guard_grave), [make charm](#t-mortal-make_charm), [offer to spirit](#t-mortal-offer_to_spirit), [petition spirit](#t-mortal-petition_spirit), [place grave goods](#t-mortal-place_grave_goods), [preach](#t-mortal-preach), [preach end time](#t-mortal-preach_end_time), [prophesy](#t-mortal-prophesy), [purify self](#t-mortal-purify_self), [read the sky](#t-mortal-read_the_sky), [resurrection rite](#t-mortal-resurrection_rite), [rob grave](#t-mortal-rob_grave), [seek vision](#t-mortal-seek_vision), [take care](#t-mortal-take_care), [undertake heroquest](#t-mortal-undertake_heroquest), [worship](#t-mortal-worship).

<a id="t-mortal-argue_doctrine"></a>

#### Argue doctrine

As one of a faith's clergy, argue how it should hold the divine on one axis; your theology decides whether it is heard. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Conceptions of the divine: how a faith holds the number, nearness and nature of gods and the Creator, and how it reads each god](interconnections.md#m-divine_conceptions).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Faith | The faith you serve |
| Conception axis | The axis (count, nearness, personhood, creator) |
| Stance | How the divine is on it: for count one, one_above_many, many, pairs, all_is_divine, none; for nearness distant, present, within; for personhood persons, forces, principle, mixed; for creator acknowledged, unknown, denied, rival |

<a id="t-mortal-consult_diviner"></a>

#### Consult diviner

As ruler you have your court diviners read the omens on a question (battle, war, wedding, coronation, harvest, voyage, founding, illness, succession, lost_things), by a method your court keeps (augury, haruspicy, astrology, oracle, lots, dream_reading, scrying, spirit_consultation, earth_signs, numerology, body_reading) or by the one it trusts most; if you heed diviners you act on what they read. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Divination systems: peoples read omens by their own ways, valid only where a god answers, a seer is born or a law of nature makes signs real](interconnections.md#m-divination) and [Fortune is reckoned from blessings, curses, charms, oaths, powers over fate and a streak of its own, and moves the mishaps and draws of a life](interconnections.md#m-fortune).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Question | What you ask |
| Method | The method of divination, or empty for the one your court trusts most |

<a id="t-mortal-deny_god"></a>

#### Deny god

You deny your god: you stop worshipping that god and become godless. Your god's existence weakens, and the creator of the gods cannot draw strength from godless communities. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Reason | Why you turn away |

<a id="t-mortal-guard_grave"></a>

#### Guard grave

Pay to set a guard over a grave where you are: hidden, sealed, watched, trapped, or, where the laws of the land allow, cursed or warded. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Grave goods and tomb robbing: what the dead keep, the guards over it, the robbers who take it and the diggers who find it](interconnections.md#m-grave_goods).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grave | The grave |
| Guard | The guard to set |

<a id="t-mortal-make_charm"></a>

#### Make charm

You make an item you own a charm (protection, fertility, luck, healing, love, travel, concealment, courage); its power works where the item is enchanted, blessed or holds a spirit, and it calms you whether or not its power is real. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fortune is reckoned from blessings, curses, charms, oaths, powers over fate and a streak of its own, and moves the mishaps and draws of a life](interconnections.md#m-fortune) and [Misfortune and luck: misfortunes befall towns, peoples blame them by their values and luck beliefs, and answer them their way](interconnections.md#m-mishaps).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | The charm's power |

<a id="t-mortal-offer_to_spirit"></a>

#### Offer to spirit

You bring an offering to a spirit that dwells in a place; a content spirit grants favour to its community. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Place spirits: notable places, towns and houses gain spirits that take offerings from their community and give favour or misfortune](interconnections.md#m-genius_loci).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The spirit |

<a id="t-mortal-petition_spirit"></a>

#### Petition spirit

You petition a spirit office (healing, harvest, fertility, safe_passage, prosperity, suit, rain, protection, vengeance, finding, justice); the office reads it after a delay and grants or denies it, and a denied petition may be appealed to the office above. A suit is brought against an offended spirit under that office; a petition for vengeance names the one who wronged you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Spirit offices: spirits hold ranks under the gods, read petitions after delays and gifts, take appeals and suits, and rise or fall by service](interconnections.md#m-celestial_bureaucracy).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Spirit | The spirit office you petition |
| Kind | What you ask for |
| Target | The one who wronged you, for vengeance |

<a id="t-mortal-place_grave_goods"></a>

#### Place grave goods

Lay things you own with the dead in a grave where you are. They leave you and stay with the dead. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Grave goods and tomb robbing: what the dead keep, the guards over it, the robbers who take it and the diggers who find it](interconnections.md#m-grave_goods).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grave | The grave |
| Item | The things you lay |

<a id="t-mortal-preach"></a>

#### Preach

You spread your god's word to a neighbouring community; if it takes hold they too worship your god and your disciples increase. It is hard among those who worship another god. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** cults.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The community or mortal |
| Sermon | Your sermon |

<a id="t-mortal-preach_end_time"></a>

#### Preach end time

As a mystic or seer you preach that the end is near in your town; the faithful gather, stop working as the day nears and, when it passes, read the prophecy anew, become a sect or disperse. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Millenarian movements: prophecies, omens and immiseration stir towns to await the end, give up goods, march, and then redate, become sects or disperse](interconnections.md#m-millenarianism).

<a id="t-mortal-prophesy"></a>

#### Prophesy

You make a prophecy: you say something that will happen in the future. If it comes true it is tracked and your reputation (charisma) rises; if it is misread it is shaken. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** mortals, cults and spirits.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Predicate | What exactly must happen for the prophecy to come true, which the world itself will check: dies, falls, born_in, sky_event, war_between, ruler_of, within_epochs |
| Subject | The one the foretelling names: a person, a place, a house, a realm, or the first side of a war |
| Second | For a war: the second side |
| Archetype | For who will rule: the kind of legend the coming ruler must fit |
| Sky kind | For a sign in the sky: which sign |
| Event kind | For a foretold happening: what kind |
| Within epochs | In how many ages it must come true; if it does not, your standing falls and your followers' devotion drops |
| Consequence | What it means when it comes true: rule, fall, death, war, birth, omen |
| Consequence | Whom the consequence touches |
| Name | The prophecy's name, from your own language |
| Saying | The words of the prophecy: testable; who or what, and when |
| Related | Records the prophecy concerns (at most 3) |

<a id="t-mortal-purify_self"></a>

#### Purify self

You seek purification in the way your people knows, paying what it costs; if it takes, you are pure again and may join gatherings and unions. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Purity and caste: the impure are shut out of gatherings, unions and other ranks' work until purified; ranks pass to children](interconnections.md#m-purity).

<a id="t-mortal-read_the_sky"></a>

#### Read the sky

You read a body of your sky for your people; they come to hold that reading of it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Peoples read celestial bodies as omens, returns move piety and doom, and the epoch star lifts cults, prophecies and rites](interconnections.md#m-celestial_omens).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Celestial | The body in the sky |
| Reading | What it is taken to mean |

<a id="t-mortal-resurrection_rite"></a>

#### Resurrection rite

You perform a rite for your dead god's return: prayer (+4 devotion), wealth offering (15 from your wealth, prosperity or craft; +10), blood sacrifice (3% of your population; +20) or life sacrifice (a named mortal offers itself or your people offer it; +35). A rite held in the region where your god's remains lie counts 2 times. When devotion is full the resurrection rite may succeed. 1 action, 1 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The dead god's (son's) id |
| Kind | prayer, wealth, blood or life |
| Victim | The named mortal offered in a life sacrifice (if empty, yourself if you are a mortal) |

<a id="t-mortal-rob_grave"></a>

#### Rob grave

Rob a grave where you are of the goods laid in it. A watched or trapped grave may catch you, a cursed one may answer where the land allows, and the kin and people of the dead hold it against you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Grave goods and tomb robbing: what the dead keep, the guards over it, the robbers who take it and the diggers who find it](interconnections.md#m-grave_goods).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Grave | The grave |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_vision"></a>

#### Seek vision

You seek a direct experience of the divine; it comes only to a mind of wisdom that holds piety dear, and it draws followers. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Mysticism: wise and pious mortals have direct experiences, real when a god answers, gather followers, are revered or persecuted, and found orders or heresies](interconnections.md#m-mysticism).

<a id="t-mortal-take_care"></a>

#### Take care

You spend a little to take care this season, so the mishaps of daily life are less likely to befall you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Fortune is reckoned from blessings, curses, charms, oaths, powers over fate and a streak of its own, and moves the mishaps and draws of a life](interconnections.md#m-fortune).

<a id="t-mortal-undertake_heroquest"></a>

#### Undertake heroquest

You set out on a heroquest: you enter a mythic realm and walk a legend of your people step by step, each step a trial of skill, passion, conviction or faith. You need renown, a faithful people and a legend to walk. Following the people's version helps; departing from it may win another boon and rewrites the legend. A fall may hurt, madden or kill you, leave you stuck in the mythic realm, unable to act, or break the myth. Companions share the trials and the outcome. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Heroquests: mortals walk a legend step by step in a mythic realm for a boon, rewrite it by departing from it, or are trapped and break it](interconnections.md#m-heroquests).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Legend | The legend you walk (a legend id); leave it out to walk your people's own |
| Companion | Mortals where you are who walk beside you (ids), if any |

<a id="t-mortal-worship"></a>

#### Worship

You worship a god (a son) or change your god; if you give son_id 0 you worship the creator god directly, maker of all the sons. Your old god's favour toward you falls. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The son's id, or 0 for the creator |
| Offering | Your offering and vow |

<a id="p-mortal-magic"></a>

### Magic

10 tools: [call tribunal](#t-mortal-call_tribunal), [command construct](#t-mortal-command_construct), [design rite](#t-mortal-design_rite), [foresee](#t-mortal-foresee), [found enchantment](#t-mortal-found_enchantment), [link minds](#t-mortal-link_minds), [search magic sites](#t-mortal-search_magic_sites), [take longevity](#t-mortal-take_longevity), [train power](#t-mortal-train_power), [use power](#t-mortal-use_power).

<a id="t-mortal-call_tribunal"></a>

#### Call tribunal

As leader of a magical order you call its tribunal against a mortal for breaking one of the order's laws; it may fine them, strip their rank or cast them out. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Magical orders: laws among mages, tribunals that judge breaches, and covenants that draw vis from held sites and teach magic to their town](interconnections.md#m-magical_orders).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Accused | The accused |
| Law | The law they broke |

<a id="t-mortal-command_construct"></a>

#### Command construct

You give a construct you command a new first order (guard, serve, obey_holder, build, gather, protect, destroy, report, maintain, replicate, hold_still, follow, forbid, carry, tend, teach, heal, hunt), aimed at a target or at no one, kept under a condition (always, while_maker_lives, until_date, when_attacked, when_token_shown, until_done, at_set_times, while_holder_lives, when_named); an order kept at set times runs only then (day, night, season, sky_event). 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Constructs: makers build bodies moved by a power source, bound by typed orders to whoever holds the token, wearing down and growing a will](interconnections.md#m-constructs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Construct | The construct |
| Kind | The order |
| Target | Whom or what it aims at, or empty |
| Condition | When the order holds |
| Until epoch | The epoch an order kept until a date ends |
| Times | When an order kept at set times runs |

<a id="t-mortal-design_rite"></a>

#### Design rite

You devise a new rite of magic with the workings you choose; it must still be cast to take effect. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Spell grammar: every rite is a technique worked on a form, with range, duration and target, designed in laboratories and set down in lab texts](interconnections.md#m-spell_grammar).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A proper name for the rite |
| School | Its school of magic: elemental_fire, elemental_water, earth, storm, life, death, mind, illusion, divination, warding, binding, summoning, blood, void |
| Source | What it draws on: ley, divine, blood, soul, reagent, void; divine costs a god's essence, blood costs blood (captives' first), void costs sanity |
| Power | Its power, 1 to 10; stronger rites cost more and backfire more often |
| Workings | What it does, at most 4 workings |
| Workings › Effect | The working: damage, heal, weather_change, curse, bless, ward, scry, bind, summon, raise_dead, fertility, harvest_blessing, seal_soul, unseal_soul, exorcise, dispel, longevity, move_target, move_self, push, shape_matter, read_record, seize_machine, alter_record, sense_emotion, sway_emotion, turn_fortune, drain_life, drain_power, copy_power, project_self, possess, change_form, conceal, nullify, hasten, slow, age, renew, foresee |
| Workings › Magnitude | Its strength, 1 to 100 |
| Workings › Damage type | For destruction: the kind of harm it deals |
| Workings › Summon form | For a summoning: spirit, beast or undead |
| Technique | How the rite works: create, perceive, transform, destroy, control, bind, summon, ward, drain, mimic, heal, move, speak; leave it out to use its school's usual technique |
| Form | What the rite works on: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream, law; leave it out to use its school's usual form |
| Range | How far it reaches: touch, sight, region, world, linked, cosmos, dimension |
| Duration | How long it lasts: instant, season, epoch, permanent, sustained, until_condition, lifetime |
| Target | What it touches: one, group, structure, region, item, line, world, species, people |
| Condition | For a rite that lasts until a condition: what ends it (act, time, word, deed) |
| Condition act | For a condition of an act or a deed: the act that ends it |
| Condition epoch | For a condition of time: the epoch it ends |
| Paradigm | The theory of magic it is designed under (a theory id), if any; a truer theory makes it more reliable |
| Myth | A legend it re-enacts (a legend id), if any; a believed myth gives it strength |
| Holder | Ignored: you devise it yourself |

<a id="t-mortal-foresee"></a>

#### Foresee

You use a power over time to see what may come. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | The power |

<a id="t-mortal-found_enchantment"></a>

#### Found enchantment

You lay a global enchantment over your world: a natural law of your choosing (none: Narrative only; no mechanical effect.; immortality: Mortals do not age; population grows more slowly.; short_life: Mortals age faster; population grows faster.; dead_return: Fallen heroes may return as ghosts.; magic_price: Peoples steeped in magic pay part of their population to it every epoch.; war_ban: Wars break out less often but are bloodier.; bounty: The fertility of every land rises each epoch.; drought: The fertility of every land falls each epoch.; sea_ban: Seas cannot be crossed; seafaring is useless.; one_language: Languages spread faster through conquest and trade.; disasters: Natural disasters happen more often.; souls_reincarnate: Souls that pass judgement may be born again into a new life.; gift_flowering: The magical gift is born more often in every bloodline.; gift_withering: The magical gift is born more rarely and fades from bloodlines.; no_iron: Iron cannot be smelted or worked.; eternal_night: The sun does not rise over its reach; fields yield far less.; magic_requires_blood: Every rite costs the caster's blood as well as its price.; gravity_shift: Things weigh differently; building and marching are slower.; seasons_reversed: The seasons run backwards; growth comes in the cold half of the year.; long_seasons: Summers and winters run for years of no set length; scholars forecast their turn and peoples store food against them.; dreams_are_real: Dream routes never close and mortals dream more often.; oaths_bind: Whoever breaks an oath is branded at once.; the_dead_speak: Spirits take turns in the world: they speak, send word, foretell and pray, and tell the legends of the places they dwell in.; metal_rusts_fast: Metal weapons and tools wear out far faster.; no_gunpowder: Powder will not burn: no gunpowder, firearms or cannon can be made.). It holds only while you, your order or your people pay its upkeep in vis each epoch, and rival casters may dispel it. Needs spellcraft of 60. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Global enchantments: natural laws laid by a caster that hold only while their upkeep is paid and fall when dispelled](interconnections.md#m-global_enchantments).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | Its name in your language |
| Effect | The law it makes |
| Form | The vis that keeps it: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream, law |
| Strength | Its strength, 1 to 100 |

<a id="t-mortal-link_minds"></a>

#### Link minds

You link your power with another who holds one; linked, you are stronger together and share the strain. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Partner | Whom you link with |

<a id="t-mortal-search_magic_sites"></a>

#### Search magic sites

With spellcraft of 40 or more, you search the land around you for hidden magic sites. A site you find is held by your community and adds to its holiness; some yield reagents, some strengthen rites, some guard, some open a way to another world. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Thrones of power and magic sites: thrones held by the god whose dominion rules their land, and hidden sources, school seats, wards and gateways found by searching mages](interconnections.md#m-thrones_and_sites).

<a id="t-mortal-take_longevity"></a>

#### Take longevity

You take longevity magic: it adds years to your life each epoch it is paid in vis of the form you choose, and you come to need it; when the vis runs out the added years are lost. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Longevity magic: years bought with vis, a growing need for it and the years lost when it lapses](interconnections.md#m-longevity).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Form | The vis it uses: fire, water, air, earth, plant, animal, body, mind, image, spirit, time, void, death, light, space, force, sound, emotion, fate, machine, information, dream, law |

<a id="t-mortal-train_power"></a>

#### Train power

You train a power you hold to master it better. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | The power |

<a id="t-mortal-use_power"></a>

#### Use power

You use a power you hold, on someone or on yourself. Each use strains you; past your limit it harms you. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Beings hold powers that wake, are used, strain and scar, burn out, grow, link, fade with age and come by birth, exposure, gift or line](interconnections.md#m-powers).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Power | The power |
| Target | Whom or what you use it on, or empty for yourself |

<a id="p-mortal-knowledge"></a>

### Knowledge

13 tools: [choose research](#t-mortal-choose_research), [decipher script](#t-mortal-decipher_script), [dig site](#t-mortal-dig_site), [join programme](#t-mortal-join_programme), [join school](#t-mortal-join_school), [learn knowledge](#t-mortal-learn_knowledge), [propose theory](#t-mortal-propose_theory), [salvage](#t-mortal-salvage), [sit examination](#t-mortal-sit_examination), [study relic](#t-mortal-study_relic), [teach lore](#t-mortal-teach_lore), [test lore](#t-mortal-test_lore), [trade lore](#t-mortal-trade_lore).

<a id="t-mortal-choose_research"></a>

#### Choose research

You choose which technique your community's scholars work on next, from the ones within reach. The chosen technique advances at full pace, the others slowly. Your current research and its choices are in your context. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Each epoch scholars draw a few techniques within reach, weighted by need and boosts; the focus advances at full pace, the rest slowly](interconnections.md#m-research_focus).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Technique | The technique to research; one of the choices within your reach |

<a id="t-mortal-decipher_script"></a>

#### Decipher script

You work at the dead script of a tongue: progress grows with your languages skill, the texts that survive, any bilingual text and the living tongues related to it. Partial readings can go wrong. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Decipherment: scholars read dead scripts from surviving texts, bilingual finds and living kin tongues, and their misreadings take hold](interconnections.md#m-decipherment).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Language | The dead tongue |

<a id="t-mortal-dig_site"></a>

#### Dig site

With lore enough, you begin a dig at an old site, the remains of an earlier creation or a wreck, and lead it. It goes through five stages; each needs a skill and sometimes a technique, and each may hold a risk or a find that passes to your realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Digs at ruins, places where gods died, craters, burial grounds, earlier creations and wrecks, stage by stage, with their risks and finds](interconnections.md#m-excavations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Site | The site you dig |

<a id="t-mortal-join_programme"></a>

#### Join programme

If you are skilled in science, engineering or computing, you work on your realm's programme toward a technique and speed it on. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Technique programmes: a realm plans, builds and tests its way to a dangerous technique, paid by its treasury and set back by spies](interconnections.md#m-tech_programmes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Programme | The programme |

<a id="t-mortal-join_school"></a>

#### Join school

You join a school of thought as a disciple of its master; masters teach and the school keeps its learning. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Schools of thought: masters and disciples form lineages that teach, keep knowledge through hard times and contend with rivals](interconnections.md#m-school_lineages).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| School | The school |

<a id="t-mortal-learn_knowledge"></a>

#### Learn knowledge

You learn a piece of knowledge known by your trade partner, ally or neighbour. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Knowledge | The knowledge's id |
| From | Who teaches it: a community or mortal that knows it and that you trade with, are allied to, rule, or share a region with |

<a id="t-mortal-propose_theory"></a>

#### Propose theory

You set out a theory: your community's account of one field. Whether it is true is hidden; a true one helps the work it guides and a false one meets anomalies until a rival overturns it. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Peoples hold theories of each field; a true ruling theory helps the work it guides, a false one meets anomalies until a rival overturns it](interconnections.md#m-theories).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Domain | The field it explains: medicine, cosmology, matter, life, mind, magic, history, society, language, law |
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Claim | What it holds, in one or two sentences |

<a id="t-mortal-salvage"></a>

#### Salvage

You take what can be used from a salvage site. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [What its keepers can no longer make becomes a relic that wears, is studied and revered; salvage sites yield to their holders](interconnections.md#m-made_relics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Site | The site |

<a id="t-mortal-sit_examination"></a>

#### Sit examination

You sit the examinations of your realm; with enough learning in law, letters or lore you earn a credential that opens offices. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Examinations and credentials: officials chosen by tests of learning, and degrees and ordinations that open careers and offices](interconnections.md#m-examinations).

<a id="t-mortal-study_relic"></a>

#### Study relic

You study a relic of a making your people have lost, to learn how it works. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [What its keepers can no longer make becomes a relic that wears, is studied and revered; salvage sites yield to their holders](interconnections.md#m-made_relics).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Item | The relic |

<a id="t-mortal-teach_lore"></a>

#### Teach lore

You teach what your people know of a domain to someone. A learner of another people brings its accuracy closer to yours where yours is higher; a learner of your own people deepens what you all know. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Folk knowledge: how each people classes weather, sky, matter, land, waters, plants, animals, body, illness, mind, society, number and time, how accurate it is and what its numbers mean](interconnections.md#m-folk_knowledge).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Domain | The domain: weather, sky, matter, land, waters, plants, animals, body, illness, mind, society, number or time |
| Learner | Whom you teach |

<a id="t-mortal-test_lore"></a>

#### Test lore

You put one of your people's classes to the test against the world. A class they had wrong is put right and their knowledge grows more accurate; testing something harmful taken for harmless costs you health. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Folk knowledge: how each people classes weather, sky, matter, land, waters, plants, animals, body, illness, mind, society, number and time, how accurate it is and what its numbers mean](interconnections.md#m-folk_knowledge).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Domain | The domain: weather, sky, matter, land, waters, plants, animals, body, illness, mind, society, number or time |
| Class | The class you test |

<a id="t-mortal-trade_lore"></a>

#### Trade lore

You trade a piece of lore you hold (a technique, a hidden site, knowledge of a land or a prophecy) to a neighbour who lacks it. Rarer lore is worth more, and each telling makes it less rare. You may name the price: wealth, favour, renown, exchange, service or oath. Exchange takes a piece of lore the buyer holds and you lack; service is a deed the buyer must do before the lore passes; an oath binds the buyer, and breaking it is oathbreaking. Otherwise the buyer pays as it can. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Reaches** [Factions, cults, scholars and beasts value techniques, hidden sites, maps and prophecies; lore is traded for wealth, favours and renown](interconnections.md#m-lore_currency).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Buyer | Who receives the lore |
| Kind | The kind of lore |
| Key | Which piece: the technique key, or site, region or prophecy |
| Subject | The site, land or prophecy, if any |
| Price | The price, if you name it |

<a id="p-mortal-culture"></a>

### Culture

25 tools: [adopt kind](#t-mortal-adopt_kind), [buy work](#t-mortal-buy_work), [challenge to contest](#t-mortal-challenge_to_contest), [claim right](#t-mortal-claim_right), [commemorate](#t-mortal-commemorate), [commission account](#t-mortal-commission_account), [create dish](#t-mortal-create_dish), [fix contest](#t-mortal-fix_contest), [forbid kind](#t-mortal-forbid_kind), [forge record](#t-mortal-forge_record), [forge work](#t-mortal-forge_work), [invent art form](#t-mortal-invent_art_form), [invent game](#t-mortal-invent_game), [invent kind](#t-mortal-invent_kind), [join road folk](#t-mortal-join_road_folk), [keep legacy](#t-mortal-keep_legacy), [lead revival](#t-mortal-lead_revival), [mark body](#t-mortal-mark_body), [publish](#t-mortal-publish), [reckon years](#t-mortal-reckon_years), [resist drift](#t-mortal-resist_drift), [review work](#t-mortal-review_work), [strike memory](#t-mortal-strike_memory), [swear vengeance](#t-mortal-swear_vengeance), [tend culture](#t-mortal-tend_culture).

<a id="t-mortal-adopt_kind"></a>

#### Adopt kind

Your people takes up a kind of thing that another people made or holds. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Peoples make their own kinds of things as they awaken and grow; kinds spread by contact and trade, fade and retire when unheld](interconnections.md#m-ways_of_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Concept | What kind of thing it is |
| Kind | The kind to take up |

<a id="t-mortal-buy_work"></a>

#### Buy work

You buy a work of art at its market price from whoever holds it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Art markets: collectors, dealers, patrons and connoisseurs price works by quality, renown, canon, provenance and taste, and buy them at fairs](interconnections.md#m-art_market).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Artwork | The work |

<a id="t-mortal-challenge_to_contest"></a>

#### Challenge to contest

You challenge another mortal where you are to a contest of wit for a stake. The winner gains renown; a famous contest may become the subject of an artwork. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Rivals, suitors, captives, courtiers and priests meet in riddle games, contests of insults, debates, games, trials, songs and tales for a stake](interconnections.md#m-contests).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | The mortal you challenge |
| Kind | The contest |
| Stake | What is at stake |

<a id="t-mortal-claim_right"></a>

#### Claim right

You claim a right (song, dance, story, rite, design, name, title, trademark, market_privilege, recipe, technique, performance, likeness) over something you made or know: a work, a song, a name, a dish you created or a technique you hold. Or you take a right staked in a contest you won, or a right your partner's line passes with your union. Others who use it then owe you or infringe it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The kind of right |
| Name | What the right is called |
| Subject | The work, rite, house or realm it covers, or empty |
| Dish | The dish a right to a recipe covers, or empty |
| Technique | The technique a right to a technique covers, or empty |
| Contest | A contest you won in which the right was staked, or empty |
| Marriage | Your union through which the right passes, or empty |

<a id="t-mortal-commemorate"></a>

#### Commemorate

Mark the land you stand on with a memory of what happened there (battle, massacre, sack, disaster, plague, famine, god_death, saint_burial, miracle, founding, treaty, expulsion, discovery, uprising, union, accession); the land keeps it at magnitude 15. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The land remembers battles, massacres, sacks, disasters, plagues, famines, saints, miracles and fallen gods by magnitude](interconnections.md#m-region_memory).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land you stand on |
| Memory | What the land should remember |
| Title | What it is called |

<a id="t-mortal-commission_account"></a>

#### Commission account

As ruler you have your realm issue its official account of an event; you choose whom it praises and whom it blames, and the further it strays from what happened the more a scholar may one day expose. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Accounts: chronicles, folk memory, foreign accounts, gospels, epics and inscriptions of events, with stances, fidelity and credence](interconnections.md#m-historiography).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Collection | The event |
| Praise | Whom the account praises |
| Blame | Whom the account blames |

<a id="t-mortal-create_dish"></a>

#### Create dish

You first cook a new dish for your people, or for your town if you belong to no people, made in a way they know; their towns cook it from their stores from then on, and how it is made sets how it nourishes, delights and keeps. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Dishes: each people invents named dishes from its stores and ways of cooking; towns cook them to feed, calm and honour, armies carry the ones that keep](interconnections.md#m-dishes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Method | How it is made; one of: raw, boiled, stewed, steamed, roasted, grilled, baked, fried, smoked, dried, cured, pickled, fermented, braised, simmered, ground, pressed, synthesised, printed, vat_grown, freeze_dried, irradiated, conjured, infused, distilled, churned, sprouted, alkali_treated, cultured, cured_with_mineral, aged, extruded, pressure_cooked, earth_oven, parched |
| Course | When it is eaten; one of: staple, side, festive, rite, ration, delicacy, remedy, drink |
| Ingredients | One to four foods it is made of; each one of: grain, tubers, fruit, meat, fish, milk, cheese, eggs, herbs, brew_base, bread |
| Name | What the dish is called; left empty, your people names it in its own tongue |
| Description | What sets the dish apart, in your words |

<a id="t-mortal-fix_contest"></a>

#### Fix contest

You arrange that a club of a match you name wins its next contest, for a wager or a patron. If it is found out, by 20% chance, the wagers are void, both clubs lose standing, you are tried for it and the crowd may rise. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Match | A match between the clubs |
| Side | The club of that match that is to win |

<a id="t-mortal-forbid_kind"></a>

#### Forbid kind

As ruler of a realm or head of a cult, you forbid a kind of thing to those you lead. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Peoples make their own kinds of things as they awaken and grow; kinds spread by contact and trade, fade and retire when unheld](interconnections.md#m-ways_of_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Concept | What kind of thing it is |
| Kind | The kind to forbid |

<a id="t-mortal-forge_record"></a>

#### Forge record

You write a false record about someone or something and pass it as genuine. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [A people's trust in its records falls with censorship and found forgeries and slowly recovers](interconnections.md#m-information_trust).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Subject | What the false record is about |

<a id="t-mortal-forge_work"></a>

#### Forge work

You make a forgery (artwork, relic, document, brand, coin, lineage, seal, identity): a work, a relic, a charter or a town's brand to copy, a realm whose coin you counterfeit or whose seal passes your false orders, or a title whose lineage you forge to claim it. It may serve as the real thing until the expert of its kind sees through it; then you are tried for its crime. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Forgery: skilled makers forge works, relics, charters and brands that pass for the real thing until an expert exposes them](interconnections.md#m-forgery).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Original | What you copy, or the realm or title you forge against |
| Kind | The kind of forgery, or empty to let what you copy decide |

<a id="t-mortal-invent_art_form"></a>

#### Invent art form

You invent an art form others can take up, practise and master. A named mortal needs a fitting skill of at least 60. Free.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **Reaches** [Art forms are invented and fused, spread by trade, mastered, lent to artworks, hallowed in rites and lost when no one keeps them](interconnections.md#m-art_forms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Discipline | Its discipline: verbal, musical, movement, flat_image, solid_image, built, worn, culinary, scent, light, recorded, interactive, landscape, body, ritual_performance, textile_image, made_mind_art, tactile, mind |
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Description | What it is and how it is practised |
| Structure | Its shape, keyed by the discipline's parts: verbal: metre, rhyme, stanza, line_length, subjects; musical: scale, rhythm, voices, instruments; movement: tempo, group_size, movement; ritual_performance: form, cast, subjects; flat_image: palette, motifs, materials; solid_image: motifs, materials, scale; built: motifs, materials, proportions; textile_image: palette, motifs, pattern; worn: cut, ornament, colours; culinary: flavours, courses, presentation; scent: notes, strength, occasion; light: colour, motion, timing; recorded: medium, length, subjects; interactive: rules, players, choices; landscape: plants, paths, views; body: placement, pattern, meaning; made_mind_art: source, method, curation; tactile: texture, form, materials; mind: theme, mood, sharing; each value a short phrase |

<a id="t-mortal-invent_game"></a>

#### Invent game

You make up a game for your people and name it in their tongue; it may spread to their neighbours. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Games and sports: peoples invent games, players practise their skill, chance and betting breed gambling and crime, crowds ease unrest](interconnections.md#m-games).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | What sort of game it is |

<a id="t-mortal-invent_kind"></a>

#### Invent kind

You make a new kind of thing for your people, of a category its kind of thing allows, and give it a name and a description; your people makes it, and it may pass to neighbours and trading partners. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Peoples make their own kinds of things as they awaken and grow; kinds spread by contact and trade, fade and retire when unheld](interconnections.md#m-ways_of_life).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Concept | What kind of thing it is |
| Category | The category key it belongs to; each kind of thing has categories of its own |
| Name | Its name |
| Description | What it is, in a sentence or two |

<a id="t-mortal-join_road_folk"></a>

#### Join road folk

You bring your town into the culture of a busy road between worlds: it comes to share that culture's ways and speech. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Cultures of the roads between worlds: the travellers of a busy road grow a culture of their own with shared values and a common tongue](interconnections.md#m-cosmopolitan).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Culture | The culture of the road |

<a id="t-mortal-keep_legacy"></a>

#### Keep legacy

You keep among your people something of a people who came before: a way, a name or a belief they left. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Legacy traits: successor peoples keep martial styles, aesthetics, rites, beliefs and values of their predecessors, named in their founding myth](interconnections.md#m-legacy_traits).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Predecessor | The people who came before |
| Kind | What is kept of them |

<a id="t-mortal-lead_revival"></a>

#### Lead revival

You lead a movement of your own people against those who press on it, choosing its way (restore, synthesis, adopt, await, withdraw, migrate) and its goal (revive_language, restore_god, expel_foreigners, restore_land, restore_rule, restore_law); you become its prophet. Restoring may seek any goal; synthesis seeks the old god or the language; adopting seeks rule by the people itself; waiting seeks rule, land or the foreigners gone; withdrawing seeks the language or the old god. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Folk customs: revival movements against pressing cultures, intoxicant customs, body marks with meanings, time budgets, naming and age-sets](interconnections.md#m-customs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | Your people |
| Way | The way the movement takes |
| Goal | What it seeks |

<a id="t-mortal-mark_body"></a>

#### Mark body

You put a mark on your own body, or on someone where you are who takes it from you. Marks: tattoo, scar, piercing, shaping, filed_teeth, cosmetic, jewellery, brand, hair, implant, paint, stain; an implant shows an augment the bearer carries and has not yet shown. Meanings: status, clan, deed, age_set, marriage, mourning, protection, crime, faith, profession, beauty, healing, bound_labour. What a mark tells changes how others see its bearer, as the people of your town read it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Folk customs: revival movements against pressing cultures, intoxicant customs, body marks with meanings, time budgets, naming and age-sets](interconnections.md#m-customs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The mark |
| Meaning | What it tells |
| Target | Who bears it; leave empty for yourself |

<a id="t-mortal-publish"></a>

#### Publish

You publish a true record about someone or something, kept in your home. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [A people's trust in its records falls with censorship and found forgeries and slowly recovers](interconnections.md#m-information_trust).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Subject | What the record is about |

<a id="t-mortal-reckon_years"></a>

#### Reckon years

As ruler of a realm, you choose how its years are counted from now on. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Dating systems: realms count by reign, peoples by founding, cults by their era and the learned by celestial cycles, and reform their calendars](interconnections.md#m-dating_systems).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | How years are counted |
| Cycle years | Length of a cycle in years, for counts that run in cycles |

<a id="t-mortal-resist_drift"></a>

#### Resist drift

You hold your town to its ways against one cause that moves them; its effect is halved. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Culture drift from famine, scholars' ideologies and wars lost by cohesion](interconnections.md#m-culture_drift_causes).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Cause | The cause you resist |

<a id="t-mortal-review_work"></a>

#### Review work

As a critic of renown you judge a work before the market: praise raises its price and the standing of its movement, a pan sinks them, by up to 20% as your renown allows. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Artwork | The work you judge |

<a id="t-mortal-strike_memory"></a>

#### Strike memory

As ruler you strike a figure from your realm's records: its accounts omit them, public mentions of them are removed and works made of them for the realm are destroyed. Their fame fades unless the folk or foreigners keep it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Accounts: chronicles, folk memory, foreign accounts, gospels, epics and inscriptions of events, with stances, fidelity and credence](interconnections.md#m-historiography).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Figure | Whom you remove from the realm's records |

<a id="t-mortal-swear_vengeance"></a>

#### Swear vengeance

You swear vengeance on one who killed your kin; you start a pursuit of vengeance, which may end in their fall, in yours or in neither. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Narrative arcs: lives are read as tales from their typed state, with counterpart figures, stages reached by shared events and recorded outcomes](interconnections.md#m-narrative_arcs).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Foe | The one who killed your kin |

<a id="t-mortal-tend_culture"></a>

#### Tend culture

You tend a ferment culture of your town, or start one if it has none of that kind; a kept culture makes the town's baked, cured, fermented, pickled and brewed food better. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Ferment cultures: towns that bake, cure, ferment or pickle keep cultures that improve with use, travel along trade routes and die in plagues](interconnections.md#m-fermentation).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The culture: yeast, starter, mould, lactic, vinegar, alkaline or wild |

<a id="p-mortal-language"></a>

### Language

6 tools: [coin words](#t-mortal-coin_words), [found language](#t-mortal-found_language), [interpret](#t-mortal-interpret), [reform script](#t-mortal-reform_script), [set speech](#t-mortal-set_speech), [teach tongue](#t-mortal-teach_tongue).

<a id="t-mortal-coin_words"></a>

#### Coin words

You add new words to the language you speak (meaning, type, origin). Free, 2 times per age, at most 8 words each time.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 2 times an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|

Skilled in tongues, you carry the words of an exchange between two sides who share none, faithfully or slanted toward your own side. A slant may win your side more and may come to light, wronging the other and you with it. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

<a id="t-mortal-protect_area"></a>

#### Protect area

You set a land or a site within it under protection, barring uses there. Barred uses fall by the protection's strength and its wardens, and the land and its wildlife recover; want around it draws poachers, and towns whose livelihood it bars grow poorer and restless. Protection by law needs one who rules the land. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Protected nature: protected lands, protected living kinds, reintroduction and movements for the wild](interconnections.md#m-protected_nature).

|---|---|

| Basis | By what right: law, faith, custom, charter or divine_decree |
| Banned uses | The uses barred: hunting, logging, farming, mining, settlement, entry, fishing or building |

You set a living kind under protection. A hunting ban spares it; a trade ban starves its poachers of buyers; a breeding programme raises its numbers; reintroduction brings it back to lands it left that can still hold it; a habitat guard lets its lands' forests regrow. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Protected nature: protected lands, protected living kinds, reintroduction and movements for the wild](interconnections.md#m-protected_nature).

|---|---|

| Basis | By what right: law, faith, custom, charter or divine_decree |

A bore into water may flood. 1 action.

Lift poison from the land you stand on, by works, by a rite or by a power (works, rite, power). Works lift 20, a rite 10 and a power 30 of its depth; a power needs land with magic density of at least 30. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

You walk a site that has rooms, room by room. A room may hold a trap, a guardian, a puzzle or a hazard, and things, spirits or beasts within; you take what you win and stop at the first room you cannot pass. Reaching its last room wins renown. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.

#### Dig deeper

Dig deeper beneath the land you stand on, adding 5 to its mining; the next layer of its ground (soil, clay, sand, limestone, sandstone, shale, granite, basalt, marble, obsidian, slate, crystal_bedrock, frozen_methane, bone, glass, halite, coal, chalk, tuff, gneiss, quartzite, water_ice, iron, carbon, living_tissue, pumice, serpentinite, travertine) is reached when the mining goes deep enough. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land you stand on |

<a id="t-mortal-dig_passage"></a>

#### Dig passage

Your people dig a passage through the deep, or carry on digging one (level, vertical, opening): between the same stratum beneath two neighbouring regions, between two strata beneath one region, or from a region's surface down to a cell beneath it. A stratum names a level beneath the region; leave it empty for the surface. Your people must know metallurgy. The passage stands digging, open, flooded, collapsed, sealed as it is dug, opens, floods, falls or is sealed. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| From region | The region at one end |
| From stratum | The level beneath it at that end, or empty for its surface |
| To region | The region at the other end |
| To stratum | The level beneath it at the other end, or empty for its surface |

Turn the river of the land you stand on through a canal of your people into a neighbouring land. Fields and wells follow the water; a realm downstream that loses it holds the turning against those who rule your land. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [River courses, desertification, eruptions and saline soils](interconnections.md#m-geomorphology).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Water, ice and sediment: ice comes and goes, snow melts into rivers, sediment builds deltas and silts harbours, coasts build or wear and towns warm their own air](interconnections.md#m-water_and_ice).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The land whose harbour you dredge; your own if empty |

#### Drill reservoir

Drill the reservoir beneath the land you stand on for fuel; it yields 10 at a time and may blow out under high pressure. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).
