<a id="t-mortal-explore_land"></a>

#### Explore land

You set out to explore beyond the known lands: you find either a new region not on the map or, in your own or a neighbouring region, a place no one has set foot on. Success depends on your wits (your craft if you are a community) and your path knowledge (seafaring, pathfinding, astronomy). If you are a community your pioneers can settle there and found a new settlement. 1 action, 3 per age.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **At most** 3 times an epoch.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples, mortals, cults and factions.
- **Only** a people that has grown to a tribe or beyond may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | region (new lands not on the map) or place (a new place in your own or a neighbouring region) |
| Name | An original proper name; fitting your language's naming rule |
| Subtype | What kind of land or place it is |
| Description | What you found, how you found it, what the first to set foot there saw |
| Location | Only for a place: the region it lies in (your own or a neighbouring one); if empty, your own region |
| Stratum | Only when the place is a region: the level beneath it where it stands (permafrost_layer, aquifer, under_ocean, reservoir, cavern_upper, cavern_middle, fault_zone, cavern_lower, hollow_interior, magma_sea, deep_underworld), or empty for the surface |
| Settle | If you are a community, your pioneers settle there and found a new settlement |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-mortal-forecast"></a>

#### Forecast

You read the coming weather of your land by one of the ways your people read it (sight, lore, almanac, instrument, network_of_observers, model, network, power, divination, animal_signs, dream). How true the reading is follows how well that way reads the weather; name a season to also read whether it will come early, on time, late, or fail next year. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Forecasts and weather works: peoples foresee hard seasons by their ways, and weather works turn a land's sky at the cost of the land downwind](interconnections.md#m-forecasting).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Method | The way you read it |
| Season position | Optional: the position of a season of your world (0 to 7) whose coming you read |

<a id="t-mortal-forecast_seasons"></a>

#### Forecast seasons

As one learned in the sky, you read when a long season of your world will end; your people may heed you and store food. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Irregular seasons: an orbit, a natural law or a god makes summers and winters run for years; scholars forecast them and peoples store food](interconnections.md#m-irregular_seasons).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Long season | The id of the long season (see the long seasons of your world) |

<a id="t-mortal-improve_soil"></a>

#### Improve soil

You work the soil of the land you live in to restore one of its stocks (fixed, mineral, organic, salinity, seed_bank, soil_life); salinity is washed out rather than raised. How much it takes depends on how easily the soil there is worked and how acid it is. Soils: thin, loam, clay, sand, volcanic, peat, saline, tephra, crystalline, calcareous, laterite, permafrost, regolith, artificial, humic, leached, silt, gley, cracking_clay. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Soil nutrients: fixed element, minerals, organic matter and salt, drawn by crops and restored by rotation, fallow, manure, floods and ash](interconnections.md#m-nutrients).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Nutrient | The stock you restore |

<a id="t-mortal-prepare_town"></a>

#### Prepare town

You pay to raise one of your town's works against disaster (levee, firebreak, stockpile, drill, watchtower, seawall, quarantine_plan, shelters, warning_network, evacuation_routes, ward, reserves_of_energy, immunisation_plan, quake_building); each level lowers the losses of the disaster it guards against. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Works against disaster: levees, firebreaks, stockpiles, drills, watchtowers and seawalls lower losses, and their holding is recorded](interconnections.md#m-resilience).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The work you raise |

<a id="t-mortal-seal_depth"></a>

#### Seal depth

Seal the deep beneath a place in the land you stand on (structure, rite, divine, power, living, pact, made, though no mortal makes a god's seal). A pact names an oath you swore to those below, a power names a power you hold, a grown barrier names a living line your people keep and a made seal names a made mind your people made, which keeps it only while it stays awake, bound and true. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [The deep: rock columns, cells joined by passages and the peoples below, reached by digging, bored, breached and sealed](interconnections.md#m-the_deep).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Place | The place whose deep you seal |
| Kind | What holds the seal |
| Oath | For a pact: your oath |
| Power | For a power: your power |
| Line | For a grown barrier: your people's living line |
| Mind | For a made seal: a made mind your people made |

<a id="t-mortal-study_fossils"></a>

#### Study fossils

You study a bed of remains found in your land and read it one way (legend, relic, deep_time, material, medicine, sacred, omen). Read as deep time, the theories you hold grow stronger, and with living craft a kind whose remains are well preserved may be raised again; read otherwise, your people tell it, revere it, work it as a substance or grind it into remedies. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Fossils: remains of what died out lie in beds, are found by digging and upheaval, and are read as remains of legend, sacred relics or evidence of deep time](interconnections.md#m-fossils).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Bed | The bed of remains you study |
| Reading | How you read it |

<a id="t-mortal-warn_coast"></a>

#### Warn coast

Warn the coast you stand on of the sea by a way your people watch the waters, so that a wave in the coming epoch finds them ready and kills fewer. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sea waves from quakes, slides and storms: great waves that cross a sea and strike all its coasts, warned or not](interconnections.md#m-sea_waves).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Region | The coast you stand on |
| Method | How you watch the waters (sight, lore, almanac, instrument, network_of_observers, model, network, power, divination, animal_signs, dream) |

<a id="t-mortal-work_weather"></a>

#### Work weather

You work the weather of your land toward rain, dry, calm, cold or warmth (rain, dry, calm, cold, warm, wind, snow) by a way you know (rite, power, seeding, diversion, made_structure, living_craft, bargain, made_mind), at a cost in coin that grows with its strength. The land downwind takes the opposite, and those harmed there may turn against you and make you stop. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Forecasts and weather works: peoples foresee hard seasons by their ways, and weather works turn a land's sky at the cost of the land downwind](interconnections.md#m-forecasting).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Aim | What you work the weather toward |
| Method | How you work it |
| Strength | How strongly, 1 to 100 |

<a id="p-mortal-gods"></a>

### Gods

3 tools: [challenge god](#t-mortal-challenge_god), [make offering](#t-mortal-make_offering), [pray to creator](#t-mortal-pray_to_creator).

<a id="t-mortal-challenge_god"></a>

#### Challenge god

You stand up to a god (a son): your strength, agility, wits and weapons against the god's might. If you win the god is wounded, their fame shaken and you become a legend; if you lose you die or are cursed. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Son | The god's id |
| Reason | Why you defy this god |

<a id="t-mortal-make_offering"></a>

#### Make offering

You make an offering to your god from your purse; the offerings your people have made rise, and the god is bound to answer it. You may give it in a way of worship (devotion, appeasement, bargain, union, kinship); given as a bargain it names a return and sets your people to bargain. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Sacrifice as exchange: offerings bind gods to answer, and gods who leave them unanswered lose standing](interconnections.md#m-sacrifice_exchange).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Value | Coin the offering is worth |
| Mode | The way of worship it is given in, or empty |

<a id="t-mortal-pray_to_creator"></a>

#### Pray to creator

You pray directly to the creator god above the sons, the maker of all worlds. Your prayer reaches the creator; any answer comes later. Free, 1 per age.

- **Costs** nothing.
- **At most** 1 time an epoch.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Prayer | Your prayer |
| Request | What you ask of the creator |

<a id="p-mortal-cosmos"></a>

### Cosmos

10 tools: [build realm](#t-mortal-build_realm), [build ship](#t-mortal-build_ship), [confine in realm](#t-mortal-confine_in_realm), [draw structure type](#t-mortal-draw_structure_type), [enter realm](#t-mortal-enter_realm), [find route](#t-mortal-find_route), [launch expedition](#t-mortal-launch_expedition), [propose resolution](#t-mortal-propose_resolution), [seek true name](#t-mortal-seek_true_name), [withdraw people](#t-mortal-withdraw_people).

<a id="t-mortal-build_realm"></a>

#### Build realm

You make a realm kept running by the host you name, for the purpose you name, at the time ratio you set. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Host | What holds it |
| Purpose | What it is for |
| Ratio | How fast it runs against world time (%) |

<a id="t-mortal-build_ship"></a>

#### Build ship

You build an inter-world ship (requires shipbuilding knowledge). Its power depends on your craft, your mining and your metallurgy. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** settlements, peoples and factions.
- **Only** a people that has grown to a kingdom or beyond may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Name | A name from your own language |
| Subtype | the kind of ship, in your own words |
| Description | What it is, how it came to be, what makes it distinctive and concrete |
| Image prompt | An English image-generation prompt of the subject as its account gives it. Image prompt rules: describe only what IS visible; never use negating words because the image model ignores them and draws the thing anyway; describe what is there instead of what is missing. Draw the subject from its own account rather than a generic stock scene. Describe only what is seen; never name an artistic medium, art style, camera or lens, because the world's chosen image style is applied to every prompt separately. |

<a id="t-mortal-confine_in_realm"></a>

#### Confine in realm

As ruler, you confine someone's mind in a made realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Target | Whom you confine |
| Realm | The made realm |

<a id="t-mortal-draw_structure_type"></a>

#### Draw structure type

As a builder, you draw a building type for your people, or for your town if you belong to no people, for one kind of structure, with the form and storeys you choose; its builders raise that kind to it from then on. Needs engineering of 30. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Megastructures: star collectors, beacons, orbital rings, planetary engines and habitat rings work on their stars, routes and worlds while their control cores hold](interconnections.md#m-megastructures).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Structure kind | The kind of structure it is a type of; one of: temple (Temple), shrine (Shrine), monastery (Monastery), necropolis (Necropolis), library (Library), school (School), observatory (Observatory), academy (Academy), hospital (Hospital), wall (Walls), gate (Gatehouse), tower (Tower), citadel (Citadel), watchtower (Watchtower), fort (Fort), palace (Palace), court (Court), prison (Prison), market (Market), harbour (Harbour), warehouse (Warehouse), granary (Granary), caravanserai (Caravanserai), bank (Bank), forge (Forge), mill (Mill), tannery (Tannery), mine (Mine), quarry (Quarry), shipyard (Shipyard), workshop (Workshop), alchemist_lab (Reagent laboratory), road (Road), bridge (Bridge), aqueduct (Aqueduct), sewer (Sewers), canal (Canal), irrigation (Irrigation works), dam (Dam), monument (Monument), tomb (Tomb), arena (Arena), theatre (Theatre), wonder (Wonder), cosmic_gate (Cosmic gate), cosmic_archive (Cosmic archive), firehouse (Firehouse), bathhouse (Bathhouse), fountain (Fountain), park (Park), slaughterhouse (Slaughterhouse), almshouse (Almshouse), brewery (Brewery), sawmill (Sawmill), fishery (Fishery), charcoal_kiln (Charcoal kiln), stables (Stables), siege_workshop (Siege yard), mint (Mint), tavern (Tavern), town_hall (Town hall), guildhall (Trade hall), barracks (Barracks), orphanage (Orphanage), apothecary (Apothecary), training_ground (Training ground), museum (Museum), plaza (Plaza), kontor (Trading post), embassy (Embassy), lighthouse (Lighthouse), signal_tower (Signal tower), customs_house (Customs house), toll_gate (Toll gate), courthouse (Courthouse), archive (Archive), printing_house (Printing house), laboratory (Laboratory), foundry (Foundry), smelter (Smelter), kiln (Kiln), glassworks (Glassworks), textile_mill (Textile mill), dye_works (Dye works), paper_mill (Paper mill), bakery (Bakery), dairy (Dairy), distillery (Distillery), refinery (Refinery), power_plant (Power plant), silo (Silo), cistern (Cistern), reservoir (Reservoir), canal_lock (Canal lock), tunnel (Tunnel), rail_station (Rail station), airfield (Airfield), spaceport (Spaceport), dry_dock (Dry dock), arsenal (Arsenal), kennel (Kennel), menagerie (Menagerie), botanical_garden (Botanical garden), cemetery (Cemetery), gymnasium (Gymnasium), asylum (Asylum), quarantine_house (Quarantine house), inn (Inn), market_hall (Market hall), exchange (Exchange), university (University), seminary (Seminary), conservatory (Conservatory), opera_house (Opera house), gallery (Gallery), stadium (Stadium), orbital_ring (Orbital ring), space_elevator (Space elevator), space_station (Space station), star_collector (Star collector), beacon (Beacon), planetary_engine (Planetary engine), habitat_ring (Habitat ring), factory (Factory), data_centre (Data centre), office_tower (Office tower), apartment_block (Apartment block), mall (Mall), broadcast_tower (Broadcast tower), satellite_array (Satellite array), military_base (Military base), missile_silo (Missile silo), shield_generator (Shield generator), terraformer (Terraformer), vertical_farm (Vertical farm), recycling_plant (Recycling plant), water_purification (Water purification), cloning_facility (Cloning facility), robotics_works (Robotics works), ai_core (Machine mind core), teleporter (Teleporter), covenant (Covenant) |
| Form | Its building form, if you choose; one of: hall, longhouse, tower, keep, dome, spire, stepped, pyramid, courtyard, ring, tent, mound, arcade, terrace, platform, bridge_form, wall_line, tunnel_form, orbital, high_rise, arcology, bunker, floating, geodesic, modular, grown, carved, stilt, vault |
| Storeys | How many storeys it rises, if you choose |
| Name | What the type is called; left empty, your people names it in its own tongue |
| Description | What sets the type apart, in your words |

<a id="t-mortal-enter_realm"></a>

#### Enter realm

You go into a made realm; your mind runs there. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Realm | The made realm |

<a id="t-mortal-find_route"></a>

#### Find route

You search for the way to another world. The way you find depends on what you know: gate lore an ancient gate, sky navigation a sail route, pathfinding folding, magic a dream road. Rarely you may discover a world no one has known. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Only** a people that has grown to a city state or beyond may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| How | How you search for the way |

<a id="t-mortal-launch_expedition"></a>

#### Launch expedition

You organize a journey to another world along a known route: to found a colony, open trade, explore, conquer, go on pilgrimage, send away those you will not keep, carry your people to safety or bear an embassy. The journey may take several ages; on the way it meets the dangers its route holds, and some journeys never arrive. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Only** a people that has grown to a city state or beyond may use it.

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Route | The route's id |
| Intent | colony, trade, exploration, conquest, pilgrimage, exile, flight, embassy, rescue or return |
| Percent | What percent of your community sets out (for colony or conquest; at most 30); default 10 |
| Vessel | The ship or vessel to use |

<a id="t-mortal-propose_resolution"></a>

#### Propose resolution

As ruler or regent of a member you put a resolution to your council of worlds for its members to vote on: route security, embargo, void ban, sanction, quarantine, arbitration, aid, peacekeeping or recognition. An embargo, sanction, quarantine, arbitration, aid, peacekeeping or recognition names what it is about: a realm, a god, a world struck by plague, a member at odds, a stricken member, a member at war or a new polity to admit. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Interworld assemblies vote resolutions by member weight, clients voting with their powers, and bind members to embargoes, sanctions and bans](interconnections.md#m-interworld_assembly).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Kind | The resolution |
| Target | What it is about |

<a id="t-mortal-seek_true_name"></a>

#### Seek true name

You seek a true name, your own if you have lost it or another's, from someone in your land who keeps it: a beast or a mortal who knows it. Learning a name gives you power over its bearer. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Reaches** [Transformations: a son falls or is corrupted, a people loses its sapience, a faith or a realm forges a people, a star or a world gains a god](interconnections.md#m-transformations).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| Keeper | Who you seek it from (an id) |
| Bearer | Whose true name you seek (an id); leave it out to seek your own |

<a id="t-mortal-withdraw_people"></a>

#### Withdraw people

As ruler, you move a people's minds into a made realm. 1 action.

- **Costs** 1 action point.
- **Used by** mortals.
- **Reaches** [Made realms run at their own time, stand still when their host goes down, yield to their keepers and hold the confined](interconnections.md#m-made_realms).

| What it chooses | What it means |
|---|---|
| People | The people |
| Realm | The made realm |
